# Complete Hospital ERP PostgreSQL Schema

## 📁 PART 1: FOUNDATION & MULTI-TENANCY

```sql
-- ============================================
-- HOSPITAL ERP - COMPLETE DATABASE SCHEMA
-- PostgreSQL 15+
-- ============================================

-- Enable required extensions
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pg_trgm";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "btree_gist";

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 1: CORE ENUMS
-- ============================================

-- Gender
CREATE TYPE gender_type AS ENUM ('male', 'female', 'other', 'unknown');

-- Blood Groups
CREATE TYPE blood_group_type AS ENUM ('A+', 'A-', 'B+', 'B-', 'AB+', 'AB-', 'O+', 'O-', 'unknown');

-- Marital Status
CREATE TYPE marital_status_type AS ENUM ('single', 'married', 'divorced', 'widowed', 'separated', 'other');

-- Patient Types
CREATE TYPE patient_type AS ENUM ('opd', 'ipd', 'emergency', 'daycare', 'telemedicine');

-- Visit Status
CREATE TYPE visit_status_type AS ENUM ('scheduled', 'checked_in', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled', 'no_show');

-- Admission Status
CREATE TYPE admission_status_type AS ENUM ('pending', 'admitted', 'discharged', 'transferred', 'expired', 'lama', 'dor');

-- Bed Status
CREATE TYPE bed_status_type AS ENUM ('available', 'occupied', 'reserved', 'cleaning', 'maintenance', 'blocked');

-- Order Status
CREATE TYPE order_status_type AS ENUM ('pending', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled', 'on_hold');

-- Priority Levels
CREATE TYPE priority_type AS ENUM ('routine', 'urgent', 'stat', 'emergency');

-- Payment Status
CREATE TYPE payment_status_type AS ENUM ('pending', 'partial', 'paid', 'refunded', 'cancelled', 'write_off');

-- Payment Methods
CREATE TYPE payment_method_type AS ENUM ('cash', 'card', 'upi', 'bank_transfer', 'cheque', 'mobile_banking', 'insurance', 'corporate', 'other');

-- Shift Types
CREATE TYPE shift_type AS ENUM ('morning', 'evening', 'night', 'general', 'split');

-- Employment Types
CREATE TYPE employment_type AS ENUM ('permanent', 'contract', 'probation', 'intern', 'consultant', 'visiting');

-- Leave Status
CREATE TYPE leave_status_type AS ENUM ('pending', 'approved', 'rejected', 'cancelled');

-- Maintenance Priority
CREATE TYPE maintenance_priority_type AS ENUM ('critical', 'high', 'normal', 'low');

-- Ticket Status
CREATE TYPE ticket_status_type AS ENUM ('open', 'assigned', 'in_progress', 'pending_parts', 'resolved', 'closed');

-- Consent Status
CREATE TYPE consent_status_type AS ENUM ('pending', 'obtained', 'refused', 'withdrawn', 'not_applicable');

-- Insurance Claim Status
CREATE TYPE claim_status_type AS ENUM ('draft', 'submitted', 'under_review', 'query_raised', 'approved', 'partial_approved', 'rejected', 'settled', 'appealed');

-- Specimen Status
CREATE TYPE specimen_status_type AS ENUM ('ordered', 'collected', 'in_transit', 'received', 'processing', 'completed', 'rejected');

-- Result Status
CREATE TYPE result_status_type AS ENUM ('pending', 'preliminary', 'final', 'amended', 'cancelled');

-- Document Types
CREATE TYPE document_type AS ENUM ('lab_report', 'radiology', 'prescription', 'consent', 'discharge_summary', 'insurance', 'id_proof', 'photo', 'other');

-- Notification Channels
CREATE TYPE notification_channel_type AS ENUM ('sms', 'email', 'whatsapp', 'push', 'in_app');

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 2: MULTI-TENANCY FOUNDATION
-- ============================================

-- Organizations (Top-level tenants)
CREATE TABLE organizations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    code VARCHAR(20) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    legal_name VARCHAR(255),
    organization_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'hospital',
    
    -- Contact Information
    email VARCHAR(255),
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    website VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Address
    address_line1 VARCHAR(255),
    address_line2 VARCHAR(255),
    city VARCHAR(100),
    district VARCHAR(100),
    division VARCHAR(100),
    postal_code VARCHAR(20),
    country VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Bangladesh',
    
    -- Legal & Registration
    trade_license VARCHAR(100),
    tin_number VARCHAR(50),
    bin_number VARCHAR(50),
    dghs_license VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Branding
    logo_url TEXT,
    favicon_url TEXT,
    primary_color VARCHAR(10),
    secondary_color VARCHAR(10),
    
    -- Settings (JSONB for flexibility)
    settings JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    features JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    
    -- Subscription
    subscription_plan VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'basic',
    subscription_start DATE,
    subscription_end DATE,
    max_branches INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    max_users INTEGER DEFAULT 50,
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID,
    updated_by UUID
);

-- Branches
CREATE TABLE branches (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    branch_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'hospital',
    
    -- Contact
    email VARCHAR(255),
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    emergency_phone VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Address
    address_line1 VARCHAR(255),
    address_line2 VARCHAR(255),
    city VARCHAR(100),
    district VARCHAR(100),
    division VARCHAR(100),
    postal_code VARCHAR(20),
    latitude DECIMAL(10, 8),
    longitude DECIMAL(11, 8),
    
    -- Licenses
    dghs_license VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Capacity
    total_beds INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_ots INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Settings
    settings JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    timezone VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'Asia/Dhaka',
    
    is_main_branch BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID,
    updated_by UUID,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, code)
);

-- Departments
CREATE TABLE departments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    parent_department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    department_type VARCHAR(50), -- clinical, administrative, support
    
    head_of_department_id UUID,
    
    -- Contact
    email VARCHAR(255),
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    extension VARCHAR(10),
    location VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Settings
    settings JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    color_code VARCHAR(10),
    icon VARCHAR(50),
    
    display_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    is_clinical BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, code)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 3: USER & ACCESS CONTROL
-- ============================================

-- Roles
CREATE TABLE roles (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id), -- NULL for system roles
    
    code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    role_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'custom', -- system, custom
    role_level INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- hierarchy level
    
    parent_role_id UUID REFERENCES roles(id),
    
    is_system_role BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Permissions
CREATE TABLE permissions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    
    module VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    sub_module VARCHAR(100),
    action VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- create, read, update, delete, export, approve, etc.
    
    code VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Role Permissions Mapping
CREATE TABLE role_permissions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    role_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES roles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    permission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES permissions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    -- Scope restrictions
    branch_restricted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    department_restricted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    own_records_only BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID,
    
    UNIQUE(role_id, permission_id)
);

-- Users
CREATE TABLE users (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    -- Authentication
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(255),
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Profile
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name VARCHAR(100),
    full_name VARCHAR(255) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (first_name || ' ' || COALESCE(last_name, '')) STORED,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    
    gender gender_type,
    date_of_birth DATE,
    photo_url TEXT,
    
    -- Employment (if staff)
    employee_id VARCHAR(50),
    designation VARCHAR(100),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    -- Security
    is_email_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_phone_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    two_factor_enabled BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    two_factor_secret VARCHAR(255),
    
    password_changed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    must_change_password BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    failed_login_attempts INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    locked_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_login_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_login_ip INET,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, username),
    UNIQUE(organization_id, email)
);

-- User Roles Mapping
CREATE TABLE user_roles (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    role_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES roles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    -- Scope
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    is_primary BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    valid_from DATE,
    valid_until DATE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID,
    
    UNIQUE(user_id, role_id, branch_id, department_id)
);

-- User Branch Access
CREATE TABLE user_branch_access (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    is_default BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID,
    
    UNIQUE(user_id, branch_id)
);

-- User Sessions
CREATE TABLE user_sessions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    session_token VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    refresh_token VARCHAR(500),
    
    device_type VARCHAR(50),
    device_name VARCHAR(255),
    browser VARCHAR(100),
    os VARCHAR(100),
    ip_address INET,
    user_agent TEXT,
    
    location_city VARCHAR(100),
    location_country VARCHAR(100),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    last_activity_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 4: AUDIT LOGGING
-- ============================================

-- Audit Logs
CREATE TABLE audit_logs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    -- User Info
    user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    username VARCHAR(100),
    ip_address INET,
    user_agent TEXT,
    
    -- Action Details
    action VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- CREATE, UPDATE, DELETE, LOGIN, LOGOUT, VIEW, EXPORT, etc.
    module VARCHAR(100),
    sub_module VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Entity Info
    table_name VARCHAR(100),
    record_id UUID,
    
    -- Data Changes
    old_values JSONB,
    new_values JSONB,
    changed_fields TEXT[],
    
    -- Context
    description TEXT,
    metadata JSONB,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Create hypertable for audit_logs if using TimescaleDB
-- SELECT create_hypertable('audit_logs', 'created_at', if_not_exists => TRUE);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 5: MASTER DATA
-- ============================================

-- Countries
CREATE TABLE countries (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    code VARCHAR(3) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(100),
    phone_code VARCHAR(10),
    currency_code VARCHAR(3),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- Divisions (Bangladesh specific)
CREATE TABLE divisions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    country_id UUID REFERENCES countries(id),
    code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(100),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- Districts
CREATE TABLE districts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    division_id UUID REFERENCES divisions(id),
    code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(100),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- Upazilas/Thanas
CREATE TABLE upazilas (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    district_id UUID REFERENCES districts(id),
    code VARCHAR(10),
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(100),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- ICD-10 Diagnosis Codes
CREATE TABLE icd_codes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    code VARCHAR(20) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    short_description VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    long_description TEXT,
    category VARCHAR(100),
    chapter VARCHAR(100),
    is_billable BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- CPT Procedure Codes
CREATE TABLE cpt_codes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    code VARCHAR(20) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    description VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    category VARCHAR(100),
    base_rate DECIMAL(12, 2),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- Drug Database (DGDA)
CREATE TABLE drug_master (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    
    generic_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    brand_name VARCHAR(255),
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255),
    
    drug_type VARCHAR(50), -- tablet, capsule, syrup, injection, etc.
    strength VARCHAR(100),
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    therapeutic_class VARCHAR(100),
    pharmacological_class VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Regulatory
    dgda_registration VARCHAR(100),
    is_controlled BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_narcotic BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    schedule_type VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Pricing
    mrp DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Clinical
    route_of_administration VARCHAR(50),
    pregnancy_category VARCHAR(10),
    
    -- Flags
    is_generic BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    requires_prescription BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Drug Interactions
CREATE TABLE drug_interactions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    drug_id_1 UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    drug_id_2 UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    
    severity VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- mild, moderate, severe, contraindicated
    description TEXT,
    clinical_effect TEXT,
    management TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(drug_id_1, drug_id_2)
);

-- Allergens
CREATE TABLE allergens (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    code VARCHAR(50),
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    category VARCHAR(50), -- drug, food, environment, other
    description TEXT,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 6: INDEXES FOR FOUNDATION TABLES
-- ============================================

CREATE INDEX idx_organizations_code ON organizations(code);
CREATE INDEX idx_organizations_active ON organizations(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_branches_org ON branches(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_branches_code ON branches(organization_id, code);
CREATE INDEX idx_branches_active ON branches(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_departments_org ON departments(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_departments_branch ON departments(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_departments_parent ON departments(parent_department_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_users_org ON users(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_username ON users(organization_id, username);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_email ON users(organization_id, email);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_employee ON users(employee_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_department ON users(department_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_user_roles_user ON user_roles(user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_user_roles_role ON user_roles(role_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_logs_org ON audit_logs(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_logs_user ON audit_logs(user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_logs_table ON audit_logs(table_name, record_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_logs_created ON audit_logs(created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_logs_action ON audit_logs(action);

CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_generic ON drug_master(generic_name);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_brand ON drug_master(brand_name);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_search ON drug_master USING gin(
    (generic_name || ' ' || COALESCE(brand_name, '')) gin_trgm_ops
);
```


## 📁 PART 2: PATIENT MANAGEMENT

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 7: PATIENT MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Patients
CREATE TABLE patients (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    -- Unique Identifiers
    mrn VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- Medical Record Number
    old_mrn VARCHAR(50), -- For migration
    
    -- Basic Info
    title VARCHAR(20), -- Mr, Mrs, Dr, etc.
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    middle_name VARCHAR(100),
    last_name VARCHAR(100),
    full_name VARCHAR(255) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        COALESCE(title || ' ', '') || first_name || 
        COALESCE(' ' || middle_name, '') || 
        COALESCE(' ' || last_name, '')
    ) STORED,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Demographics
    gender gender_type NOT NULL,
    date_of_birth DATE,
    blood_group blood_group_type,
    marital_status marital_status_type,
    
    -- Identity Documents
    nid VARCHAR(20),
    birth_certificate_no VARCHAR(50),
    passport_no VARCHAR(50),
    passport_expiry DATE,
    
    -- Contact
    primary_phone VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    secondary_phone VARCHAR(20),
    email VARCHAR(255),
    emergency_contact_name VARCHAR(255),
    emergency_contact_phone VARCHAR(20),
    emergency_contact_relation VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Address - Present
    present_address_line1 VARCHAR(255),
    present_address_line2 VARCHAR(255),
    present_city VARCHAR(100),
    present_district VARCHAR(100),
    present_division VARCHAR(100),
    present_postal_code VARCHAR(20),
    present_country VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Bangladesh',
    
    -- Address - Permanent
    permanent_address_line1 VARCHAR(255),
    permanent_address_line2 VARCHAR(255),
    permanent_city VARCHAR(100),
    permanent_district VARCHAR(100),
    permanent_division VARCHAR(100),
    permanent_postal_code VARCHAR(20),
    permanent_country VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Bangladesh',
    
    -- Occupation & Social
    occupation VARCHAR(100),
    employer VARCHAR(255),
    religion VARCHAR(50),
    nationality VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Bangladeshi',
    
    -- Photo
    photo_url TEXT,
    
    -- Tags/Flags
    is_vip BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_corporate BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    corporate_id UUID, -- Reference to corporate company
    is_foreign BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_medico_legal BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Family Linking
    family_id UUID,
    relationship_to_head VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Registration Info
    registration_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    registered_branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    referred_by VARCHAR(255),
    referral_source VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Consent
    consent_for_sms BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    consent_for_email BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    consent_for_data_sharing BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Portal Access
    portal_enabled BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    portal_username VARCHAR(100),
    portal_password_hash VARCHAR(255),
    portal_last_login TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_deceased BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    deceased_date DATE,
    
    -- Metadata
    metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}',
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    updated_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, mrn)
);

-- Patient QR/Barcode
CREATE TABLE patient_identifiers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    identifier_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- qr_code, barcode, rfid, wristband
    identifier_value VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    issued_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    expiry_date DATE,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Patient Medical History
CREATE TABLE patient_medical_history (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    history_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- pmh, psh, family, social
    
    -- For medical/surgical history
    condition_name VARCHAR(255),
    icd_code_id UUID REFERENCES icd_codes(id),
    diagnosis_date DATE,
    is_current BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    notes TEXT,
    
    -- For surgical history
    surgery_name VARCHAR(255),
    surgery_date DATE,
    surgeon VARCHAR(255),
    hospital VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- For family history
    relative_relation VARCHAR(50),
    relative_condition VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- For social history
    smoking_status VARCHAR(50), -- never, former, current
    smoking_years INTEGER,
    packs_per_day DECIMAL(4,2),
    alcohol_status VARCHAR(50),
    alcohol_frequency VARCHAR(50),
    occupation_hazards TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Patient Allergies
CREATE TABLE patient_allergies (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    allergen_id UUID REFERENCES allergens(id),
    allergen_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    allergy_type VARCHAR(50), -- drug, food, environment, other
    
    reaction TEXT,
    severity VARCHAR(20), -- mild, moderate, severe, life_threatening
    
    onset_date DATE,
    is_confirmed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    confirmed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    confirmed_date DATE,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Patient Current Medications
CREATE TABLE patient_medications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    drug_id UUID REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    medication_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    dose VARCHAR(100),
    frequency VARCHAR(100),
    route VARCHAR(50),
    
    start_date DATE,
    end_date DATE,
    
    prescriber VARCHAR(255),
    prescriber_facility VARCHAR(255),
    
    purpose TEXT,
    notes TEXT,
    
    is_current BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Patient Chronic Conditions
CREATE TABLE patient_chronic_conditions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    condition_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    icd_code_id UUID REFERENCES icd_codes(id),
    
    diagnosis_date DATE,
    diagnosed_by VARCHAR(255),
    
    severity VARCHAR(20),
    is_controlled BOOLEAN,
    
    treatment_plan TEXT,
    notes TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Patient Immunizations
CREATE TABLE patient_immunizations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    vaccine_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    dose_number INTEGER,
    
    administration_date DATE NOT NULL,
    administered_by VARCHAR(255),
    administration_site VARCHAR(100),
    
    lot_number VARCHAR(100),
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255),
    expiry_date DATE,
    
    next_due_date DATE,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Patient Documents
CREATE TABLE patient_documents (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    document_type document_type NOT NULL,
    document_category VARCHAR(50),
    
    title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    file_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_size BIGINT,
    mime_type VARCHAR(100),
    
    upload_source VARCHAR(50), -- hospital, external, patient_portal
    source_hospital VARCHAR(255),
    
    document_date DATE,
    
    is_confidential BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Patient Family Group
CREATE TABLE patient_families (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    family_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    family_name VARCHAR(255),
    head_patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    address TEXT,
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, family_code)
);

-- Patient Death Registry
CREATE TABLE patient_death_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    death_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    place_of_death VARCHAR(255),
    
    primary_cause VARCHAR(500),
    secondary_causes TEXT[],
    
    -- Death certificate
    certificate_number VARCHAR(100),
    certificate_issued_date DATE,
    certificate_issued_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Certifying physician
    certifying_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Autopsy
    autopsy_performed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    autopsy_findings TEXT,
    
    -- For MLC cases
    is_medico_legal BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    police_station VARCHAR(255),
    police_case_no VARCHAR(100),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Patient Satisfaction Surveys
CREATE TABLE patient_surveys (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    visit_id UUID, -- Reference to OPD visit or admission
    
    survey_type VARCHAR(50), -- discharge, opd_visit, post_procedure
    
    -- Ratings (1-5)
    overall_rating INTEGER CHECK (overall_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    doctor_rating INTEGER CHECK (doctor_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    nurse_rating INTEGER CHECK (nurse_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    facility_rating INTEGER CHECK (facility_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    cleanliness_rating INTEGER CHECK (cleanliness_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    food_rating INTEGER CHECK (food_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    billing_rating INTEGER CHECK (billing_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    wait_time_rating INTEGER CHECK (wait_time_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    
    -- NPS
    nps_score INTEGER CHECK (nps_score BETWEEN 0 AND 10),
    
    -- Free text
    positive_feedback TEXT,
    improvement_suggestions TEXT,
    
    -- Metadata
    submitted_via VARCHAR(50), -- app, sms, call, kiosk
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Indexes for Patient tables
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_org ON patients(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_mrn ON patients(organization_id, mrn);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_phone ON patients(primary_phone);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_nid ON patients(nid) WHERE nid IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_name ON patients USING gin(full_name gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_name_bangla ON patients USING gin(name_bangla gin_trgm_ops) WHERE name_bangla IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_family ON patients(family_id) WHERE family_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_active ON patients(organization_id, is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_patient_allergies_patient ON patient_allergies(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_allergies_active ON patient_allergies(patient_id, is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_patient_medical_history_patient ON patient_medical_history(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_medications_patient ON patient_medications(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_medications_current ON patient_medications(patient_id, is_current) WHERE is_current = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_patient_documents_patient ON patient_documents(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_documents_type ON patient_documents(patient_id, document_type);
```


## 📁 PART 3: VISITS, APPOINTMENTS & ADMISSIONS

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 8: OPD VISITS & APPOINTMENTS
-- ============================================

-- Doctor Profiles
CREATE TABLE doctors (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    -- Professional Info
    doctor_code VARCHAR(50),
    bmdc_registration VARCHAR(50),
    bmdc_expiry_date DATE,
    
    specialization VARCHAR(100),
    sub_specialization VARCHAR(100),
    
    qualification TEXT,
    experience_years INTEGER,
    
    -- Fees
    opd_fee DECIMAL(10, 2),
    followup_fee DECIMAL(10, 2),
    telemedicine_fee DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Settings
    consultation_duration INTEGER DEFAULT 15, -- minutes
    max_patients_per_slot INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    
    -- Digital
    digital_signature_url TEXT,
    
    is_available_opd BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_available_telemedicine BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_visiting BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, doctor_code)
);

-- Doctor Schedule Templates
CREATE TABLE doctor_schedule_templates (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    day_of_week INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (day_of_week BETWEEN 0 AND 6), -- 0=Sunday
    
    -- Session 1
    session1_start TIME,
    session1_end TIME,
    session1_max_patients INTEGER,
    session1_room VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Session 2
    session2_start TIME,
    session2_end TIME,
    session2_max_patients INTEGER,
    session2_room VARCHAR(50),
    
    consultation_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'opd', -- opd, telemedicine
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(doctor_id, branch_id, day_of_week, consultation_type)
);

-- Doctor Schedule Overrides (Leaves, Holidays)
CREATE TABLE doctor_schedule_overrides (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    override_date DATE NOT NULL,
    override_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- leave, holiday, extra_session
    
    -- If extra session
    session_start TIME,
    session_end TIME,
    max_patients INTEGER,
    room VARCHAR(50),
    
    reason TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Appointments
CREATE TABLE appointments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    -- Booking Info
    appointment_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    token_number INTEGER,
    
    -- Parties
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    -- Schedule
    appointment_date DATE NOT NULL,
    appointment_time TIME NOT NULL,
    appointment_end_time TIME,
    slot_duration INTEGER DEFAULT 15, -- minutes
    
    -- Type
    appointment_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'new', -- new, followup, review
    consultation_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'opd', -- opd, telemedicine
    visit_type patient_type DEFAULT 'opd',
    
    -- Status
    status visit_status_type DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    
    -- Check-in/out
    checked_in_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_start_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_end_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Telemedicine specific
    video_link TEXT,
    meeting_password VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Referral
    referred_by_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    referral_notes TEXT,
    
    -- Booking Details
    booked_via VARCHAR(50), -- counter, app, web, call
    booked_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Cancellation
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    cancelled_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Rescheduling
    rescheduled_from UUID REFERENCES appointments(id),
    rescheduled_to UUID REFERENCES appointments(id),
    
    -- Payment
    consultation_fee DECIMAL(10, 2),
    payment_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, appointment_number)
);

-- OPD Visits (Consultation Records)
CREATE TABLE opd_visits (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    visit_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID REFERENCES appointments(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    visit_date DATE NOT NULL,
    visit_time TIME,
    
    visit_type VARCHAR(50), -- new, followup, routine_checkup, emergency_opd
    
    -- Chief Complaint
    chief_complaint TEXT,
    complaint_duration VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Vitals (can be recorded by nurse before consultation)
    vitals_recorded BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    vitals_recorded_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    vitals_recorded_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Consultation
    consultation_start_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_end_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_duration INTEGER, -- minutes, auto-calculated
    
    -- Status
    status visit_status_type DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    
    -- Follow-up
    followup_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    followup_date DATE,
    followup_notes TEXT,
    
    -- Admission Decision
    admission_advised BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- MLC
    is_medico_legal BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    mlc_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, visit_number)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 9: IPD ADMISSIONS
-- ============================================

-- Admissions
CREATE TABLE admissions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    -- Admission Info
    admission_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    ip_number VARCHAR(50), -- Inpatient number
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- Dates
    admission_date DATE NOT NULL,
    admission_time TIME NOT NULL,
    expected_discharge_date DATE,
    actual_discharge_date DATE,
    actual_discharge_time TIME,
    
    -- Type
    admission_type VARCHAR(50), -- planned, emergency, transfer_in, daycare
    admission_source VARCHAR(50), -- opd, emergency, direct, referral
    
    -- Status
    status admission_status_type DEFAULT 'admitted',
    
    -- Medical Team
    primary_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    admitting_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    consulting_doctors UUID[], -- Array of doctor IDs
    
    -- Department/Ward
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    admitting_diagnosis TEXT,
    
    -- Bed Info (current)
    current_ward_id UUID,
    current_bed_id UUID,
    
    -- Provisional Diagnosis
    provisional_diagnosis TEXT,
    icd_codes UUID[], -- Array of ICD code IDs
    
    -- Surgery Info
    surgery_planned BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    surgery_date DATE,
    
    -- MLC
    is_medico_legal BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    mlc_number VARCHAR(50),
    police_case_number VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Consent
    general_consent_obtained BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Attendant
    attendant_name VARCHAR(255),
    attendant_phone VARCHAR(20),
    attendant_relation VARCHAR(50),
    attendant_address TEXT,
    
    -- Referral
    referred_from VARCHAR(255),
    referral_letter_url TEXT,
    
    -- Package/Insurance
    is_package BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    package_id UUID,
    is_insurance BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    insurance_preauth_id UUID,
    
    -- Discharge
    discharge_type VARCHAR(50), -- normal, lama, dor, expired, transfer
    discharge_summary_completed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, admission_number)
);

-- Admission Deposits
CREATE TABLE admission_deposits (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    deposit_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    amount DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    payment_method payment_method_type NOT NULL,
    
    transaction_reference VARCHAR(100),
    
    deposit_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    deposited_by VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- For refunds
    is_refunded BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    refund_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    refund_date DATE,
    refund_reason TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Bed Allocations (History)
CREATE TABLE bed_allocations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    bed_id UUID NOT NULL,
    
    allocation_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    release_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    allocation_type VARCHAR(50), -- initial, transfer, upgrade, downgrade
    
    daily_rate DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    allocated_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    released_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    notes TEXT
);

-- Discharge Summary
CREATE TABLE discharge_summaries (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- Dates
    discharge_date DATE NOT NULL,
    discharge_time TIME,
    
    -- Diagnoses
    admission_diagnosis TEXT,
    final_diagnosis TEXT,
    icd_codes UUID[],
    
    -- Procedures
    procedures_performed TEXT,
    cpt_codes UUID[],
    
    -- Treatment Summary
    treatment_given TEXT,
    course_in_hospital TEXT,
    
    -- Condition at Discharge
    condition_at_discharge VARCHAR(50), -- improved, unchanged, deteriorated, expired
    
    -- Instructions
    discharge_advice TEXT,
    diet_instructions TEXT,
    activity_restrictions TEXT,
    wound_care_instructions TEXT,
    
    -- Medications
    discharge_medications JSONB,
    
    -- Follow-up
    followup_date DATE,
    followup_instructions TEXT,
    
    -- References
    investigations_pending TEXT,
    referral_to VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Prepared By
    prepared_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    prepared_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- PDF
    summary_pdf_url TEXT
);

-- Emergency Visits
CREATE TABLE emergency_visits (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    emergency_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    arrival_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    arrival_mode VARCHAR(50), -- walk_in, ambulance, police, referred
    
    -- Triage
    triage_level VARCHAR(20), -- resuscitation, emergency, urgent, standard, non_urgent
    triage_color VARCHAR(20), -- red, orange, yellow, green, blue
    triage_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    triaged_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Chief Complaint
    chief_complaint TEXT,
    presenting_symptoms TEXT,
    
    -- Vitals at arrival
    initial_vitals JSONB,
    
    -- Attending
    attending_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(50), -- waiting, under_treatment, observation, admitted, discharged, expired
    
    -- Disposition
    disposition VARCHAR(50), -- discharged, admitted, referred, lama, expired
    disposition_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- If admitted
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- MLC
    is_medico_legal BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    mlc_number VARCHAR(50),
    brought_by_police BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    police_officer_name VARCHAR(255),
    police_station VARCHAR(255),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, emergency_number)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_appointments_org ON appointments(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_appointments_branch ON appointments(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_appointments_patient ON appointments(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_appointments_doctor ON appointments(doctor_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_appointments_date ON appointments(appointment_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_appointments_status ON appointments(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_appointments_doctor_date ON appointments(doctor_id, appointment_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_org ON opd_visits(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_patient ON opd_visits(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_doctor ON opd_visits(doctor_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_date ON opd_visits(visit_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_org ON admissions(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_patient ON admissions(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_status ON admissions(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_date ON admissions(admission_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_doctor ON admissions(primary_doctor_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_admissions_active ON admissions(organization_id, status) WHERE status = 'admitted';

CREATE INDEX idx_emergency_visits_org ON emergency_visits(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_emergency_visits_patient ON emergency_visits(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_emergency_visits_arrival ON emergency_visits(arrival_datetime);
```

## 📁 PART 4: EMR & CLINICAL DOCUMENTATION

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 10: EMR - ELECTRONIC MEDICAL RECORDS
-- ============================================

-- Vitals
CREATE TABLE patient_vitals (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- Context
    visit_id UUID, -- OPD visit
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id), -- IPD
    emergency_id UUID REFERENCES emergency_visits(id),
    
    recorded_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    recorded_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Vital Signs
    temperature DECIMAL(4, 1), -- Celsius
    temperature_site VARCHAR(20), -- oral, axillary, rectal, tympanic
    
    pulse_rate INTEGER, -- bpm
    pulse_rhythm VARCHAR(20), -- regular, irregular
    pulse_volume VARCHAR(20), -- normal, weak, bounding
    
    respiratory_rate INTEGER, -- per minute
    
    blood_pressure_systolic INTEGER, -- mmHg
    blood_pressure_diastolic INTEGER, -- mmHg
    blood_pressure_position VARCHAR(20), -- sitting, lying, standing
    
    spo2 INTEGER, -- percentage
    spo2_on_oxygen BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    oxygen_flow_rate DECIMAL(4, 1), -- L/min
    
    -- Measurements
    height DECIMAL(5, 2), -- cm
    weight DECIMAL(5, 2), -- kg
    bmi DECIMAL(4, 1), -- auto-calculated
    bsa DECIMAL(4, 2), -- body surface area
    
    head_circumference DECIMAL(4, 1), -- cm (for pediatrics)
    waist_circumference DECIMAL(5, 1),
    hip_circumference DECIMAL(5, 1),
    
    -- Blood Sugar
    blood_sugar DECIMAL(5, 1), -- mg/dL
    blood_sugar_type VARCHAR(20), -- fasting, random, pp
    
    -- Pain
    pain_score INTEGER CHECK (pain_score BETWEEN 0 AND 10),
    pain_location VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Neurological
    gcs_eye INTEGER CHECK (gcs_eye BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    gcs_verbal INTEGER CHECK (gcs_verbal BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    gcs_motor INTEGER CHECK (gcs_motor BETWEEN 1 AND 6),
    gcs_total INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (gcs_eye + gcs_verbal + gcs_motor) STORED,
    
    pupil_left VARCHAR(50),
    pupil_right VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Assessment Scores
    fall_risk_score INTEGER,
    pressure_ulcer_risk INTEGER, -- Braden scale
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Clinical Notes
CREATE TABLE clinical_notes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- Context
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    emergency_id UUID REFERENCES emergency_visits(id),
    
    note_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
    -- consultation_note, progress_note, nursing_note, procedure_note, 
    -- handoff_note, discharge_note, telephone_note
    
    note_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    author_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- SOAP Format
    subjective TEXT,
    objective TEXT,
    assessment TEXT,
    plan TEXT,
    
    -- Or free text
    note_text TEXT,
    
    -- Structured data
    structured_data JSONB,
    
    -- Signing
    is_signed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    signed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Co-sign (for residents)
    requires_cosign BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    cosigned_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    cosigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Amendments
    is_amended BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    amendment_reason TEXT,
    amended_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Confidential
    is_confidential BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Diagnoses (for visits/admissions)
CREATE TABLE clinical_diagnoses (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    icd_code_id UUID REFERENCES icd_codes(id),
    diagnosis_code VARCHAR(20),
    diagnosis_description VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    
    diagnosis_type VARCHAR(50), -- primary, secondary, admitting, discharge
    is_primary BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    onset_date DATE,
    resolution_date DATE,
    
    diagnosed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    diagnosed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    is_chronic BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_resolved BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    notes TEXT
);

-- Procedures Performed
CREATE TABLE clinical_procedures (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    surgery_id UUID, -- Reference to OT surgery
    
    cpt_code_id UUID REFERENCES cpt_codes(id),
    procedure_code VARCHAR(20),
    procedure_name VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    
    procedure_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    performed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    assistant_ids UUID[],
    
    -- Location
    location VARCHAR(100),
    laterality VARCHAR(20), -- left, right, bilateral
    
    -- Anesthesia
    anesthesia_type VARCHAR(50),
    anesthesia_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Details
    indications TEXT,
    procedure_description TEXT,
    findings TEXT,
    complications TEXT,
    
    -- Duration
    start_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    end_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    duration_minutes INTEGER,
    
    -- Specimens
    specimen_sent BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    specimen_details TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Doctor Templates
CREATE TABLE doctor_templates (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id), -- NULL for organization-wide
    
    template_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- chief_complaint, examination, diagnosis, prescription, advice
    category VARCHAR(100),
    
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    shortcut VARCHAR(50),
    
    content TEXT NOT NULL,
    structured_content JSONB,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    usage_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Indexes for EMR
CREATE INDEX idx_vitals_patient ON patient_vitals(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_vitals_admission ON patient_vitals(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_vitals_datetime ON patient_vitals(recorded_datetime DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_clinical_notes_patient ON clinical_notes(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_clinical_notes_admission ON clinical_notes(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_clinical_notes_type ON clinical_notes(note_type);
CREATE INDEX idx_clinical_notes_datetime ON clinical_notes(note_datetime DESC);

CREATE INDEX idx_diagnoses_patient ON clinical_diagnoses(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_diagnoses_admission ON clinical_diagnoses(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_diagnoses_icd ON clinical_diagnoses(icd_code_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_procedures_patient ON clinical_procedures(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_procedures_admission ON clinical_procedures(admission_id);
```

## 📁 PART 5: PRESCRIPTION (Rx) MANAGEMENT

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 11: PRESCRIPTION MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Prescriptions
CREATE TABLE prescriptions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    prescription_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    -- Context
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    prescription_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    prescription_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    prescription_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'opd', -- opd, ipd, discharge, telemedicine
    
    -- Clinical Context
    diagnosis TEXT,
    icd_codes UUID[],
    
    -- Patient Info Snapshot
    patient_age_at_prescription VARCHAR(50),
    patient_weight DECIMAL(5, 2),
    
    -- Allergy Warning Acknowledged
    allergy_warning_shown BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    allergy_warning_acknowledged BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Interaction Warning
    interaction_warning_shown BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    interaction_warning_acknowledged BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Advice & Instructions
    general_advice TEXT,
    diet_advice TEXT,
    exercise_advice TEXT,
    
    -- Follow-up
    followup_date DATE,
    followup_instructions TEXT,
    
    -- Signature
    is_signed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    digital_signature_url TEXT,
    
    -- Dispensing
    is_dispensed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    dispensed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    dispensed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- PDF
    prescription_pdf_url TEXT,
    qr_code VARCHAR(255),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, prescription_number)
);

-- Prescription Items (Medications)
CREATE TABLE prescription_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES prescriptions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    item_order INTEGER NOT NULL,
    
    -- Drug Info
    drug_id UUID REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    drug_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    generic_name VARCHAR(255),
    is_generic BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Dosage
    strength VARCHAR(100),
    dose VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, -- e.g., "1 tablet", "5ml"
    dose_unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Frequency
    frequency VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, -- e.g., "1+0+1", "TID", "QID"
    frequency_times INTEGER, -- times per day
    specific_times TIME[], -- specific times if needed
    
    -- Duration
    duration INTEGER, -- number of days
    duration_unit VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'days', -- days, weeks, months
    
    -- Route
    route VARCHAR(50), -- oral, iv, im, topical, etc.
    
    -- Instructions
    instructions TEXT, -- Before food, After food, etc.
    instructions_bangla TEXT,
    
    -- Quantity
    quantity_prescribed INTEGER,
    quantity_unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Refill
    refills_allowed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    refills_remaining INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Dispensing
    quantity_dispensed INTEGER,
    is_dispensed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Substitution
    allow_substitution BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    substitution_reason TEXT,
    
    -- PRN (As needed)
    is_prn BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    prn_reason TEXT,
    max_daily_dose VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Controlled
    is_controlled BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Warnings
    has_interaction BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    interaction_details TEXT,
    has_allergy_warning BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    allergy_details TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Prescription Refill Log
CREATE TABLE prescription_refills (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES prescriptions(id),
    prescription_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES prescription_items(id),
    
    refill_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
    refill_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    quantity_dispensed INTEGER,
    
    dispensed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    pharmacy_location VARCHAR(255),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Prescription Templates
CREATE TABLE prescription_templates (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    category VARCHAR(100), -- e.g., "Hypertension", "Diabetes"
    description TEXT,
    
    -- Template items stored as JSONB
    medications JSONB NOT NULL,
    advice TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    usage_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Narcotic Drug Log
CREATE TABLE narcotic_prescriptions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES prescriptions(id),
    prescription_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES prescription_items(id),
    
    drug_id UUID REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    drug_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    quantity_prescribed INTEGER NOT NULL,
    
    -- Patient Info
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    patient_name VARCHAR(255),
    patient_nid VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Doctor Info
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    doctor_bmdc VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Register Entry
    register_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    dispensed_quantity INTEGER,
    dispensed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    dispensed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    witness_name VARCHAR(255),
    witness_id VARCHAR(50),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_prescriptions_org ON prescriptions(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_prescriptions_patient ON prescriptions(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_prescriptions_doctor ON prescriptions(doctor_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_prescriptions_date ON prescriptions(prescription_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_prescriptions_admission ON prescriptions(admission_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_prescription_items_rx ON prescription_items(prescription_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_prescription_items_drug ON prescription_items(drug_id);
```


## 📁 PART 6: NURSE STATION MANAGEMENT

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 12: NURSE STATION MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Nurse Profiles
CREATE TABLE nurses (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    nurse_code VARCHAR(50),
    nursing_council_reg VARCHAR(50),
    registration_expiry DATE,
    
    qualification VARCHAR(255),
    specialization VARCHAR(100),
    
    experience_years INTEGER,
    
    current_ward_id UUID,
    current_shift shift_type,
    
    is_head_nurse BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, nurse_code)
);

-- Nurse Duty Roster
CREATE TABLE nurse_duty_roster (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    nurse_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nurses(id),
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    
    duty_date DATE NOT NULL,
    shift shift_type NOT NULL,
    
    shift_start_time TIME NOT NULL,
    shift_end_time TIME NOT NULL,
    
    actual_start_time TIME,
    actual_end_time TIME,
    
    is_on_call BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'scheduled', -- scheduled, completed, absent, swapped
    
    swapped_with_nurse_id UUID REFERENCES nurses(id),
    swap_approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    UNIQUE(nurse_id, duty_date, shift)
);

-- Nurse Handover Notes
CREATE TABLE nurse_handover_notes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    
    handover_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    outgoing_nurse_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nurses(id),
    incoming_nurse_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    outgoing_shift shift_type NOT NULL,
    incoming_shift shift_type NOT NULL,
    
    -- Summary
    total_patients INTEGER,
    critical_patients INTEGER,
    new_admissions INTEGER,
    expected_discharges INTEGER,
    
    -- Notes
    general_notes TEXT,
    critical_patient_notes TEXT,
    pending_tasks TEXT,
    special_instructions TEXT,
    
    -- Acknowledgment
    acknowledged_by_incoming BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    acknowledged_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Patient Handover Details
CREATE TABLE nurse_handover_patients (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    handover_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nurse_handover_notes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    bed_number VARCHAR(20),
    
    diagnosis TEXT,
    current_status TEXT,
    
    -- Concerns/Alerts
    critical_alerts TEXT,
    pending_orders TEXT,
    special_care TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT
);

-- Doctor Orders (for Nurse execution)
CREATE TABLE doctor_orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    order_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    ordered_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    order_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    order_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
    -- medication, lab_test, radiology, diet, nursing_care, 
    -- procedure, consultation, monitoring, iv_fluid, blood_transfusion
    
    priority priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    
    -- Order Details
    order_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    structured_order JSONB,
    
    -- Frequency (for recurring orders)
    frequency VARCHAR(50), -- once, daily, bid, tid, qid, prn
    start_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    end_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    status order_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    -- Execution
    executed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    executed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    execution_notes TEXT,
    
    -- Discontinuation
    discontinued_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    discontinued_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    discontinuation_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Clarification
    clarification_requested BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    clarification_request_text TEXT,
    clarification_response TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, order_number)
);

-- Order Execution Log
CREATE TABLE order_execution_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctor_orders(id),
    
    execution_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    executed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    action VARCHAR(50), -- executed, held, refused, missed
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Medication Administration Record (MAR)
CREATE TABLE medication_administration_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    order_id UUID REFERENCES doctor_orders(id),
    
    drug_id UUID REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    medication_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    dose VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    route VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Scheduled
    scheduled_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    
    -- Actual Administration
    administered_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    administered_by UUID REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    -- Five Rights Verified
    right_patient BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    right_drug BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    right_dose BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    right_route BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    right_time BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Barcode Verification
    patient_barcode_scanned BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    medication_barcode_scanned BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(50), -- administered, held, refused, missed, delayed
    
    -- If not administered
    reason_not_given TEXT,
    
    -- Witness (for narcotics)
    witness_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- IV Fluid Chart
CREATE TABLE iv_fluid_charts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    order_id UUID REFERENCES doctor_orders(id),
    
    fluid_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    volume_ml INTEGER NOT NULL,
    additives TEXT,
    
    start_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    end_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    rate_ml_per_hour INTEGER,
    
    site VARCHAR(50), -- right_hand, left_hand, etc.
    cannula_size VARCHAR(20),
    
    started_by UUID REFERENCES nurses(id),
    completed_by UUID REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    status VARCHAR(50), -- running, completed, stopped
    stop_reason TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Intake Output Chart
CREATE TABLE intake_output_charts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    recorded_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    recorded_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    chart_date DATE NOT NULL,
    shift shift_type,
    hour INTEGER CHECK (hour BETWEEN 0 AND 23),
    
    -- Intake (ml)
    oral_intake INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    iv_fluid_intake INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    blood_intake INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    ng_tube_intake INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    other_intake INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_intake INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        COALESCE(oral_intake, 0) + COALESCE(iv_fluid_intake, 0) + 
        COALESCE(blood_intake, 0) + COALESCE(ng_tube_intake, 0) + 
        COALESCE(other_intake, 0)
    ) STORED,
    
    -- Output (ml)
    urine_output INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    vomit_output INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    drain_output INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    ng_aspiration INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    stool_output INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    other_output INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    total_output INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        COALESCE(urine_output, 0) + COALESCE(vomit_output, 0) + 
        COALESCE(drain_output, 0) + COALESCE(ng_aspiration, 0) + 
        COALESCE(stool_output, 0) + COALESCE(other_output, 0)
    ) STORED,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Nursing Assessment
CREATE TABLE nursing_assessments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    assessment_type VARCHAR(50), -- initial, daily, reassessment
    assessment_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    assessed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    -- General Appearance
    general_appearance TEXT,
    consciousness_level VARCHAR(50),
    orientation TEXT,
    
    -- System Review
    neurological_assessment JSONB,
    cardiovascular_assessment JSONB,
    respiratory_assessment JSONB,
    gastrointestinal_assessment JSONB,
    genitourinary_assessment JSONB,
    musculoskeletal_assessment JSONB,
    skin_assessment JSONB,
    
    -- Risk Assessments
    fall_risk_score INTEGER,
    fall_risk_level VARCHAR(20),
    pressure_ulcer_score INTEGER, -- Braden
    pressure_ulcer_risk VARCHAR(20),
    nutritional_score INTEGER,
    nutritional_risk VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- ADL Assessment
    mobility_level VARCHAR(50),
    self_care_ability VARCHAR(50),
    communication_ability VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Psychosocial
    emotional_status VARCHAR(100),
    family_support TEXT,
    
    -- Care Plan
    nursing_problems TEXT[],
    nursing_goals TEXT,
    nursing_interventions TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Nursing Care Log
CREATE TABLE nursing_care_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    care_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    care_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
    -- bath, oral_care, position_change, wound_dressing, 
    -- catheter_care, ng_care, tracheostomy_care, etc.
    
    details TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Wound Care Documentation
CREATE TABLE wound_care_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    wound_id VARCHAR(50), -- For tracking multiple wounds
    wound_location VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    wound_type VARCHAR(50), -- surgical, pressure, traumatic, etc.
    
    assessment_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    assessed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    -- Wound Assessment
    length_cm DECIMAL(5,2),
    width_cm DECIMAL(5,2),
    depth_cm DECIMAL(5,2),
    
    wound_bed_description TEXT,
    exudate_type VARCHAR(50),
    exudate_amount VARCHAR(50),
    odor VARCHAR(50),
    surrounding_skin TEXT,
    signs_of_infection BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    infection_signs TEXT,
    
    -- Dressing
    dressing_type VARCHAR(255),
    dressing_changed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    photo_url TEXT,
    
    healing_progress VARCHAR(50),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Specimen Collection
CREATE TABLE specimen_collections (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    order_id UUID REFERENCES doctor_orders(id),
    lab_order_id UUID, -- Reference to lab orders
    
    specimen_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    specimen_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, -- blood, urine, stool, sputum, etc.
    collection_container VARCHAR(100),
    
    collected_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    collected_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    collection_site VARCHAR(100),
    quantity VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Verification
    patient_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    barcode_label_printed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Transport
    sent_to_lab_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    sent_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    received_in_lab_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    received_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    status specimen_status_type DEFAULT 'collected',
    
    -- If rejected
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    recollection_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, specimen_number)
);

-- Patient Call Bell Log
CREATE TABLE call_bell_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    bed_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    call_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    urgency VARCHAR(20), -- normal, urgent, emergency
    reason VARCHAR(255),
    
    responded_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    response_time_seconds INTEGER,
    responded_by UUID REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    resolution TEXT,
    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_nurse_roster_nurse ON nurse_duty_roster(nurse_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_nurse_roster_date ON nurse_duty_roster(duty_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_nurse_roster_ward ON nurse_duty_roster(ward_id, duty_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_doctor_orders_patient ON doctor_orders(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_doctor_orders_admission ON doctor_orders(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_doctor_orders_status ON doctor_orders(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_doctor_orders_pending ON doctor_orders(admission_id, status) WHERE status = 'pending';

CREATE INDEX idx_mar_admission ON medication_administration_records(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_mar_scheduled ON medication_administration_records(scheduled_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_mar_patient_date ON medication_administration_records(patient_id, scheduled_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_io_chart_admission ON intake_output_charts(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_io_chart_date ON intake_output_charts(chart_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_specimen_patient ON specimen_collections(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_specimen_status ON specimen_collections(status);
```
