## 📁 PART 7: BED & WARD MANAGEMENT

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 13: BED & WARD MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Wards
CREATE TABLE wards (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(100),
    
    ward_type VARCHAR(50), -- general, private, icu, nicu, picu, ccu, hdu, maternity, pediatric, psychiatric
    
    building VARCHAR(50),
    floor VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Capacity
    total_beds INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Gender Policy
    gender_restriction gender_type, -- NULL means both allowed
    
    -- Nursing Station
    nursing_station_phone VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Head Nurse
    head_nurse_id UUID REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    -- Settings
    visiting_hours TEXT,
    max_attendants INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    
    color_code VARCHAR(10),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, code)
);

-- Rooms
CREATE TABLE rooms (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES wards(id),
    
    room_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    
    room_type VARCHAR(50), -- general, private, semi_private, suite, isolation
    
    -- Capacity
    total_beds INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    
    -- Amenities
    has_attached_bathroom BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_ac BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_tv BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_refrigerator BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    floor_area_sqft DECIMAL(8,2),
    
    -- Rates
    daily_rate DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(ward_id, room_number)
);

-- Beds
CREATE TABLE beds (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES wards(id),
    room_id UUID REFERENCES rooms(id),
    
    bed_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    
    bed_type VARCHAR(50), -- standard, icu, nicu, bariatric, pediatric, electric, manual
    
    -- Features
    has_oxygen BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_suction BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_monitor BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_ventilator_support BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_side_rails BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Status
    status bed_status_type DEFAULT 'available',
    current_patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    current_admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- Housekeeping
    last_cleaned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_cleaned_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Maintenance
    last_maintenance_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    next_maintenance_due DATE,
    
    -- Rates
    daily_rate DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Display
    display_order INTEGER,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(ward_id, bed_number)
);

-- Bed Status History
CREATE TABLE bed_status_history (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    bed_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES beds(id),
    
    previous_status bed_status_type,
    new_status bed_status_type NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    changed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    changed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    reason TEXT
);

-- Bed Reservations
CREATE TABLE bed_reservations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    bed_id UUID REFERENCES beds(id), -- Specific bed
    ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id), -- Or just ward preference
    bed_type VARCHAR(50), -- Or just bed type preference
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    reserved_from TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    reserved_until TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    
    expected_admission_date DATE,
    surgery_date DATE,
    
    reservation_reason TEXT,
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active', -- active, confirmed, cancelled, expired
    
    reserved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reserved_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    confirmed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    confirmed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    cancelled_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Bed Transfer Requests
CREATE TABLE bed_transfer_requests (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- From
    from_ward_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES wards(id),
    from_bed_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES beds(id),
    
    -- To
    to_ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id),
    to_bed_id UUID REFERENCES beds(id),
    
    transfer_type VARCHAR(50), -- upgrade, downgrade, icu_escalation, step_down, same_ward, patient_request
    
    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    
    requested_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- Approval
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, approved, rejected, completed, cancelled
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Execution
    transferred_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    transferred_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Rate change
    old_daily_rate DECIMAL(10, 2),
    new_daily_rate DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Bed Cleaning Tasks
CREATE TABLE bed_cleaning_tasks (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    bed_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES beds(id),
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES wards(id),
    
    cleaning_type VARCHAR(50), -- routine, discharge, terminal, isolation
    
    -- Trigger
    triggered_by VARCHAR(50), -- discharge, transfer, manual, scheduled
    previous_patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- Assignment
    assigned_to UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Progress
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Checklist
    checklist JSONB,
    
    -- QA
    qa_checked BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    qa_checked_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    qa_checked_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    qa_passed BOOLEAN,
    qa_remarks TEXT,
    
    -- Photos
    before_photos TEXT[],
    after_photos TEXT[],
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, assigned, in_progress, completed, qa_pending, approved
    
    notes TEXT
);

-- Ward Census (Daily snapshot)
CREATE TABLE ward_census (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES wards(id),
    
    census_date DATE NOT NULL,
    census_time TIME DEFAULT '00:00',
    
    total_beds INTEGER,
    occupied_beds INTEGER,
    available_beds INTEGER,
    reserved_beds INTEGER,
    cleaning_beds INTEGER,
    maintenance_beds INTEGER,
    
    occupancy_percentage DECIMAL(5,2),
    
    -- Patient movements
    admissions_today INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    discharges_today INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    transfers_in INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    transfers_out INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    deaths_today INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    recorded_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(ward_id, census_date)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_wards_branch ON wards(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_wards_type ON wards(ward_type);

CREATE INDEX idx_beds_ward ON beds(ward_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_beds_status ON beds(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_beds_available ON beds(ward_id, status) WHERE status = 'available';

CREATE INDEX idx_bed_reservations_bed ON bed_reservations(bed_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_bed_reservations_patient ON bed_reservations(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_bed_reservations_dates ON bed_reservations(reserved_from, reserved_until);

CREATE INDEX idx_bed_cleaning_bed ON bed_cleaning_tasks(bed_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_bed_cleaning_status ON bed_cleaning_tasks(status);
```

## 📁 PART 8: OT (OPERATION THEATRE) MANAGEMENT

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 14: OPERATION THEATRE MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- OT Rooms
CREATE TABLE ot_rooms (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    room_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100),
    
    ot_type VARCHAR(50), -- major, minor, emergency, cardiac, neuro, ortho, eye, ent
    
    -- Specifications
    floor_area_sqft DECIMAL(8,2),
    laminar_flow BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Equipment List
    equipment JSONB,
    
    -- Rates
    hourly_rate DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'available', -- available, in_use, cleaning, maintenance
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, room_number)
);

-- Surgery Types/Procedures
CREATE TABLE surgery_types (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    category VARCHAR(100), -- general, cardiac, neuro, ortho, etc.
    sub_category VARCHAR(100),
    
    cpt_code_id UUID REFERENCES cpt_codes(id),
    
    typical_duration_minutes INTEGER,
    
    ot_type_required VARCHAR(50),
    anesthesia_type_typical VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Charges
    surgeon_fee DECIMAL(10, 2),
    ot_charge DECIMAL(10, 2),
    anesthesia_charge DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    requires_blood_reservation BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    requires_icu_booking BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    pre_op_instructions TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, code)
);

-- Surgery Scheduling
CREATE TABLE surgeries (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    surgery_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- Scheduling
    scheduled_date DATE NOT NULL,
    scheduled_start_time TIME NOT NULL,
    scheduled_end_time TIME,
    estimated_duration_minutes INTEGER,
    
    ot_room_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES ot_rooms(id),
    
    surgery_type_id UUID REFERENCES surgery_types(id),
    surgery_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    surgery_category VARCHAR(50), -- elective, emergency, daycare
    
    -- Laterality
    laterality VARCHAR(20), -- left, right, bilateral, not_applicable
    
    -- Team
    primary_surgeon_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    assistant_surgeon_ids UUID[],
    anesthesiologist_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    scrub_nurse_id UUID REFERENCES nurses(id),
    circulating_nurse_id UUID REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    -- Anesthesia Plan
    anesthesia_type VARCHAR(50), -- general, spinal, epidural, local, regional
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'scheduled', 
    -- scheduled, pre_op_started, in_ot, surgery_started, surgery_completed, in_pacu, completed, cancelled
    
    -- Actual Times
    actual_start_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    actual_end_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    actual_duration_minutes INTEGER,
    
    -- Cancellation
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    cancelled_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Pre-Auth
    insurance_preauth_id UUID,
    preauth_approved BOOLEAN,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, surgery_number)
);

-- Pre-Operative Checklist
CREATE TABLE surgery_preop_checklist (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    surgery_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES surgeries(id),
    
    -- Identity
    patient_identity_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    consent_signed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    site_marked BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Clinical
    allergies_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    fasting_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    fasting_hours INTEGER,
    
    -- Preparations
    preop_bath_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    jewelry_removed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    dentures_removed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    nail_polish_removed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    hospital_gown_worn BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    id_band_on BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Investigations
    blood_group_confirmed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    cross_match_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    blood_units_reserved INTEGER,
    
    investigations_reviewed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    ecg_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    chest_xray_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Medications
    preop_medications_given BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    antibiotic_prophylaxis BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    current_medications_held TEXT,
    
    -- Anesthesia
    anesthesia_clearance BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    asa_grade VARCHAR(10),
    mallampati_score INTEGER,
    
    -- WHO Checklist Sign In
    who_sign_in_completed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    who_sign_in_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    who_sign_in_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    completed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Intra-Operative Record
CREATE TABLE surgery_intraop_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    surgery_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES surgeries(id),
    
    -- Times
    patient_in_ot_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    anesthesia_start_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    incision_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    closure_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    anesthesia_end_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    patient_out_ot_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Positioning
    patient_position VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Anesthesia Details
    anesthesia_type VARCHAR(50),
    anesthesia_drugs JSONB,
    airway_management VARCHAR(100),
    ett_size VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Vitals during surgery (array of readings)
    intraop_vitals JSONB,
    
    -- IV Access
    iv_access_site VARCHAR(100),
    iv_cannula_size VARCHAR(20),
    central_line BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    central_line_site VARCHAR(50),
    arterial_line BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Fluids
    iv_fluids_given JSONB,
    estimated_blood_loss_ml INTEGER,
    blood_transfused JSONB,
    urine_output_ml INTEGER,
    
    -- Counts
    instrument_count_before INTEGER,
    instrument_count_after INTEGER,
    instrument_count_correct BOOLEAN,
    swab_count_before INTEGER,
    swab_count_after INTEGER,
    swab_count_correct BOOLEAN,
    needle_count_correct BOOLEAN,
    
    -- Specimens
    specimen_sent BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    specimen_details TEXT,
    frozen_section BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    frozen_section_result TEXT,
    
    -- Implants
    implants_used JSONB, -- [{name, manufacturer, lot_number, size, expiry}]
    
    -- WHO Checklist
    who_time_out_completed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    who_time_out_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    who_sign_out_completed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    who_sign_out_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Surgical Notes
    procedure_performed TEXT,
    findings TEXT,
    complications TEXT,
    
    -- Photos/Videos
    surgical_photos TEXT[],
    surgical_video_url TEXT,
    
    recorded_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Anesthesia Record
CREATE TABLE anesthesia_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    surgery_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES surgeries(id),
    
    anesthesiologist_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    -- Pre-Anesthesia Assessment
    asa_classification VARCHAR(10),
    airway_assessment JSONB,
    previous_anesthesia_history TEXT,
    anticipated_difficulty TEXT,
    
    -- Anesthesia Plan
    anesthesia_type VARCHAR(50),
    anesthesia_technique TEXT,
    
    -- Pre-Medication
    premedication JSONB,
    
    -- Induction
    induction_agents JSONB,
    airway_device VARCHAR(100),
    ett_size VARCHAR(20),
    cuff_pressure DECIMAL(4,1),
    
    -- Maintenance
    maintenance_agents JSONB,
    muscle_relaxants JSONB,
    
    -- Monitoring
    monitoring_devices TEXT[],
    
    -- Vital Signs (time series)
    vital_signs_chart JSONB,
    
    -- Drugs Administered
    drugs_administered JSONB,
    
    -- Fluids & Blood
    fluid_balance JSONB,
    
    -- Events
    intraop_events TEXT,
    
    -- Recovery
    reversal_agents JSONB,
    extubation_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    extubation_status VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Post-Anesthesia Instructions
    post_op_orders TEXT,
    pain_management_plan TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Post-Operative Record (PACU)
CREATE TABLE surgery_postop_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    surgery_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES surgeries(id),
    
    -- PACU Arrival
    pacu_arrival_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    received_by UUID REFERENCES nurses(id),
    
    -- Initial Assessment
    initial_vitals JSONB,
    consciousness_level VARCHAR(50),
    airway_status VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Aldrete Score (every 15 mins)
    aldrete_scores JSONB, -- [{time, activity, respiration, circulation, consciousness, oxygen_saturation, total}]
    
    -- Pain Management
    pain_scores JSONB,
    analgesics_given JSONB,
    
    -- Complications
    nausea_vomiting BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    antiemetic_given JSONB,
    
    shivering BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    hypothermia BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    complications TEXT,
    
    -- Discharge from PACU
    pacu_discharge_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    discharge_aldrete_score INTEGER,
    discharge_destination VARCHAR(50), -- ward, icu, hdu
    
    discharged_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- OT Sterilization Log
CREATE TABLE ot_sterilization_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    ot_room_id UUID REFERENCES ot_rooms(id),
    
    sterilization_type VARCHAR(50), -- fumigation, uv, terminal_cleaning
    
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    performed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    chemicals_used TEXT,
    duration_minutes INTEGER,
    
    verification_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    next_sterilization_due TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- OT Equipment Usage
CREATE TABLE ot_equipment_usage (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    surgery_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES surgeries(id),
    
    equipment_id UUID, -- Reference to equipment registry
    equipment_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    usage_start_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    usage_end_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    condition_before VARCHAR(50),
    condition_after VARCHAR(50),
    
    issues_reported TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_surgeries_org ON surgeries(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_surgeries_patient ON surgeries(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_surgeries_date ON surgeries(scheduled_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_surgeries_ot ON surgeries(ot_room_id, scheduled_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_surgeries_surgeon ON surgeries(primary_surgeon_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_surgeries_status ON surgeries(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_surgeries_admission ON surgeries(admission_id);
```

## 📁 PART 9: DIAGNOSTIC MANAGEMENT (LAB & RADIOLOGY)

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 15: DIAGNOSTIC MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Test Catalog
CREATE TABLE test_catalog (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    
    department VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- biochemistry, hematology, microbiology, pathology, radiology
    sub_department VARCHAR(50),
    category VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Test Type
    test_type VARCHAR(50), -- single, panel, profile
    
    -- For panels/profiles
    is_panel BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    panel_tests UUID[], -- Array of test IDs
    
    -- Specimen
    specimen_type VARCHAR(100),
    specimen_container VARCHAR(100),
    specimen_volume VARCHAR(50),
    collection_instructions TEXT,
    
    -- Processing
    processing_department VARCHAR(50),
    method VARCHAR(255),
    equipment VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Turnaround Time
    tat_routine_hours INTEGER,
    tat_urgent_hours INTEGER,
    tat_stat_minutes INTEGER,
    
    -- Reporting
    report_format VARCHAR(50), -- numeric, text, image
    result_unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Pricing
    price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Clinical
    loinc_code VARCHAR(20),
    cpt_code_id UUID REFERENCES cpt_codes(id),
    
    -- Flags
    requires_fasting BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_outsourced BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    outsource_lab VARCHAR(255),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, code)
);

-- Test Reference Ranges
CREATE TABLE test_reference_ranges (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    test_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_catalog(id),
    
    -- Demographics
    gender gender_type, -- NULL for all
    age_min_years DECIMAL(5,2),
    age_max_years DECIMAL(5,2),
    age_min_days INTEGER, -- For neonates
    age_max_days INTEGER,
    
    -- Normal Range
    normal_min DECIMAL(15, 5),
    normal_max DECIMAL(15, 5),
    normal_text VARCHAR(255), -- For non-numeric results
    
    -- Critical Values
    critical_low DECIMAL(15, 5),
    critical_high DECIMAL(15, 5),
    
    -- Panic Values
    panic_low DECIMAL(15, 5),
    panic_high DECIMAL(15, 5),
    
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    interpretation_guide TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Lab Orders
CREATE TABLE lab_orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    order_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    visit_id UUID, -- OPD
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id), -- IPD
    
    ordered_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    order_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    priority priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    
    -- Clinical Info
    clinical_history TEXT,
    provisional_diagnosis TEXT,
    
    -- Fasting Status
    is_fasting BOOLEAN,
    last_meal_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'ordered', 
    -- ordered, collected, received, processing, partially_completed, completed, cancelled
    
    -- Billing
    billing_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    bill_id UUID,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, order_number)
);

-- Lab Order Items
CREATE TABLE lab_order_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES lab_orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    test_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_catalog(id),
    test_code VARCHAR(20),
    test_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    priority priority_type,
    
    -- Specimen
    specimen_id UUID, -- Reference to specimen_collections
    specimen_collected BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    specimen_received BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Processing
    processing_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    processing_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Status
    status result_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    -- If cancelled
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    cancelled_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Outsourcing
    is_outsourced BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    outsource_lab VARCHAR(255),
    outsource_sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    outsource_received_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Lab Results
CREATE TABLE lab_results (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES lab_order_items(id),
    order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES lab_orders(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    test_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_catalog(id),
    
    result_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- For panels, this might be a sub-test
    parameter_name VARCHAR(255),
    parameter_code VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Result
    result_value VARCHAR(500),
    result_numeric DECIMAL(15, 5),
    result_unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Reference Range (snapshot at time of result)
    reference_min DECIMAL(15, 5),
    reference_max DECIMAL(15, 5),
    reference_text VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Flags
    abnormal_flag VARCHAR(10), -- N, L, H, LL, HH, A
    is_critical BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    critical_notified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    critical_notified_to UUID,
    critical_notified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Delta Check
    previous_value DECIMAL(15, 5),
    previous_date DATE,
    delta_flag BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Comments
    result_comment TEXT,
    technical_comment TEXT,
    
    -- Verification
    entered_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    entered_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    status result_status_type DEFAULT 'preliminary',
    
    -- Machine Interface
    analyzer_name VARCHAR(100),
    analyzer_result_id VARCHAR(100),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Lab Result Amendments
CREATE TABLE lab_result_amendments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    result_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES lab_results(id),
    
    previous_value VARCHAR(500),
    new_value VARCHAR(500),
    
    amendment_reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    
    amended_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    amended_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- Microbiology Culture Results
CREATE TABLE microbiology_results (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES lab_order_items(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    specimen_type VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Gram Stain
    gram_stain_result TEXT,
    
    -- Culture
    culture_result VARCHAR(50), -- positive, negative, no_growth
    growth_description TEXT,
    incubation_hours INTEGER,
    
    -- Organisms
    organisms JSONB, -- [{name, colony_count, significance}]
    
    -- Sensitivity (Antibiogram)
    sensitivity_results JSONB, 
    -- [{antibiotic, mic, interpretation (S/I/R)}]
    
    -- Comments
    clinical_significance TEXT,
    recommendations TEXT,
    
    reported_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reported_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Histopathology Reports
CREATE TABLE histopathology_reports (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES lab_order_items(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- Specimen Info
    specimen_type VARCHAR(255),
    specimen_received_date DATE,
    specimen_site VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Gross Examination
    gross_description TEXT,
    
    -- Microscopic Examination
    microscopic_description TEXT,
    
    -- Diagnosis
    diagnosis TEXT,
    
    -- IHC (Immunohistochemistry)
    ihc_performed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    ihc_markers JSONB,
    
    -- Staging/Grading
    staging TEXT,
    grading TEXT,
    
    -- Margins
    margin_status VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Additional Studies
    special_stains JSONB,
    molecular_studies JSONB,
    
    -- Synoptic Report
    synoptic_report JSONB,
    
    -- Comments
    pathologist_comments TEXT,
    
    -- Sign-off
    reported_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reported_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Images
    microscopy_images TEXT[],
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- RADIOLOGY
-- ============================================

-- Radiology Modalities
CREATE TABLE radiology_modalities (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    modality_type VARCHAR(50), -- xray, ct, mri, usg, mammo, fluoroscopy, nuclear
    
    room_number VARCHAR(20),
    
    ae_title VARCHAR(50), -- DICOM AE Title
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, code)
);

-- Radiology Orders
CREATE TABLE radiology_orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    order_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    accession_number VARCHAR(50), -- DICOM
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    ordered_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    order_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- Test Info
    test_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_catalog(id),
    procedure_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    modality_type VARCHAR(50),
    body_part VARCHAR(100),
    laterality VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Clinical
    clinical_history TEXT,
    indication TEXT,
    
    priority priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    
    -- Scheduling
    scheduled_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    modality_id UUID REFERENCES radiology_modalities(id),
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'ordered',
    -- ordered, scheduled, patient_arrived, in_progress, completed, reported, cancelled
    
    -- Execution
    performed_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    performed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id), -- Technician
    
    -- Contrast
    contrast_used BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    contrast_type VARCHAR(100),
    contrast_volume VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- For special preps
    patient_prep TEXT,
    
    -- Billing
    billing_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, order_number)
);

-- Radiology Images (DICOM Study reference)
CREATE TABLE radiology_studies (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES radiology_orders(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- DICOM UIDs
    study_instance_uid VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE,
    study_date DATE,
    study_time TIME,
    
    modality VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Study Info
    study_description VARCHAR(255),
    number_of_series INTEGER,
    number_of_images INTEGER,
    
    -- Storage
    pacs_location TEXT,
    storage_size_mb DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Viewing
    dicom_viewer_url TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Radiology Reports
CREATE TABLE radiology_reports (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES radiology_orders(id),
    study_id UUID REFERENCES radiology_studies(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- Report Content
    findings TEXT,
    impression TEXT,
    recommendations TEXT,
    
    -- Structured Report (for some modalities)
    structured_report JSONB,
    
    -- Comparison
    comparison_study_id UUID REFERENCES radiology_studies(id),
    comparison_notes TEXT,
    
    -- Critical Finding
    is_critical BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    critical_finding TEXT,
    critical_notified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    critical_notified_to UUID,
    critical_notified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Sign-off
    reported_by UUID REFERENCES users(id), -- Radiologist
    reported_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    status result_status_type DEFAULT 'preliminary',
    
    -- Addendum
    addendum TEXT,
    addendum_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    addendum_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- PDF
    report_pdf_url TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- QC (Quality Control) Records
CREATE TABLE lab_qc_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    test_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_catalog(id),
    analyzer_name VARCHAR(100),
    
    qc_date DATE NOT NULL,
    qc_level VARCHAR(20), -- level1, level2, level3
    qc_material_lot VARCHAR(50),
    
    expected_mean DECIMAL(15, 5),
    expected_sd DECIMAL(15, 5),
    
    observed_value DECIMAL(15, 5),
    
    -- Westgard Rules
    qc_status VARCHAR(20), -- pass, fail, warning
    rules_violated TEXT[],
    
    corrective_action TEXT,
    
    performed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reviewed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_test_catalog_org ON test_catalog(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_test_catalog_dept ON test_catalog(department);
CREATE INDEX idx_test_catalog_code ON test_catalog(organization_id, code);

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_org ON lab_orders(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_patient ON lab_orders(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_date ON lab_orders(order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_status ON lab_orders(status);

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_patient ON lab_results(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_order ON lab_results(order_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_critical ON lab_results(is_critical) WHERE is_critical = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_patient ON radiology_orders(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_date ON radiology_orders(order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_status ON radiology_orders(status);
```

## 📁 PART 10: PHARMACY MANAGEMENT

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 16: PHARMACY MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Pharmacy Locations
CREATE TABLE pharmacy_locations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    location_type VARCHAR(50), -- main, satellite, ward, emergency, ot
    
    floor VARCHAR(20),
    building VARCHAR(50),
    
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    
    operating_hours TEXT,
    
    -- For ward pharmacies
    ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, code)
);

-- Medicine Inventory
CREATE TABLE medicine_inventory (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    pharmacy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy_locations(id),
    
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    
    -- Current Stock
    quantity_on_hand INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    quantity_reserved INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- Reserved for orders
    quantity_available INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (quantity_on_hand - quantity_reserved) STORED,
    
    -- Reorder Levels
    reorder_level INTEGER,
    reorder_quantity INTEGER,
    max_stock INTEGER,
    
    -- Unit
    stock_unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Cost
    last_purchase_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    average_cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    selling_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Location
    bin_location VARCHAR(50),
    shelf_number VARCHAR(20),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_stock_date TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(pharmacy_id, drug_id)
);

-- Medicine Batches
CREATE TABLE medicine_batches (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    inventory_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES medicine_inventory(id),
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    pharmacy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy_locations(id),
    
    batch_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    lot_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    manufacturing_date DATE,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Quantity
    received_quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
    current_quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
    
    -- Cost
    purchase_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    selling_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Supplier
    supplier_id UUID,
    grn_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_quarantine BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(pharmacy_id, drug_id, batch_number)
);

-- Medicine Stock Transactions
CREATE TABLE medicine_stock_transactions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    pharmacy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy_locations(id),
    
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    batch_id UUID REFERENCES medicine_batches(id),
    
    transaction_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    -- purchase, sale, return_supplier, return_patient, transfer_in, transfer_out,
    -- adjustment_in, adjustment_out, expired, damaged, consumption
    
    transaction_date TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
    unit_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    total_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Reference
    reference_type VARCHAR(50), -- purchase_order, prescription, return, transfer, adjustment
    reference_id UUID,
    reference_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Balance
    balance_before INTEGER,
    balance_after INTEGER,
    
    remarks TEXT,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Medicine Dispensing
CREATE TABLE medicine_dispensing (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    pharmacy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy_locations(id),
    
    dispensing_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    prescription_id UUID REFERENCES prescriptions(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    dispensing_type VARCHAR(50), -- opd, ipd, discharge, emergency, otc
    
    dispensing_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    dispensed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Billing
    total_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    discount_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    net_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    payment_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    bill_id UUID,
    
    -- Counseling
    counseling_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    counseling_notes TEXT,
    
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'completed', -- pending, partial, completed, cancelled
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, dispensing_number)
);

-- Medicine Dispensing Items
CREATE TABLE medicine_dispensing_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    dispensing_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES medicine_dispensing(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    batch_id UUID REFERENCES medicine_batches(id),
    
    prescription_item_id UUID REFERENCES prescription_items(id),
    
    drug_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    quantity_prescribed INTEGER,
    quantity_dispensed INTEGER NOT NULL,
    
    -- Substitution
    is_substituted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    original_drug_id UUID REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    substitution_reason TEXT,
    
    unit_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    discount_percent DECIMAL(5, 2),
    total_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Dosage Instructions
    dose VARCHAR(100),
    frequency VARCHAR(100),
    duration VARCHAR(50),
    instructions TEXT,
    
    -- Barcode Verification
    barcode_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Medicine Returns
CREATE TABLE medicine_returns (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    pharmacy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy_locations(id),
    
    return_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    return_type VARCHAR(50), -- patient_return, ward_return, supplier_return
    
    -- Source
    patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    dispensing_id UUID REFERENCES medicine_dispensing(id),
    ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id),
    supplier_id UUID,
    
    return_date TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    returned_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    reason TEXT,
    
    total_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- For patient returns - refund
    refund_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    refund_processed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'completed',
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, return_number)
);

-- Medicine Return Items
CREATE TABLE medicine_return_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    return_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES medicine_returns(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    batch_id UUID REFERENCES medicine_batches(id),
    
    quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
    unit_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    total_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    condition VARCHAR(50), -- good, damaged, expired
    restock BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Ward Stock (Floor Stock)
CREATE TABLE ward_stock (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES wards(id),
    
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    
    current_quantity INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    max_quantity INTEGER,
    reorder_level INTEGER,
    
    last_replenished_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_replenished_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(ward_id, drug_id)
);

-- Crash Cart Inventory
CREATE TABLE crash_cart_inventory (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    cart_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id),
    location VARCHAR(255),
    
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    
    required_quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
    current_quantity INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    last_checked_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_checked_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    expiry_date DATE, -- Earliest expiry in cart
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Narcotic Drug Register
CREATE TABLE narcotic_register (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    pharmacy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy_locations(id),
    
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    
    -- Register Entry
    register_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    entry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    transaction_type VARCHAR(50), -- receipt, issue, adjustment, wastage
    
    -- Quantities
    quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
    balance_before INTEGER,
    balance_after INTEGER,
    
    -- For Issues
    patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    patient_name VARCHAR(255),
    prescription_id UUID REFERENCES prescriptions(id),
    
    -- Witness
    witness_name VARCHAR(255),
    witness_id_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Signatures (digital reference)
    pharmacist_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    witness_signature_url TEXT,
    
    remarks TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, register_number)
);

-- Cold Chain Log
CREATE TABLE cold_chain_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    pharmacy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pharmacy_locations(id),
    
    refrigerator_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    refrigerator_name VARCHAR(100),
    
    recorded_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    temperature_celsius DECIMAL(4, 1) NOT NULL,
    humidity_percent DECIMAL(4, 1),
    
    is_within_range BOOLEAN,
    min_temp DECIMAL(4, 1),
    max_temp DECIMAL(4, 1),
    
    excursion_detected BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    excursion_action TEXT,
    
    recorded_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_medicine_inventory_pharmacy ON medicine_inventory(pharmacy_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_medicine_inventory_drug ON medicine_inventory(drug_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_medicine_inventory_low_stock ON medicine_inventory(pharmacy_id) 
    WHERE quantity_available <= reorder_level;

CREATE INDEX idx_medicine_batches_inventory ON medicine_batches(inventory_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_medicine_batches_expiry ON medicine_batches(expiry_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_medicine_batches_expiring ON medicine_batches(expiry_date) 
    WHERE expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '90 days';

CREATE INDEX idx_stock_transactions_pharmacy ON medicine_stock_transactions(pharmacy_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_transactions_drug ON medicine_stock_transactions(drug_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_transactions_date ON medicine_stock_transactions(transaction_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_patient ON medicine_dispensing(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_prescription ON medicine_dispensing(prescription_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_date ON medicine_dispensing(dispensing_datetime);
```

## 📁 PART 11: INVENTORY & SUPPLY CHAIN

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 17: INVENTORY & SUPPLY CHAIN
-- ============================================

-- Item Categories
CREATE TABLE item_categories (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    parent_category_id UUID REFERENCES item_categories(id
```

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 17: INVENTORY & SUPPLY CHAIN (Continued)
-- ============================================

-- Item Categories (continued)
CREATE TABLE item_categories (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    parent_category_id UUID REFERENCES item_categories(id),
    
    category_type VARCHAR(50), -- medical_supplies, surgical, linen, housekeeping, office, food, it, ppe
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, code)
);

-- Item Master
CREATE TABLE item_master (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    item_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    description TEXT,
    
    category_id UUID REFERENCES item_categories(id),
    sub_category VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Units
    base_unit VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- piece, box, pack, kg, liter
    purchase_unit VARCHAR(50),
    issue_unit VARCHAR(50),
    conversion_factor DECIMAL(10, 4) DEFAULT 1,
    
    -- Specifications
    brand VARCHAR(100),
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255),
    model VARCHAR(100),
    specifications JSONB,
    
    -- Tracking
    is_serialized BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_batch_tracked BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_expiry BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Pricing
    standard_cost DECIMAL(12, 2),
    last_purchase_price DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Stock Control
    reorder_level INTEGER,
    reorder_quantity INTEGER,
    min_stock INTEGER,
    max_stock INTEGER,
    lead_time_days INTEGER,
    
    -- Classification
    abc_class VARCHAR(1), -- A, B, C
    ved_class VARCHAR(1), -- V(ital), E(ssential), D(esirable)
    
    -- For medical items
    is_sterile BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_implant BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_consignment BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Images
    image_url TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, item_code)
);

-- Suppliers
CREATE TABLE suppliers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    supplier_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    supplier_type VARCHAR(50), -- manufacturer, distributor, dealer, importer
    
    -- Contact
    contact_person VARCHAR(255),
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    email VARCHAR(255),
    website VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Address
    address_line1 VARCHAR(255),
    address_line2 VARCHAR(255),
    city VARCHAR(100),
    district VARCHAR(100),
    postal_code VARCHAR(20),
    country VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Bangladesh',
    
    -- Legal/Tax
    trade_license VARCHAR(100),
    tin_number VARCHAR(50),
    vat_registration VARCHAR(50),
    drug_license VARCHAR(100), -- For pharma suppliers
    
    -- Banking
    bank_name VARCHAR(100),
    bank_account VARCHAR(50),
    bank_branch VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Terms
    payment_terms INTEGER DEFAULT 30, -- Days
    credit_limit DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Rating
    rating INTEGER CHECK (rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    
    -- Status
    is_approved BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_date DATE,
    
    is_blacklisted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    blacklist_reason TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, supplier_code)
);

-- Supplier Items (Items supplied by each supplier)
CREATE TABLE supplier_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    supplier_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    
    supplier_item_code VARCHAR(50),
    supplier_item_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    unit_price DECIMAL(12, 2),
    currency VARCHAR(3) DEFAULT 'BDT',
    
    lead_time_days INTEGER,
    min_order_quantity INTEGER,
    
    is_preferred BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    last_purchase_date DATE,
    last_purchase_price DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(supplier_id, item_id)
);

-- Stores/Warehouses
CREATE TABLE stores (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    store_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    store_type VARCHAR(50), -- central, sub_store, department_store, pharmacy
    
    location VARCHAR(255),
    floor VARCHAR(20),
    
    manager_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, store_code)
);

-- Store Inventory
CREATE TABLE store_inventory (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    store_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stores(id),
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    
    quantity_on_hand DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    quantity_reserved DECIMAL(12, 3) DEFAULT 0,
    quantity_available DECIMAL(12, 3) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (quantity_on_hand - quantity_reserved) STORED,
    
    -- Location within store
    bin_location VARCHAR(50),
    shelf_number VARCHAR(20),
    rack_number VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Valuation
    average_cost DECIMAL(12, 4),
    last_purchase_price DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Stock Levels
    reorder_level DECIMAL(12, 3),
    reorder_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    
    last_receipt_date TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_issue_date TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(store_id, item_id)
);

-- Item Batches
CREATE TABLE item_batches (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    store_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stores(id),
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    inventory_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES store_inventory(id),
    
    batch_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    lot_number VARCHAR(50),
    serial_number VARCHAR(100),
    
    manufacturing_date DATE,
    expiry_date DATE,
    
    received_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    current_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    
    unit_cost DECIMAL(12, 4),
    
    supplier_id UUID REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    grn_id UUID,
    
    -- For sterilized items
    sterilization_date DATE,
    sterilization_expiry DATE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(store_id, item_id, batch_number)
);

-- Purchase Requisitions
CREATE TABLE purchase_requisitions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    pr_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    requesting_department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    requesting_store_id UUID REFERENCES stores(id),
    
    requested_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    request_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    
    required_date DATE,
    
    priority VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal', -- low, normal, high, urgent
    
    purpose TEXT,
    
    -- Approval
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft, submitted, approved, rejected, converted, cancelled
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    approval_remarks TEXT,
    
    -- Conversion to PO
    converted_to_po BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    po_id UUID,
    
    total_estimated_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, pr_number)
);

-- Purchase Requisition Items
CREATE TABLE purchase_requisition_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    pr_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES purchase_requisitions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    
    requested_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    current_stock DECIMAL(12, 3),
    
    estimated_unit_price DECIMAL(12, 2),
    estimated_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    preferred_supplier_id UUID REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    
    specifications TEXT,
    
    -- Approval
    approved_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Request for Quotation
CREATE TABLE rfq (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    rfq_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    pr_id UUID REFERENCES purchase_requisitions(id),
    
    rfq_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    submission_deadline TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft, sent, closed, evaluated
    
    terms_and_conditions TEXT,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, rfq_number)
);

-- RFQ Suppliers
CREATE TABLE rfq_suppliers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    rfq_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES rfq(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    supplier_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    
    sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    sent_via VARCHAR(50), -- email, portal, manual
    
    response_received BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    response_date TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    total_quoted_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    is_selected BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    UNIQUE(rfq_id, supplier_id)
);

-- Supplier Quotations
CREATE TABLE supplier_quotations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    rfq_supplier_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES rfq_suppliers(id),
    rfq_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES rfq(id),
    supplier_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    
    quotation_number VARCHAR(50),
    quotation_date DATE,
    
    validity_date DATE,
    delivery_days INTEGER,
    payment_terms TEXT,
    
    total_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    terms_accepted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Quotation Items
CREATE TABLE quotation_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    quotation_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES supplier_quotations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    
    quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    unit_price DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    discount_percent DECIMAL(5, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_percent DECIMAL(5, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    total_price DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    brand_offered VARCHAR(100),
    delivery_days INTEGER,
    
    remarks TEXT
);

-- Purchase Orders
CREATE TABLE purchase_orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    po_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    supplier_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    
    pr_id UUID REFERENCES purchase_requisitions(id),
    quotation_id UUID REFERENCES supplier_quotations(id),
    
    po_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    expected_delivery_date DATE,
    
    delivery_address TEXT,
    delivery_store_id UUID REFERENCES stores(id),
    
    -- Amounts
    subtotal DECIMAL(14, 2),
    discount_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    shipping_amount DECIMAL(10, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    currency VARCHAR(3) DEFAULT 'BDT',
    
    payment_terms TEXT,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', 
    -- draft, pending_approval, approved, sent, partial_received, received, cancelled
    
    -- Approval
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Sending
    sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    sent_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    terms_and_conditions TEXT,
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, po_number)
);

-- Purchase Order Items
CREATE TABLE purchase_order_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    po_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES purchase_orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    
    ordered_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    unit_price DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    discount_percent DECIMAL(5, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_percent DECIMAL(5, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    line_total DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    received_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) DEFAULT 0,
    pending_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    
    expected_delivery_date DATE,
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, partial, received, cancelled
    
    remarks TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Goods Receipt Note (GRN)
CREATE TABLE goods_receipt_notes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    grn_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    po_id UUID REFERENCES purchase_orders(id),
    supplier_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    
    receiving_store_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stores(id),
    
    receipt_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    
    -- Supplier Documents
    supplier_invoice_number VARCHAR(50),
    supplier_invoice_date DATE,
    supplier_challan_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Inspection
    inspection_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    inspection_status VARCHAR(20), -- pending, passed, failed, partial
    inspected_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    inspected_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Amounts
    invoice_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    received_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    variance_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft, inspecting, approved, posted, rejected
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    posted_to_inventory BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    posted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, grn_number)
);

-- GRN Items
CREATE TABLE grn_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    grn_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES goods_receipt_notes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    po_item_id UUID REFERENCES purchase_order_items(id),
    
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    
    -- Quantities
    ordered_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    received_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    accepted_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    rejected_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) DEFAULT 0,
    
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Batch Info
    batch_number VARCHAR(50),
    manufacturing_date DATE,
    expiry_date DATE,
    
    -- Pricing
    unit_price DECIMAL(12, 2),
    line_total DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Inspection
    inspection_status VARCHAR(20),
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Storage
    bin_location VARCHAR(50),
    
    remarks TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Stock Issue (Department Indent)
CREATE TABLE stock_issues (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    issue_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    from_store_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stores(id),
    to_department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    to_ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id),
    to_cost_center VARCHAR(100),
    
    indent_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    requested_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    request_date DATE,
    
    issue_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    issued_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    received_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    total_value DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'issued', -- draft, approved, issued, received, cancelled
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, issue_number)
);

-- Stock Issue Items
CREATE TABLE stock_issue_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    issue_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_issues(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    batch_id UUID REFERENCES item_batches(id),
    
    requested_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    issued_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    unit_cost DECIMAL(12, 4),
    line_value DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Stock Transfers
CREATE TABLE stock_transfers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    transfer_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    from_store_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stores(id),
    to_store_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stores(id),
    
    transfer_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    
    requested_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Dispatch
    dispatched_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    dispatched_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Receipt
    received_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    total_value DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, approved, in_transit, received, cancelled
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, transfer_number)
);

-- Stock Transfer Items
CREATE TABLE stock_transfer_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    transfer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_transfers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    batch_id UUID REFERENCES item_batches(id),
    
    requested_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    dispatched_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    received_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    unit_cost DECIMAL(12, 4),
    
    variance_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    variance_reason TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Stock Adjustments
CREATE TABLE stock_adjustments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    adjustment_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    store_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stores(id),
    
    adjustment_type VARCHAR(50), -- physical_count, damage, expiry, theft, write_off, correction
    
    adjustment_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    
    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    reference_document VARCHAR(100),
    
    total_adjustment_value DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft, pending_approval, approved, posted, rejected
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, adjustment_number)
);

-- Stock Adjustment Items
CREATE TABLE stock_adjustment_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    adjustment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_adjustments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    batch_id UUID REFERENCES item_batches(id),
    
    system_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    physical_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    adjustment_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (physical_quantity - system_quantity) STORED,
    
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    unit_cost DECIMAL(12, 4),
    adjustment_value DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    reason TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Stock Transactions
CREATE TABLE inventory_transactions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    store_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stores(id),
    
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    batch_id UUID REFERENCES item_batches(id),
    
    transaction_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    -- grn_receipt, po_return, issue, issue_return, transfer_out, transfer_in,
    -- adjustment_in, adjustment_out, opening_balance
    
    transaction_date TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    quantity DECIMAL(12, 3) NOT NULL,
    unit_cost DECIMAL(12, 4),
    total_value DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    balance_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    balance_value DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    reference_type VARCHAR(50),
    reference_id UUID,
    reference_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    remarks TEXT,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Physical Inventory Count
CREATE TABLE physical_inventory_counts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    count_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    store_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES stores(id),
    
    count_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    count_type VARCHAR(50), -- full, cycle, spot
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'in_progress', -- in_progress, completed, approved, posted
    
    counted_by UUID[] ,
    supervised_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, count_number)
);

-- Physical Count Items
CREATE TABLE physical_count_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    count_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES physical_inventory_counts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES item_master(id),
    batch_id UUID REFERENCES item_batches(id),
    bin_location VARCHAR(50),
    
    system_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    counted_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    variance_quantity DECIMAL(12, 3),
    
    unit_cost DECIMAL(12, 4),
    variance_value DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    count_1 DECIMAL(12, 3),
    count_2 DECIMAL(12, 3),
    count_3 DECIMAL(12, 3),
    
    remarks TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Indexes for Inventory
CREATE INDEX idx_item_master_org ON item_master(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_item_master_category ON item_master(category_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_item_master_code ON item_master(organization_id, item_code);

CREATE INDEX idx_suppliers_org ON suppliers(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_suppliers_active ON suppliers(organization_id, is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_store_inventory_store ON store_inventory(store_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_store_inventory_item ON store_inventory(item_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_store_inventory_low ON store_inventory(store_id) WHERE quantity_available <= reorder_level;

CREATE INDEX idx_item_batches_store ON item_batches(store_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_item_batches_expiry ON item_batches(expiry_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_po_org ON purchase_orders(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_po_supplier ON purchase_orders(supplier_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_po_status ON purchase_orders(status);

CREATE INDEX idx_grn_org ON goods_receipt_notes(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_grn_po ON goods_receipt_notes(po_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_inv_trans_store ON inventory_transactions(store_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_inv_trans_item ON inventory_transactions(item_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_inv_trans_date ON inventory_transactions(transaction_date);
```
