## 📁 PART 12: INSURANCE & CLAIM MANAGEMENT

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 18: INSURANCE & CLAIM MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Insurance Companies
CREATE TABLE insurance_companies (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    company_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    short_name VARCHAR(50),
    
    company_type VARCHAR(50), -- insurance, tpa, corporate
    
    -- Contact
    contact_person VARCHAR(255),
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    email VARCHAR(255),
    website VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Address
    address TEXT,
    city VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Financial
    credit_limit DECIMAL(14, 2),
    current_outstanding DECIMAL(14, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Agreement
    agreement_start_date DATE,
    agreement_end_date DATE,
    discount_percent DECIMAL(5, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Processing
    claim_submission_email VARCHAR(255),
    claim_portal_url VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Settlement
    typical_settlement_days INTEGER,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, company_code)
);

-- Insurance Plans/Schemes
CREATE TABLE insurance_plans (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    insurance_company_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES insurance_companies(id),
    
    plan_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    plan_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    plan_type VARCHAR(50), -- individual, family, group, corporate
    
    -- Coverage
    coverage_type VARCHAR(50), -- cashless, reimbursement, both
    
    -- Limits
    sum_insured DECIMAL(14, 2),
    room_rent_limit DECIMAL(10, 2),
    room_rent_type VARCHAR(20), -- fixed, percent_of_sum
    icu_limit DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Co-pay
    copay_percent DECIMAL(5, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    copay_fixed DECIMAL(10, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Deductible
    deductible_amount DECIMAL(10, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Pre-existing
    pre_existing_waiting_years INTEGER DEFAULT 4,
    
    -- Coverage Details
    covered_services JSONB,
    exclusions JSONB,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(insurance_company_id, plan_code)
);

-- Insurance Tariff (Agreed Rates)
CREATE TABLE insurance_tariffs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    insurance_company_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES insurance_companies(id),
    
    service_type VARCHAR(50), -- room, procedure, investigation, package
    service_code VARCHAR(50),
    service_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Reference
    procedure_id UUID, -- Reference to procedures
    test_id UUID REFERENCES test_catalog(id),
    room_type VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Rates
    hospital_rate DECIMAL(10, 2),
    agreed_rate DECIMAL(10, 2),
    discount_percent DECIMAL(5, 2),
    
    effective_from DATE,
    effective_to DATE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Patient Insurance Policies
CREATE TABLE patient_insurance_policies (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    insurance_company_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES insurance_companies(id),
    plan_id UUID REFERENCES insurance_plans(id),
    
    policy_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    member_id VARCHAR(100),
    group_id VARCHAR(100),
    
    policy_holder_name VARCHAR(255),
    relationship_to_patient VARCHAR(50), -- self, spouse, child, parent
    
    -- Validity
    effective_date DATE NOT NULL,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Coverage
    sum_insured DECIMAL(14, 2),
    available_balance DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Corporate
    corporate_name VARCHAR(255),
    employee_id VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Verification
    is_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    verification_reference VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Documents
    policy_document_url TEXT,
    card_front_url TEXT,
    card_back_url TEXT,
    
    is_primary BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Pre-Authorization Requests
CREATE TABLE insurance_preauth (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    preauth_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    policy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_insurance_policies(id),
    insurance_company_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES insurance_companies(id),
    
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- Request Details
    request_type VARCHAR(50), -- planned_admission, emergency, extension, enhancement
    
    admission_type VARCHAR(50), -- planned, emergency
    expected_admission_date DATE,
    expected_discharge_date DATE,
    expected_los_days INTEGER,
    
    -- Clinical
    diagnosis TEXT,
    icd_codes VARCHAR(100)[],
    
    proposed_treatment TEXT,
    proposed_procedures TEXT[],
    
    treating_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    -- Financial
    estimated_cost DECIMAL(14, 2),
    requested_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Room
    room_type_requested VARCHAR(50),
    room_rate_per_day DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Documents
    documents JSONB, -- [{type, url, uploaded_at}]
    
    -- Status
    status claim_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    submitted_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- TPA Response
    tpa_reference_number VARCHAR(100),
    
    approved_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    approved_los_days INTEGER,
    approval_remarks TEXT,
    
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    
    responded_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Extension/Enhancement
    parent_preauth_id UUID REFERENCES insurance_preauth(id),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, preauth_number)
);

-- Pre-Auth Communication Log
CREATE TABLE preauth_communications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    preauth_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES insurance_preauth(id),
    
    communication_type VARCHAR(50), -- query, response, update, escalation
    direction VARCHAR(20), -- incoming, outgoing
    
    communication_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    subject TEXT,
    message TEXT,
    
    attachments JSONB,
    
    sent_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    received_from VARCHAR(255),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Insurance Claims
CREATE TABLE insurance_claims (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    claim_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    visit_id UUID,
    
    policy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_insurance_policies(id),
    insurance_company_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES insurance_companies(id),
    preauth_id UUID REFERENCES insurance_preauth(id),
    
    claim_type VARCHAR(50), -- cashless, reimbursement
    
    -- Clinical
    admission_date DATE,
    discharge_date DATE,
    los_days INTEGER,
    
    diagnosis TEXT,
    icd_codes VARCHAR(100)[],
    procedures_performed TEXT,
    
    -- Financial
    total_bill_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    non_payable_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    deduction_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    net_claim_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Pre-auth Amounts
    preauth_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Status
    status claim_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    
    -- Submission
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    submitted_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    submission_mode VARCHAR(50), -- online, physical, email
    
    -- TPA
    tpa_claim_number VARCHAR(100),
    tpa_received_date DATE,
    
    -- Settlement
    approved_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    disallowed_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    disallowance_reasons JSONB,
    
    settled_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    settlement_date DATE,
    settlement_reference VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Patient Responsibility
    patient_payable DECIMAL(12, 2),
    patient_paid DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Deductions
    tds_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Documents
    claim_documents JSONB,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, claim_number)
);

-- Claim Items (Bill Breakdown)
CREATE TABLE claim_items (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    claim_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES insurance_claims(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    
    item_category VARCHAR(50), -- room, professional_fees, investigation, medicine, procedure, consumables
    item_description VARCHAR(500),
    
    service_date DATE,
    quantity INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    
    billed_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Adjudication
    approved_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    disallowed_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    disallowance_code VARCHAR(20),
    disallowance_reason TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Claim Status History
CREATE TABLE claim_status_history (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    claim_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES insurance_claims(id),
    
    previous_status claim_status_type,
    new_status claim_status_type NOT NULL,
    
    changed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    changed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    remarks TEXT
);

-- Corporate Accounts
CREATE TABLE corporate_accounts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    company_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    company_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Contact
    contact_person VARCHAR(255),
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    email VARCHAR(255),
    
    address TEXT,
    
    -- Financial
    credit_limit DECIMAL(14, 2),
    current_outstanding DECIMAL(14, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Discount
    discount_percent DECIMAL(5, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Agreement
    agreement_start_date DATE,
    agreement_end_date DATE,
    
    -- Billing
    billing_cycle VARCHAR(20), -- weekly, bi_weekly, monthly
    payment_terms_days INTEGER DEFAULT 30,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, company_code)
);

-- Corporate Employees
CREATE TABLE corporate_employees (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    corporate_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES corporate_accounts(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    employee_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    employee_name VARCHAR(255),
    department VARCHAR(100),
    designation VARCHAR(100),
    
    coverage_limit DECIMAL(12, 2),
    used_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    valid_from DATE,
    valid_until DATE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(corporate_id, employee_id)
);

-- Corporate Invoices
CREATE TABLE corporate_invoices (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    invoice_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    corporate_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES corporate_accounts(id),
    
    invoice_date DATE NOT NULL,
    due_date DATE,
    
    period_from DATE,
    period_to DATE,
    
    -- Amounts
    subtotal DECIMAL(14, 2),
    discount_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    paid_amount DECIMAL(14, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    balance_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft, sent, partial_paid, paid, overdue, disputed
    
    sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, invoice_number)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_insurance_companies_org ON insurance_companies(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_policies_patient ON patient_insurance_policies(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_policies_company ON patient_insurance_policies(insurance_company_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_preauth_org ON insurance_preauth(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_preauth_patient ON insurance_preauth(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_preauth_status ON insurance_preauth(status);

CREATE INDEX idx_claims_org ON insurance_claims(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_claims_patient ON insurance_claims(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_claims_admission ON insurance_claims(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_claims_status ON insurance_claims(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_claims_company ON insurance_claims(insurance_company_id);
```

## 📁 PART 13: HOUSEKEEPING MANAGEMENT

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 19: HOUSEKEEPING MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Housekeeping Staff
CREATE TABLE housekeeping_staff (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    staff_code VARCHAR(20),
    
    designation VARCHAR(50), -- cleaner, supervisor, manager
    
    assigned_zone_id UUID,
    current_shift shift_type,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, staff_code)
);

-- Cleaning Zones
CREATE TABLE cleaning_zones (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    zone_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    zone_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    zone_type VARCHAR(50), -- ward, ot, icu, public_area, office, corridor
    
    -- Coverage
    buildings TEXT[],
    floors TEXT[],
    
    -- Risk Level
    risk_level VARCHAR(20), -- high, medium, low
    color_code VARCHAR(20), -- red, yellow, green
    
    -- Cleaning Frequency
    cleaning_frequency VARCHAR(50), -- hourly, twice_daily, daily, weekly
    
    supervisor_id UUID REFERENCES housekeeping_staff(id),
    
    special_instructions TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, zone_code)
);

-- Zone Area Mapping
CREATE TABLE zone_areas (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    zone_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cleaning_zones(id),
    
    area_type VARCHAR(50), -- ward, room, corridor, toilet, nurses_station, ot_room
    area_id UUID, -- Reference to ward/room/bed
    area_name VARCHAR(100),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- Cleaning Schedules
CREATE TABLE cleaning_schedules (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    schedule_type VARCHAR(50), -- routine, discharge, terminal, deep_cleaning
    
    zone_id UUID REFERENCES cleaning_zones(id),
    area_type VARCHAR(50),
    area_id UUID,
    bed_id UUID REFERENCES beds(id),
    
    -- Schedule
    scheduled_date DATE NOT NULL,
    scheduled_time TIME,
    
    frequency VARCHAR(50), -- once, daily, weekly, monthly
    
    -- Assignment
    assigned_to UUID REFERENCES housekeeping_staff(id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Trigger
    triggered_by VARCHAR(50), -- schedule, discharge, transfer, manual, request
    trigger_reference_id UUID,
    
    priority VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal',
    
    -- Checklist
    checklist_id UUID,
    
    -- Progress
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, assigned, in_progress, completed, verified
    
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_by UUID REFERENCES housekeeping_staff(id),
    
    -- QA
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    verification_status VARCHAR(20), -- passed, failed
    verification_remarks TEXT,
    
    -- Photos
    before_photos TEXT[],
    after_photos TEXT[],
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Cleaning Checklists
CREATE TABLE cleaning_checklists (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    checklist_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    cleaning_type VARCHAR(50), -- routine, discharge, terminal, ot, isolation
    area_type VARCHAR(50),
    
    items JSONB NOT NULL, -- [{item, is_mandatory, instructions}]
    
    estimated_duration_minutes INTEGER,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, checklist_code)
);

-- Cleaning Task Execution
CREATE TABLE cleaning_task_execution (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    schedule_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cleaning_schedules(id),
    checklist_id UUID REFERENCES cleaning_checklists(id),
    
    executed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES housekeeping_staff(id),
    
    checklist_responses JSONB, -- [{item, completed, time, remarks}]
    
    chemicals_used JSONB, -- [{chemical, quantity}]
    
    execution_time_minutes INTEGER,
    
    issues_found TEXT,
    action_taken TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Linen Management
CREATE TABLE linen_types (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    category VARCHAR(50), -- bed_sheet, pillow_cover, blanket, towel, curtain, patient_gown
    
    par_level_per_bed INTEGER,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, code)
);

-- Linen Inventory
CREATE TABLE linen_inventory (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    linen_type_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES linen_types(id),
    
    total_quantity INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    available_quantity INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    in_use_quantity INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    in_laundry_quantity INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    condemned_quantity INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    last_count_date DATE,
    
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, linen_type_id)
);

-- Linen Transactions
CREATE TABLE linen_transactions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    linen_type_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES linen_types(id),
    
    transaction_type VARCHAR(50), -- issue, return, send_laundry, receive_laundry, condemn, purchase
    
    quantity INTEGER NOT NULL,
    
    -- For issue/return
    ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id),
    bed_id UUID REFERENCES beds(id),
    
    transaction_date TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    transacted_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    remarks TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Disinfection Log
CREATE TABLE disinfection_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    disinfection_type VARCHAR(50), -- surface, fumigation, uv, fogging
    
    area_type VARCHAR(50),
    area_id UUID,
    area_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Schedule
    scheduled_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Execution
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    performed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Details
    chemicals_used JSONB,
    concentration VARCHAR(50),
    contact_time_minutes INTEGER,
    
    -- For fumigation
    room_sealed BOOLEAN,
    ventilation_time_minutes INTEGER,
    
    -- Verification
    verification_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    verification_method VARCHAR(50),
    verification_result VARCHAR(50),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Cleanliness Complaints
CREATE TABLE cleanliness_complaints (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    complaint_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    location VARCHAR(255),
    area_type VARCHAR(50),
    area_id UUID,
    
    complaint_type VARCHAR(50), -- dirty_floor, dirty_toilet, bad_smell, waste_not_cleared, linen_dirty
    
    description TEXT,
    
    reported_by VARCHAR(255),
    reported_by_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reported_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    priority VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal',
    
    -- Assignment
    assigned_to UUID REFERENCES housekeeping_staff(id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Resolution
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'open', -- open, assigned, in_progress, resolved, closed
    
    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    resolution_notes TEXT,
    
    response_time_minutes INTEGER,
    resolution_time_minutes INTEGER,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, complaint_number)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_cleaning_schedules_branch ON cleaning_schedules(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_cleaning_schedules_date ON cleaning_schedules(scheduled_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_cleaning_schedules_status ON cleaning_schedules(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_cleaning_schedules_assigned ON cleaning_schedules(assigned_to);

CREATE INDEX idx_linen_transactions_branch ON linen_transactions(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_linen_transactions_type ON linen_transactions(linen_type_id);
```

## 📁 PART 14: HR & PAYROLL

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 20: HR & PAYROLL
-- ============================================

-- Employee Master
CREATE TABLE employees (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    employee_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Personal Info
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name VARCHAR(100),
    full_name VARCHAR(255),
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    
    father_name VARCHAR(255),
    mother_name VARCHAR(255),
    spouse_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    gender gender_type,
    date_of_birth DATE,
    blood_group blood_group_type,
    marital_status marital_status_type,
    nationality VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'Bangladeshi',
    religion VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Contact
    personal_phone VARCHAR(20),
    personal_email VARCHAR(255),
    emergency_contact_name VARCHAR(255),
    emergency_contact_phone VARCHAR(20),
    emergency_contact_relation VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Identity
    nid VARCHAR(20),
    passport_no VARCHAR(50),
    passport_expiry DATE,
    driving_license VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Address
    present_address TEXT,
    permanent_address TEXT,
    
    -- Photo
    photo_url TEXT,
    
    -- Employment
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    designation VARCHAR(100),
    job_title VARCHAR(100),
    grade VARCHAR(20),
    
    reporting_manager_id UUID REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    employment_type employment_type DEFAULT 'permanent',
    
    joining_date DATE NOT NULL,
    confirmation_date DATE,
    probation_end_date DATE,
    resignation_date DATE,
    last_working_date DATE,
    
    -- Work Details
    work_location VARCHAR(100),
    shift_id UUID,
    
    -- Bank
    bank_name VARCHAR(100),
    bank_branch VARCHAR(100),
    bank_account_number VARCHAR(50),
    bank_routing_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Mobile Banking
    mobile_banking_provider VARCHAR(50), -- bkash, nagad, rocket
    mobile_banking_number VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Tax
    tin_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Salary
    basic_salary DECIMAL(12, 2),
    gross_salary DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- PF
    pf_enrolled BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    pf_number VARCHAR(50),
    pf_contribution_percent DECIMAL(5, 2),
    
    -- Status
    employment_status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active', -- active, on_notice, resigned, terminated, retired
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, employee_code)
);

-- Employee Qualifications
CREATE TABLE employee_qualifications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    employee_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    qualification_type VARCHAR(50), -- education, certification, license
    
    -- Education
    degree VARCHAR(100),
    field_of_study VARCHAR(100),
    institution VARCHAR(255),
    board_university VARCHAR(255),
    year_of_passing INTEGER,
    result VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Certification/License
    certificate_name VARCHAR(255),
    issuing_authority VARCHAR(255),
    certificate_number VARCHAR(100),
    issue_date DATE,
    expiry_date DATE,
    
    -- Document
    document_url TEXT,
    
    is_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Employee Documents
CREATE TABLE employee_documents (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    employee_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    document_type VARCHAR(50), -- nid, passport, photo, certificate, appointment_letter, contract, other
    document_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    file_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_size BIGINT,
    
    expiry_date DATE,
    
    uploaded_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    uploaded_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Shifts
CREATE TABLE shifts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    shift_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    shift_type shift_type,
    
    start_time TIME NOT NULL,
    end_time TIME NOT NULL,
    
    break_duration_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 60,
    
    -- Grace Period
    late_grace_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 15,
    early_leave_grace_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 15,
    
    -- Overtime
    ot_threshold_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 30,
    
    is_night_shift BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    applicable_days INTEGER[] DEFAULT ARRAY[0,1,2,3,4,5,6], -- 0=Sunday
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, shift_code)
);

-- Attendance
CREATE TABLE attendance (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    employee_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    attendance_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    shift_id UUID REFERENCES shifts(id),
    
    -- Punch Times
    punch_in_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    punch_out_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Source
    punch_in_source VARCHAR(50), -- biometric, rfid, manual, geo_fence, face_recognition
    punch_out_source VARCHAR(50),
    
    punch_in_device VARCHAR(100),
    punch_out_device VARCHAR(100),
    
    punch_in_location POINT, -- For geo-fence
    punch_out_location POINT,
    
    -- Calculated
    working_hours DECIMAL(4, 2),
    overtime_hours DECIMAL(4, 2),
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20), -- present, absent, half_day, leave, holiday, week_off, late, early_leave
    
    late_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    early_leave_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Regularization
    is_regularized BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    regularization_reason TEXT,
    regularized_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    remarks TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(employee_id, attendance_date)
);

-- Leave Types
CREATE TABLE leave_types (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    leave_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    leave_category VARCHAR(50), -- earned, casual, sick, maternity, paternity, unpaid, compensatory
    
    -- Annual Quota
    annual_quota INTEGER,
    
    -- Rules
    is_paid BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_carry_forward BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    max_carry_forward INTEGER,
    
    is_encashable BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    max_encashment INTEGER,
    
    min_days_per_application DECIMAL(3,1) DEFAULT 0.5,
    max_days_per_application INTEGER,
    
    requires_document BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE, -- Medical certificate etc.
    
    advance_days_required INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- Days before leave
    
    -- Applicability
    applicable_gender gender_type, -- NULL for all
    min_service_months INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, leave_code)
);

-- Leave Balance
CREATE TABLE leave_balances (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    employee_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    leave_type_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES leave_types(id),
    
    year INTEGER NOT NULL,
    
    opening_balance DECIMAL(5, 1) DEFAULT 0,
    accrued DECIMAL(5, 1) DEFAULT 0,
    taken DECIMAL(5, 1) DEFAULT 0,
    adjustment DECIMAL(5, 1) DEFAULT 0,
    carry_forward DECIMAL(5, 1) DEFAULT 0,
    encashed DECIMAL(5, 1) DEFAULT 0,
    
    available_balance DECIMAL(5, 1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        opening_balance + accrued + carry_forward + adjustment - taken - encashed
    ) STORED,
    
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(employee_id, leave_type_id, year)
);

-- Leave Applications
CREATE TABLE leave_applications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    application_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    employee_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    leave_type_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES leave_types(id),
    
    from_date DATE NOT NULL,
    to_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    number_of_days DECIMAL(4, 1) NOT NULL,
    
    is_half_day BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    half_day_type VARCHAR(20), -- first_half, second_half
    
    reason TEXT,
    
    -- Contact during leave
    contact_number VARCHAR(20),
    address_during_leave TEXT,
    
    -- Documents
    supporting_documents TEXT[],
    
    -- Substitute
    substitute_employee_id UUID REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    -- Status
    status leave_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    -- Approval
    applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- Level 1
    approver_1_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approver_1_status leave_status_type,
    approver_1_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    approver_1_remarks TEXT,
    
    -- Level 2
    approver_2_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approver_2_status leave_status_type,
    approver_2_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    approver_2_remarks TEXT,
    
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, application_number)
);

-- Salary Components
CREATE TABLE salary_components (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    component_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    component_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- earning, deduction
    
    calculation_type VARCHAR(20), -- fixed, percent_of_basic, percent_of_gross, formula
    calculation_value DECIMAL(12, 2),
    calculation_formula TEXT,
    
    is_taxable BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_statutory BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    applicable_to VARCHAR(20), -- all, specific_grades, specific_employees
    
    display_order INTEGER,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, component_code)
);

-- Employee Salary Structure
CREATE TABLE employee_salary_structures (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    employee_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    effective_from DATE NOT NULL,
    effective_to DATE,
    
    basic_salary DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Components
    components JSONB NOT NULL, -- [{component_id, amount}]
    
    gross_salary DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    ctc DECIMAL(12, 2), -- Cost to Company
    
    is_current BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Payroll Runs
CREATE TABLE payroll_runs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id), -- NULL for all branches
    
    payroll_month INTEGER NOT NULL,
    payroll_year INTEGER NOT NULL,
    
    pay_period_from DATE NOT NULL,
    pay_period_to DATE NOT NULL,
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft, processing, computed, approved, paid, cancelled
    
    -- Counts
    total_employees INTEGER,
    processed_employees INTEGER,
    
    -- Totals
    total_gross DECIMAL(14, 2),
    total_deductions DECIMAL(14, 2),
    total_net_pay DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Approval
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Payment
    payment_date DATE,
    bank_file_generated BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    bank_file_url TEXT,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, branch_id, payroll_year, payroll_month)
);

-- Payroll Details (Payslips)
CREATE TABLE payroll_details (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    payroll_run_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payroll_runs(id),
    employee_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    -- Attendance Summary
    working_days INTEGER,
    present_days DECIMAL(4, 1),
    leave_days DECIMAL(4, 1),
    absent_days DECIMAL(4, 1),
    holidays INTEGER,
    week_offs INTEGER,
    
    overtime_hours DECIMAL(6, 2),
    late_count INTEGER,
    
    -- Earnings
    basic_salary DECIMAL(12, 2),
    earnings JSONB, -- [{component_code, component_name, amount}]
    total_earnings DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Deductions
    deductions JSONB, -- [{component_code, component_name, amount}]
    total_deductions DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Net Pay
    gross_salary DECIMAL(12, 2),
    net_pay DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Tax
    tds_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- PF
    employee_pf DECIMAL(10, 2),
    employer_pf DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Loans/Advances
    loan_deduction DECIMAL(10, 2),
    advance_deduction DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Payment
    payment_mode VARCHAR(50), -- bank_transfer, cash, cheque, mobile_banking
    bank_account VARCHAR(50),
    payment_reference VARCHAR(100),
    payment_date DATE,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'computed', -- computed, approved, paid, hold
    
    hold_reason TEXT,
    
    payslip_pdf_url TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(payroll_run_id, employee_id)
);

-- Salary Advances
CREATE TABLE salary_advances (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    advance_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    employee_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    advance_type VARCHAR(50), -- salary_advance, travel_advance, other
    
    requested_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    approved_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    reason TEXT,
    
    -- Repayment
    repayment_months INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    monthly_deduction DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, approved, rejected, disbursed, recovered
    
    applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    disbursed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    disbursement_mode VARCHAR(50),
    
    recovered_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    balance_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, advance_number)
);

-- Employee Loans
CREATE TABLE employee_loans (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    loan_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    employee_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    loan_type VARCHAR(50), -- personal, housing, vehicle, education, medical
    
    loan_amount DECIMAL(14, 2) NOT NULL,
    interest_rate DECIMAL(5, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    
    tenure_months INTEGER NOT NULL,
    emi_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Disbursement
    disbursement_date DATE,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, approved, disbursed, active, closed
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Recovery
    total_recovered DECIMAL(14, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    outstanding_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    emis_paid INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    start_deduction_month DATE,
    
    guarantor_name VARCHAR(255),
    guarantor_employee_id UUID REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, loan_number)
);

-- Loan EMI Schedule
CREATE TABLE loan_emi_schedule (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    loan_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES employee_loans(id),
    
    emi_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
    due_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    principal_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    interest_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    emi_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, paid, overdue, waived
    
    paid_date DATE,
    paid_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    payroll_detail_id UUID REFERENCES payroll_details(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(loan_id, emi_number)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_employees_org ON employees(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_employees_branch ON employees(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_employees_department ON employees(department_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_employees_code ON employees(organization_id, employee_code);

CREATE INDEX idx_attendance_employee ON attendance(employee_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_attendance_date ON attendance(attendance_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_attendance_employee_date ON attendance(employee_id, attendance_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_leave_apps_employee ON leave_applications(employee_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_leave_apps_dates ON leave_applications(from_date, to_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_leave_apps_status ON leave_applications(status);

CREATE INDEX idx_payroll_details_employee ON payroll_details(employee_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_payroll_details_run ON payroll_details(payroll_run_id);
```

## 📁 PART 15: DOCTOR DUTY ROSTER & BUILDING/MAINTENANCE

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 21: DOCTOR DUTY ROSTER
-- ============================================

-- Doctor Duty Roster
CREATE TABLE doctor_duty_roster (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    duty_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    duty_type VARCHAR(50), -- opd, ipd_round, emergency, icu, on_call, ot
    
    shift shift_type,
    
    start_time TIME NOT NULL,
    end_time TIME NOT NULL,
    
    -- Ward/Location Assignment
    ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    -- OPD Room
    opd_room VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- On-Call
    is_on_call BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    on_call_priority INTEGER,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'scheduled', -- scheduled, confirmed, completed, cancelled, swapped
    
    -- Swap
    swapped_with_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    swap_reason TEXT,
    swap_approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(doctor_id, duty_date, duty_type, shift)
);

-- Doctor On-Call Schedule
CREATE TABLE doctor_oncall_schedule (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    specialization VARCHAR(100),
    
    oncall_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Time period
    start_time TIME NOT NULL,
    end_time TIME NOT NULL,
    
    primary_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    backup_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, department_id, oncall_date, start_time)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 22: BUILDING MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Buildings
CREATE TABLE buildings (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    building_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    address TEXT,
    
    -- Specifications
    total_floors INTEGER,
    total_area_sqft DECIMAL(12, 2),
    construction_year INTEGER,
    
    -- Utilities
    has_generator BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_central_ac BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_lift BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    number_of_lifts INTEGER,
    
    floor_plan_url TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, building_code)
);

-- Floors
CREATE TABLE floors (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    building_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES buildings(id),
    
    floor_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    floor_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100),
    
    area_sqft DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    floor_plan_url TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(building_id, floor_code)
);

-- Room Registry (Non-clinical rooms)
CREATE TABLE facility_rooms (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    building_id UUID REFERENCES buildings(id),
    floor_id UUID REFERENCES floors(id),
    
    room_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100),
    
    room_type VARCHAR(50), -- office, conference, store, server_room, canteen, rest_room, utility
    
    area_sqft DECIMAL(8, 2),
    capacity INTEGER,
    
    assigned_department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    -- Amenities
    has_ac BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_projector BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_phone BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    is_bookable BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE, -- For conference rooms
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, room_code)
);

-- Utility Meters
CREATE TABLE utility_meters (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    building_id UUID REFERENCES buildings(id),
    
    meter_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    meter_type VARCHAR(50), -- electricity, water, gas
    
    meter_number VARCHAR(100),
    account_number VARCHAR(100),
    
    location VARCHAR(255),
    
    is_main_meter BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    parent_meter_id UUID REFERENCES utility_meters(id),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(branch_id, meter_code)
);

-- Utility Readings
CREATE TABLE utility_readings (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    meter_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES utility_meters(id),
    
    reading_date DATE NOT NULL,
    reading_value DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    
    previous_reading DECIMAL(12, 2),
    consumption DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    billing_period_from DATE,
    billing_period_to DATE,
    
    recorded_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    photo_url TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(meter_id, reading_date)
);

-- Utility Bills
CREATE TABLE utility_bills (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    meter_id UUID REFERENCES utility_meters(id),
    
    bill_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    bill_type VARCHAR(50), -- electricity, water, gas, internet, phone
    
    billing_period_from DATE,
    billing_period_to DATE,
    
    units_consumed DECIMAL(12, 2),
    rate_per_unit DECIMAL(8, 4),
    
    bill_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    tax_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    total_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    due_date DATE,
    
    -- Payment
    paid_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    payment_date DATE,
    payment_reference VARCHAR(100),
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, paid, overdue
    
    bill_document_url TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, bill_number)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 23: MAINTENANCE MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Equipment Registry
CREATE TABLE equipment_registry (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    equipment_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    category VARCHAR(50), -- medical_equipment, non_medical, it_equipment, hvac, electrical
    sub_category VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Details
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255),
    model VARCHAR(100),
    serial_number VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Purchase
    purchase_date DATE,
    purchase_price DECIMAL(14, 2),
    supplier_id UUID REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    po_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Warranty
    warranty_start_date DATE,
    warranty_end_date DATE,
    warranty_terms TEXT,
    
    -- AMC
    amc_vendor_id UUID REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    amc_start_date DATE,
    amc_end_date DATE,
    amc_cost DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Location
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    building_id UUID REFERENCES buildings(id),
    floor_id UUID REFERENCES floors(id),
    room_id UUID,
    location_description VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Technical
    power_requirement VARCHAR(100),
    installation_requirements TEXT,
    
    -- Calibration (for medical equipment)
    requires_calibration BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    calibration_frequency_months INTEGER,
    last_calibration_date DATE,
    next_calibration_due DATE,
    
    -- Maintenance
    pm_frequency_months INTEGER, -- Preventive maintenance frequency
    last_pm_date DATE,
    next_pm_due DATE,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active', -- active, under_maintenance, under_repair, condemned, disposed
    
    -- Asset
    asset_tag VARCHAR(50),
    depreciation_rate DECIMAL(5, 2),
    current_value DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Documents
    manual_url TEXT,
    
    is_critical BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, equipment_code)
);

-- Preventive Maintenance Schedule
CREATE TABLE pm_schedules (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    equipment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES equipment_registry(id),
    
    schedule_type VARCHAR(50), -- daily, weekly, monthly, quarterly, semi_annual, annual
    
    checklist JSONB, -- [{item, instructions}]
    
    last_performed_date DATE,
    next_due_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    assigned_technician_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- PM Completion Records
CREATE TABLE pm_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    schedule_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pm_schedules(id),
    equipment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES equipment_registry(id),
    
    pm_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    checklist_responses JSONB,
    
    findings TEXT,
    actions_taken TEXT,
    
    -- Parts Used
    parts_used JSONB,
    parts_cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    labor_hours DECIMAL(4, 2),
    labor_cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    total_cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    equipment_status_after VARCHAR(50),
    
    photos TEXT[],
    
    next_pm_due DATE,
    
    reviewed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Calibration Records
CREATE TABLE calibration_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    equipment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES equipment_registry(id),
    
    calibration_date DATE NOT NULL,
    calibration_due_date DATE,
    
    calibration_type VARCHAR(50), -- internal, external
    
    calibration_agency VARCHAR(255),
    technician_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Results
    parameters_checked JSONB,
    
    result VARCHAR(20), -- pass, fail, conditional_pass
    
    deviations_found TEXT,
    corrective_actions TEXT,
    
    certificate_number VARCHAR(100),
    certificate_url TEXT,
    
    next_calibration_date DATE,
    
    cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Maintenance Tickets (Breakdown)
CREATE TABLE maintenance_tickets (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    ticket_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Type
    ticket_type VARCHAR(50), -- breakdown, corrective, request
    category VARCHAR(50), -- equipment, civil, electrical, plumbing, hvac, it, other
    
    -- Equipment (if applicable)
    equipment_id UUID REFERENCES equipment_registry(id),
    
    -- Location
    building_id UUID REFERENCES buildings(id),
    floor_id UUID REFERENCES floors(id),
    location_description VARCHAR(255),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    -- Problem
    problem_title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    problem_description TEXT,
    
    priority maintenance_priority_type DEFAULT 'normal',
    
    -- Reported
    reported_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reported_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- Impact
    patient_impact BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    safety_impact BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Assignment
    assigned_to UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    status ticket_status_type DEFAULT 'open',
    
    -- Work
    acknowledged_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    work_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    work_completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Resolution
    root_cause TEXT,
    resolution TEXT,
    resolution_type VARCHAR(50), -- repaired, replaced, temporary_fix, external_service
    
    -- Parts
    spare_parts_used JSONB,
    parts_cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Labor
    labor_hours DECIMAL(5, 2),
    labor_cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    external_vendor_id UUID REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    external_cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    total_cost DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Equipment Status
    equipment_status_after VARCHAR(50),
    equipment_downtime_hours DECIMAL(6, 2),
    
    -- Closure
    closed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    closed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Feedback
    user_feedback TEXT,
    user_rating INTEGER CHECK (user_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    
    photos TEXT[],
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, ticket_number)
);

-- Ticket Work Log
CREATE TABLE ticket_work_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    ticket_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES maintenance_tickets(id),
    
    log_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    logged_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    work_type VARCHAR(50), -- diagnosis, repair, parts_request, waiting, completed
    
    description TEXT,
    
    hours_spent DECIMAL(4, 2),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Spare Parts Inventory
CREATE TABLE spare_parts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    part_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    category VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Compatibility
    compatible_equipment_ids UUID[],
    
    -- Stock
    quantity_on_hand INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    reorder_level INTEGER,
    
    unit_cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    supplier_id UUID REFERENCES suppliers(id),
    
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    
    is_critical BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, part_code)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_equipment_org ON equipment_registry(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_equipment_branch ON equipment_registry(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_equipment_category ON equipment_registry(category);
CREATE INDEX idx_equipment_status ON equipment_registry(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_equipment_pm_due ON equipment_registry(next_pm_due);
CREATE INDEX idx_equipment_calibration_due ON equipment_registry(next_calibration_due);

CREATE INDEX idx_maint_tickets_org ON maintenance_tickets(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_maint_tickets_status ON maintenance_tickets(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_maint_tickets_priority ON maintenance_tickets(priority);
CREATE INDEX idx_maint_tickets_assigned ON maintenance_tickets(assigned_to);
CREATE INDEX idx_maint_tickets_equipment ON maintenance_tickets(equipment_id);
```

## 📁 PART 16: DIET, TELEMEDICINE, COMPLAINTS & CONSENT

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 24: DIET MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Diet Types
CREATE TABLE diet_types (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    diet_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(100),
    
    description TEXT,
    
    diet_category VARCHAR(50), -- regular, therapeutic, special
    
    -- Nutritional Targets
    calories_min INTEGER,
    calories_max INTEGER,
    protein_grams INTEGER,
    carbs_grams INTEGER,
    fat_grams INTEGER,
    sodium_mg INTEGER,
    potassium_mg INTEGER,
    
    -- Restrictions
    allowed_foods TEXT,
    restricted_foods TEXT,
    
    special_instructions TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, diet_code)
);

-- Patient Diet Orders
CREATE TABLE patient_diet_orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    diet_type_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES diet_types(id),
    
    order_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    ordered_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Custom modifications
    modifications TEXT,
    allergies_noted TEXT,
    
    -- Texture
    texture VARCHAR(50), -- regular, soft, pureed, liquid
    
    -- Fluid restriction
    fluid_restriction_ml INTEGER,
    
    -- Timing
    effective_from TIMESTAMPTZ,
    effective_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- NPO (Nothing by mouth)
    is_npo BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    npo_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Tube feeding
    is_tube_feeding BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    tube_type VARCHAR(50), -- ng, peg, nj
    formula VARCHAR(100),
    rate_ml_per_hour INTEGER,
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active', -- active, discontinued, modified
    
    discontinued_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    discontinued_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    discontinuation_reason TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Meal Plans
CREATE TABLE meal_plans (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    diet_type_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES diet_types(id),
    
    day_of_week INTEGER, -- 0-6, NULL for everyday
    
    meal_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- breakfast, lunch, dinner, snack
    meal_time TIME,
    
    menu_items JSONB, -- [{item_name, quantity, calories, notes}]
    
    total_calories INTEGER,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Patient Meal Delivery
CREATE TABLE meal_deliveries (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES admissions(id),
    diet_order_id UUID REFERENCES patient_diet_orders(id),
    
    meal_date DATE NOT NULL,
    meal_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    
    ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id),
    bed_id UUID REFERENCES beds(id),
    
    -- Menu
    menu_items JSONB,
    special_requests TEXT,
    
    -- Delivery
    prepared_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    prepared_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    delivered_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    delivered_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Consumption
    consumption_status VARCHAR(50), -- full, partial, refused, npo
    percentage_consumed INTEGER,
    
    -- Feedback
    patient_feedback TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Nutritional Assessment
CREATE TABLE nutritional_assessments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    assessment_date DATE NOT NULL,
    assessed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id), -- Dietitian
    
    -- Anthropometry
    height_cm DECIMAL(5, 2),
    weight_kg DECIMAL(5, 2),
    bmi DECIMAL(4, 1),
    ideal_body_weight DECIMAL(5, 2),
    
    waist_circumference_cm DECIMAL(5, 1),
    mid_arm_circumference_cm DECIMAL(4, 1),
    
    -- Screening Score
    screening_tool VARCHAR(50), -- MST, NRS-2002, MUST
    screening_score INTEGER,
    nutrition_risk_level VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Dietary History
    dietary_history TEXT,
    recent_weight_change TEXT,
    appetite VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Lab Values
    albumin DECIMAL(4, 2),
    prealbumin DECIMAL(4, 2),
    hemoglobin DECIMAL(4, 1),
    
    -- Assessment
    nutrition_diagnosis TEXT,
    
    -- Recommendations
    calorie_requirement INTEGER,
    protein_requirement INTEGER,
    fluid_requirement INTEGER,
    
    recommendations TEXT,
    
    -- Follow-up
    followup_date DATE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 25: TELEMEDICINE
-- ============================================

-- Telemedicine Sessions
CREATE TABLE telemedicine_sessions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    session_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    appointment_id UUID REFERENCES appointments(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    -- Schedule
    scheduled_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    scheduled_duration_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 15,
    
    -- Video Platform
    platform VARCHAR(50), -- jitsi, zoom, custom
    meeting_url TEXT,
    meeting_id VARCHAR(100),
    meeting_password VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Consent
    patient_consent_obtained BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    consent_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'scheduled', 
    -- scheduled, patient_waiting, in_progress, completed, cancelled, no_show, technical_issue
    
    -- Actual Times
    patient_joined_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    doctor_joined_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    session_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    session_ended_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    actual_duration_minutes INTEGER,
    
    -- Technical
    patient_device_type VARCHAR(50),
    connection_quality VARCHAR(20), -- excellent, good, fair, poor
    technical_issues TEXT,
    
    -- Clinical
    visit_id UUID, -- Reference to OPD visit created
    prescription_id UUID REFERENCES prescriptions(id),
    
    -- Recording
    is_recorded BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    recording_url TEXT,
    recording_consent BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Billing
    consultation_fee DECIMAL(10, 2),
    payment_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    -- Feedback
    patient_rating INTEGER CHECK (patient_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    patient_feedback TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, session_number)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 26: COMPLAINT & FEEDBACK
-- ============================================

-- Complaint Categories
CREATE TABLE complaint_categories (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    parent_category_id UUID REFERENCES complaint_categories(id),
    
    sla_hours INTEGER, -- Response SLA
    resolution_sla_hours INTEGER,
    
    escalation_level_1_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    escalation_level_2_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, code)
);

-- Complaints
CREATE TABLE complaints (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    complaint_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Complainant
    complainant_type VARCHAR(50), -- patient, attendant, staff, visitor, other
    patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    complainant_name VARCHAR(255),
    complainant_phone VARCHAR(20),
    complainant_email VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Context
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- Complaint Details
    category_id UUID REFERENCES complaint_categories(id),
    sub_category VARCHAR(100),
    
    subject VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT NOT NULL,
    
    -- Against
    against_department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    against_staff_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Source
    received_via VARCHAR(50), -- walk_in, phone, email, app, website, social_media
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    received_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    priority VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal', -- low, normal, high, critical
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'open', 
    -- open, acknowledged, investigating, pending_response, resolved, closed, reopened
    
    -- Acknowledgment
    acknowledged_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    acknowledgment_sent BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Assignment
    assigned_to UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    assigned_department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Investigation
    investigation_notes TEXT,
    root_cause TEXT,
    
    -- Resolution
    resolution TEXT,
    corrective_action TEXT,
    preventive_action TEXT,
    
    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Closure
    closed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    closed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    closure_remarks TEXT,
    
    -- Verification
    complainant_satisfied BOOLEAN,
    satisfaction_rating INTEGER CHECK (satisfaction_rating BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    
    -- SLA
    response_sla_breached BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    resolution_sla_breached BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Escalation
    escalation_level INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    escalated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    escalated_to UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    attachments TEXT[],
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, complaint_number)
);

-- Complaint Communications
CREATE TABLE complaint_communications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    complaint_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES complaints(id),
    
    communication_type VARCHAR(50), -- note, email, call, sms, meeting
    direction VARCHAR(20), -- internal, to_complainant, from_complainant
    
    communication_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    subject TEXT,
    content TEXT,
    
    communicated_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    attachments TEXT[],
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Feedback Forms
CREATE TABLE feedback_forms (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    form_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    form_type VARCHAR(50), -- discharge, opd, service_specific
    
    questions JSONB NOT NULL, -- [{question, type, options, is_required}]
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, form_code)
);

-- Feedback Responses
CREATE TABLE feedback_responses (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    form_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES feedback_forms(id),
    
    patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    respondent_type VARCHAR(50), -- patient, attendant
    respondent_name VARCHAR(255),
    respondent_phone VARCHAR(20),
    
    responses JSONB NOT NULL,
    
    -- Calculated Scores
    overall_score DECIMAL(4, 2),
    nps_score INTEGER,
    
    -- Collection
    collected_via VARCHAR(50), -- kiosk, app, sms, email, call, paper
    collected_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    collected_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    comments TEXT,
    
    -- Follow-up
    requires_followup BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    followup_notes TEXT,
    followed_up_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    followed_up_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 27: CONSENT FORM MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Consent Templates
CREATE TABLE consent_templates (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    template_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    
    consent_type VARCHAR(50), 
    -- general_treatment, surgery, anesthesia, blood_transfusion, procedure,
    -- telemedicine, research, photography, data_sharing, dnar
    
    version VARCHAR(20),
    version_date DATE,
    
    -- Content
    content_english TEXT NOT NULL,
    content_bangla TEXT,
    
    -- Structured content
    sections JSONB, -- [{title, content, requires_initial}]
    
    -- Requirements
    requires_witness BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    requires_guardian BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE, -- For minors
    requires_interpreter BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Legal Review
    legal_reviewed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    legal_reviewed_by VARCHAR(255),
    legal_reviewed_date DATE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, template_code, version)
);

-- Patient Consents
CREATE TABLE patient_consents (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    consent_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    template_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES consent_templates(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- Context
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    surgery_id UUID REFERENCES surgeries(id),
    procedure_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Consent Process
    explained_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id), -- Doctor who explained
    explanation_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    language_used VARCHAR(50),
    interpreter_used BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    interpreter_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Patient Understanding
    questions_asked TEXT,
    questions_answered BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Decision
    consent_status consent_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    consent_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Signatures
    patient_signature_url TEXT,
    patient_signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- For minors/incapacitated
    guardian_name VARCHAR(255),
    guardian_relationship VARCHAR(50),
    guardian_signature_url TEXT,
    guardian_signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    guardian_id_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Witness
    witness_name VARCHAR(255),
    witness_signature_url TEXT,
    witness_signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- If refused
    refusal_reason TEXT,
    refusal_acknowledged BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- PDF
    consent_pdf_url TEXT,
    
    -- Withdrawal
    is_withdrawn BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    withdrawn_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    withdrawn_by VARCHAR(255),
    withdrawal_reason TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, consent_number)
);

-- Consent Access Log
CREATE TABLE consent_access_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    consent_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_consents(id),
    
    accessed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    accessed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    access_type VARCHAR(50), -- view, print, download
    
    ip_address INET,
    user_agent TEXT
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_diet_orders_patient ON patient_diet_orders(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_diet_orders_admission ON patient_diet_orders(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_diet_orders_active ON patient_diet_orders(admission_id, status) WHERE status = 'active';

CREATE INDEX idx_meal_deliveries_date ON meal_deliveries(meal_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_deliveries_ward ON meal_deliveries(ward_id, meal_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_telemedicine_patient ON telemedicine_sessions(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_telemedicine_doctor ON telemedicine_sessions(doctor_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_telemedicine_datetime ON telemedicine_sessions(scheduled_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_complaints_org ON complaints(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_complaints_status ON complaints(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_complaints_patient ON complaints(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_complaints_assigned ON complaints(assigned_to);

CREATE INDEX idx_consents_patient ON patient_consents(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_consents_admission ON patient_consents(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_consents_surgery ON patient_consents(surgery_id);
```

```sql
-- Service Master
```
