## 📁 PART 18: FINAL - FUNCTIONS, TRIGGERS & ROW LEVEL SECURITY

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 31: HELPER FUNCTIONS
-- ============================================

-- Function to generate sequential numbers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION generate_sequential_number(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_prefix VARCHAR,
    p_sequence_name VARCHAR
) RETURNS VARCHAR AS $$
DECLARE
    v_sequence_table VARCHAR := 'sequence_numbers';
    v_current_number INTEGER;
    v_result VARCHAR;
BEGIN
    -- Create sequence table if not exists
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sequence_numbers (
        organization_id UUID,
        sequence_name VARCHAR(100),
        prefix VARCHAR(20),
        current_number INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        last_reset_date DATE,
        PRIMARY KEY (organization_id, sequence_name)
    );
    
    -- Get and increment
    INSERT INTO sequence_numbers (organization_id, sequence_name, prefix, current_number, last_reset_date)
    VALUES (p_organization_id, p_sequence_name, p_prefix, 1, CURRENT_DATE)
    ON CONFLICT (organization_id, sequence_name)
    DO UPDATE SET current_number = sequence_numbers.current_number + 1
    RETURNING current_number INTO v_current_number;
    
    -- Format: PREFIX-YYYYMMDD-NNNNN
    v_result := p_prefix || '-' || TO_CHAR(CURRENT_DATE, 'YYYYMMDD') || '-' || LPAD(v_current_number::TEXT, 5, '0');
    
    RETURN v_result;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Function to calculate age
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION calculate_age(birth_date DATE)
RETURNS TEXT AS $$
DECLARE
    years INTEGER;
    months INTEGER;
    days INTEGER;
    age_interval INTERVAL;
BEGIN
    IF birth_date IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NULL;
    END IF;
    
    age_interval := age(CURRENT_DATE, birth_date);
    years := EXTRACT(YEAR FROM age_interval);
    months := EXTRACT(MONTH FROM age_interval);
    days := EXTRACT(DAY FROM age_interval);
    
    IF years > 0 THEN
        RETURN years || ' years';
    ELSIF months > 0 THEN
        RETURN months || ' months';
    ELSE
        RETURN days || ' days';
    END IF;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE;

-- Function to check drug interactions
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_drug_interactions(
    p_patient_id UUID,
    p_new_drug_id UUID
) RETURNS TABLE(
    interacting_drug_name VARCHAR,
    severity VARCHAR,
    description TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        dm.generic_name,
        di.severity,
        di.description
    FROM patient_medications pm
    JOIN drug_interactions di ON (
        (di.drug_id_1 = pm.drug_id AND di.drug_id_2 = p_new_drug_id)
        OR (di.drug_id_2 = pm.drug_id AND di.drug_id_1 = p_new_drug_id)
    )
    JOIN drug_master dm ON dm.id = pm.drug_id
    WHERE pm.patient_id = p_patient_id
    AND pm.is_current = TRUE
    AND di.is_active = TRUE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Function to check patient allergies against drug
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_drug_allergy(
    p_patient_id UUID,
    p_drug_id UUID
) RETURNS TABLE(
    allergen_name VARCHAR,
    severity VARCHAR,
    reaction TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        pa.allergen_name,
        pa.severity,
        pa.reaction
    FROM patient_allergies pa
    WHERE pa.patient_id = p_patient_id
    AND pa.is_active = TRUE
    AND (
        pa.allergen_name ILIKE '%' || (SELECT generic_name FROM drug_master WHERE id = p_drug_id) || '%'
        OR pa.allergen_id IN (
            SELECT allergen_id FROM drug_allergen_mapping WHERE drug_id = p_drug_id
        )
    );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 32: TRIGGERS
-- ============================================

-- Trigger function for updated_at timestamp
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Apply updated_at trigger to relevant tables
DO $$
DECLARE
    t TEXT;
BEGIN
    FOR t IN 
        SELECT table_name 
        FROM information_schema.columns 
        WHERE column_name = 'updated_at' 
        AND table_schema = 'public'
    LOOP
        EXECUTE format('
            DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS update_%s_updated_at ON %s;
            CREATE TRIGGER update_%s_updated_at
            BEFORE UPDATE ON %s
            FOR EACH ROW
            EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column();
        ', t, t, t, t);
    END LOOP;
END;
$$;

-- Trigger for audit logging
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION audit_trigger_function()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_old_data JSONB;
    v_new_data JSONB;
    v_changed_fields TEXT[];
BEGIN
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' THEN
        v_old_data := to_jsonb(OLD);
        v_new_data := to_jsonb(NEW);
        
        -- Get changed fields
        SELECT array_agg(key) INTO v_changed_fields
        FROM jsonb_each(v_new_data)
        WHERE v_old_data -> key IS DISTINCT FROM v_new_data -> key
        AND key NOT IN ('updated_at', 'created_at');
        
        IF v_changed_fields IS NOT NULL THEN
            INSERT INTO audit_logs (
                organization_id,
                user_id,
                action,
                table_name,
                record_id,
                old_values,
                new_values,
                changed_fields
            ) VALUES (
                COALESCE(NEW.organization_id, current_setting('app.organization_id', TRUE)::UUID),
                current_setting('app.user_id', TRUE)::UUID,
                'UPDATE',
                TG_TABLE_NAME,
                NEW.id,
                v_old_data,
                v_new_data,
                v_changed_fields
            );
        END IF;
        RETURN NEW;
        
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
        v_old_data := to_jsonb(OLD);
        
        INSERT INTO audit_logs (
            organization_id,
            user_id,
            action,
            table_name,
            record_id,
            old_values
        ) VALUES (
            COALESCE(OLD.organization_id, current_setting('app.organization_id', TRUE)::UUID),
            current_setting('app.user_id', TRUE)::UUID,
            'DELETE',
            TG_TABLE_NAME,
            OLD.id,
            v_old_data
        );
        RETURN OLD;
        
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        v_new_data := to_jsonb(NEW);
        
        INSERT INTO audit_logs (
            organization_id,
            user_id,
            action,
            table_name,
            record_id,
            new_values
        ) VALUES (
            COALESCE(NEW.organization_id, current_setting('app.organization_id', TRUE)::UUID),
            current_setting('app.user_id', TRUE)::UUID,
            'INSERT',
            TG_TABLE_NAME,
            NEW.id,
            v_new_data
        );
        RETURN NEW;
    END IF;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Trigger to update bed status on admission
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_bed_on_allocation()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        UPDATE beds 
        SET status = 'occupied',
            current_patient_id = (SELECT patient_id FROM admissions WHERE id = NEW.admission_id),
            current_admission_id = NEW.admission_id,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE id = NEW.bed_id;
        
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.release_datetime IS NOT NULL AND OLD.release_datetime IS NULL THEN
        UPDATE beds 
        SET status = 'cleaning',
            current_patient_id = NULL,
            current_admission_id = NULL,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE id = OLD.bed_id;
        
        -- Create cleaning task
        INSERT INTO bed_cleaning_tasks (
            organization_id, branch_id, bed_id, ward_id,
            cleaning_type, triggered_by, previous_patient_id
        )
        SELECT 
            b.organization_id, b.branch_id, b.id, b.ward_id,
            'discharge', 'discharge', OLD.admission_id
        FROM beds b WHERE b.id = OLD.bed_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_bed_allocation_status
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON bed_allocations
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION update_bed_on_allocation();

-- Trigger to update inventory on dispensing
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_inventory_on_dispensing()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update batch quantity
    UPDATE medicine_batches
    SET current_quantity = current_quantity - NEW.quantity_dispensed,
        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    WHERE id = NEW.batch_id;
    
    -- Update inventory
    UPDATE medicine_inventory mi
    SET quantity_on_hand = quantity_on_hand - NEW.quantity_dispensed,
        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    WHERE mi.drug_id = NEW.drug_id
    AND mi.pharmacy_id = (SELECT pharmacy_id FROM medicine_dispensing WHERE id = NEW.dispensing_id);
    
    -- Create transaction record
    INSERT INTO medicine_stock_transactions (
        organization_id, pharmacy_id, drug_id, batch_id,
        transaction_type, transaction_date, quantity,
        unit_price, reference_type, reference_id
    )
    SELECT 
        md.organization_id, md.pharmacy_id, NEW.drug_id, NEW.batch_id,
        'sale', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, -NEW.quantity_dispensed,
        NEW.unit_price, 'dispensing', NEW.dispensing_id
    FROM medicine_dispensing md WHERE md.id = NEW.dispensing_id;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_dispensing_inventory
AFTER INSERT ON medicine_dispensing_items
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION update_inventory_on_dispensing();

ALTER TABLE patients ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE admissions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE appointments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE bills ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE prescriptions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

    FOR ALL
    USING (organization_id = current_setting('app.organization_id', TRUE)::UUID);

CREATE POLICY organization_isolation_policy ON admissions
    FOR ALL
    USING (organization_id = current_setting('app.organization_id', TRUE)::UUID);

CREATE POLICY organization_isolation_policy ON appointments
    FOR ALL
    USING (organization_id = current_setting('app.organization_id', TRUE)::UUID);

CREATE POLICY organization_isolation_policy ON bills
    FOR ALL
    USING (organization_id = current_setting('app.organization_id', TRUE)::UUID);

CREATE POLICY organization_isolation_policy ON prescriptions
    FOR ALL
    USING (organization_id = current_setting('app.organization_id', TRUE)::UUID);

-- SECTION 34: MATERIALIZED VIEWS FOR REPORTING

-- Daily Census View
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_daily_census AS
SELECT 
    a.organization_id,
    a.branch_id,
    b.ward_id,
    DATE(a.admission_date) as census_date,
    COUNT(DISTINCT a.id) as total_admissions,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.status = 'admitted' THEN a.id END) as current_admitted,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.actual_discharge_date = DATE(a.admission_date) THEN a.id END) as same_day_discharges
FROM admissions a
JOIN bed_allocations b ON a.id = b.admission_id
GROUP BY a.organization_id, a.branch_id, b.ward_id, DATE(a.admission_date)
WITH DATA;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_mv_daily_census ON mv_daily_census(organization_id, branch_id, ward_id, census_date);
```

# Complete Hospital ERP PostgreSQL Schema (Continued - Final Part)

## 📁 PART 18: FINAL - FUNCTIONS, TRIGGERS & ROW LEVEL SECURITY (Continued)

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 34: MATERIALIZED VIEWS FOR REPORTING (Continued)
-- ============================================

-- Revenue Summary View
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_revenue_summary AS
SELECT 
    b.organization_id,
    b.branch_id,
    DATE(b.bill_date) as revenue_date,
    b.bill_type,
    bi.category as service_category,
    COUNT(DISTINCT b.id) as bill_count,
    SUM(bi.net_amount) as gross_revenue,
    SUM(bi.discount_amount) as total_discount,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.is_insurance THEN bi.net_amount ELSE 0 END) as insurance_revenue,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.is_corporate THEN bi.net_amount ELSE 0 END) as corporate_revenue,
    SUM(CASE WHEN NOT b.is_insurance AND NOT b.is_corporate THEN bi.net_amount ELSE 0 END) as self_pay_revenue
FROM bills b
JOIN bill_items bi ON b.id = bi.bill_id
WHERE b.is_cancelled = FALSE
GROUP BY b.organization_id, b.branch_id, DATE(b.bill_date), b.bill_type, bi.category
WITH DATA;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_mv_revenue_summary ON mv_revenue_summary(organization_id, branch_id, revenue_date, bill_type, service_category);

-- Doctor Performance View
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_doctor_performance AS
SELECT 
    d.organization_id,
    d.id as doctor_id,
    d.user_id,
    u.full_name as doctor_name,
    d.specialization,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', a.appointment_date) as month,
    COUNT(DISTINCT a.id) as total_appointments,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.status = 'completed' THEN a.id END) as completed_appointments,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.status = 'no_show' THEN a.id END) as no_shows,
    COUNT(DISTINCT adm.id) as admissions,
    COUNT(DISTINCT s.id) as surgeries,
    SUM(bi.net_amount) as revenue_generated,
    AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (ov.consultation_end_at - ov.consultation_start_at))/60) as avg_consultation_minutes
FROM doctors d
JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
LEFT JOIN appointments a ON d.id = a.doctor_id
LEFT JOIN opd_visits ov ON a.id = ov.appointment_id
LEFT JOIN admissions adm ON d.id = adm.primary_doctor_id
LEFT JOIN surgeries s ON d.id = s.primary_surgeon_id
LEFT JOIN bills b ON b.visit_id::text = a.id::text OR b.admission_id = adm.id
LEFT JOIN bill_items bi ON b.id = bi.bill_id AND bi.doctor_id = d.id
GROUP BY d.organization_id, d.id, d.user_id, u.full_name, d.specialization, DATE_TRUNC('month', a.appointment_date)
WITH DATA;

CREATE INDEX idx_mv_doctor_performance ON mv_doctor_performance(organization_id, doctor_id, month);

-- Bed Occupancy View
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_bed_occupancy AS
SELECT 
    w.organization_id,
    w.branch_id,
    w.id as ward_id,
    w.name as ward_name,
    w.ward_type,
    DATE(ba.allocation_datetime) as occupancy_date,
    COUNT(DISTINCT b.id) as total_beds,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN b.id END) as occupied_beds,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN b.status = 'available' THEN b.id END) as available_beds,
    ROUND(COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN b.id END)::NUMERIC / 
          NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT b.id), 0) * 100, 2) as occupancy_rate,
    AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (COALESCE(ba.release_datetime, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) - ba.allocation_datetime))/86400) as avg_los_days
FROM wards w
JOIN beds b ON w.id = b.ward_id
LEFT JOIN bed_allocations ba ON b.id = ba.bed_id
WHERE b.is_active = TRUE
GROUP BY w.organization_id, w.branch_id, w.id, w.name, w.ward_type, DATE(ba.allocation_datetime)
WITH DATA;

CREATE INDEX idx_mv_bed_occupancy ON mv_bed_occupancy(organization_id, branch_id, ward_id, occupancy_date);

-- Lab TAT View
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_lab_tat AS
SELECT 
    lo.organization_id,
    lo.branch_id,
    DATE(lo.order_datetime) as order_date,
    tc.department,
    tc.category,
    lo.priority,
    COUNT(DISTINCT loi.id) as total_tests,
    AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.result_datetime - lo.order_datetime))/3600) as avg_tat_hours,
    PERCENTILE_CONT(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.result_datetime - lo.order_datetime))/3600) as median_tat_hours,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN lr.is_critical THEN lr.id END) as critical_results,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN 
        EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.result_datetime - lo.order_datetime))/3600 > tc.tat_routine_hours 
        THEN loi.id END) as tat_breached
FROM lab_orders lo
JOIN lab_order_items loi ON lo.id = loi.order_id
JOIN test_catalog tc ON loi.test_id = tc.id
LEFT JOIN lab_results lr ON loi.id = lr.order_item_id
WHERE lo.status = 'completed'
GROUP BY lo.organization_id, lo.branch_id, DATE(lo.order_datetime), tc.department, tc.category, lo.priority
WITH DATA;

CREATE INDEX idx_mv_lab_tat ON mv_lab_tat(organization_id, branch_id, order_date);

-- Insurance Claims Summary View
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_insurance_claims_summary AS
SELECT 
    ic.organization_id,
    ic.insurance_company_id,
    ins.name as insurance_company_name,
    DATE_TRUNC('month', ic.created_at) as claim_month,
    ic.status,
    COUNT(DISTINCT ic.id) as claim_count,
    SUM(ic.net_claim_amount) as total_claimed,
    SUM(ic.approved_amount) as total_approved,
    SUM(ic.settled_amount) as total_settled,
    SUM(ic.disallowed_amount) as total_disallowed,
    AVG(EXTRACT(DAY FROM (ic.settlement_date - ic.submitted_at::date))) as avg_settlement_days
FROM insurance_claims ic
JOIN insurance_companies ins ON ic.insurance_company_id = ins.id
GROUP BY ic.organization_id, ic.insurance_company_id, ins.name, DATE_TRUNC('month', ic.created_at), ic.status
WITH DATA;

CREATE INDEX idx_mv_insurance_claims ON mv_insurance_claims_summary(organization_id, insurance_company_id, claim_month);

-- Refresh function for materialized views
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION refresh_all_materialized_views()
RETURNS VOID AS $$
BEGIN
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv_daily_census;
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv_revenue_summary;
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv_doctor_performance;
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv_bed_occupancy;
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv_lab_tat;
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv_insurance_claims_summary;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Ambulance Management
CREATE TABLE ambulances (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    ambulance_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    registration_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    ambulance_type VARCHAR(50), -- basic, advanced, icu, neonatal
    
    -- Vehicle Details
    make VARCHAR(100),
    model VARCHAR(100),
    year INTEGER,
    
    -- Equipment
    equipment_list JSONB,
    has_ventilator BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_defibrillator BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    has_oxygen BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Capacity
    patient_capacity INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'available', -- available, on_call, maintenance, out_of_service
    current_location POINT,
    
    -- Insurance
    insurance_valid_until DATE,
    fitness_valid_until DATE,
    
    -- Assigned Staff
    current_driver_id UUID REFERENCES employees(id),
    current_paramedic_id UUID REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, ambulance_code)
);

-- Ambulance Trips
CREATE TABLE ambulance_trips (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    trip_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    ambulance_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES ambulances(id),
    
    trip_type VARCHAR(50), -- emergency_pickup, inter_hospital_transfer, patient_drop, dead_body
    
    -- Patient
    patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    patient_name VARCHAR(255),
    patient_phone VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Pickup
    pickup_address TEXT,
    pickup_latitude DECIMAL(10, 8),
    pickup_longitude DECIMAL(11, 8),
    
    -- Destination
    destination_address TEXT,
    destination_latitude DECIMAL(10, 8),
    destination_longitude DECIMAL(11, 8),
    
    -- Timing
    request_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    dispatched_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    reached_pickup_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    left_pickup_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    reached_destination_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    trip_completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Distance & Duration
    distance_km DECIMAL(8, 2),
    duration_minutes INTEGER,
    
    -- Staff
    driver_id UUID REFERENCES employees(id),
    paramedic_id UUID REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    -- Clinical
    patient_condition_at_pickup TEXT,
    vitals_during_transport JSONB,
    treatment_given TEXT,
    
    -- Billing
    trip_charge DECIMAL(10, 2),
    additional_charges DECIMAL(10, 2),
    total_charge DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    bill_id UUID REFERENCES bills(id),
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'requested', -- requested, dispatched, in_progress, completed, cancelled
    
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, trip_number)
);

-- Blood Bank Management
CREATE TABLE blood_bank_inventory (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    blood_group blood_group_type NOT NULL,
    component_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- whole_blood, prbc, ffp, platelets, cryo
    
    unit_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    bag_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Donor Info
    donor_id UUID,
    donor_name VARCHAR(255),
    donation_date DATE,
    
    -- Collection
    collection_date DATE NOT NULL,
    collection_center VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Testing
    testing_status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, passed, failed
    tested_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    tested_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    hiv_result VARCHAR(20),
    hbsag_result VARCHAR(20),
    hcv_result VARCHAR(20),
    vdrl_result VARCHAR(20),
    malaria_result VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Storage
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    storage_temperature DECIMAL(4, 1),
    
    -- Expiry
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'available', -- available, reserved, issued, expired, discarded
    
    -- If issued
    issued_to_patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    issued_for_admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    issued_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    issued_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Cross Match
    cross_match_done BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    cross_match_result VARCHAR(20),
    cross_matched_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, unit_number)
);

-- Blood Requisitions
CREATE TABLE blood_requisitions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    requisition_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    surgery_id UUID REFERENCES surgeries(id),
    
    blood_group blood_group_type NOT NULL,
    
    -- Request
    component_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    units_requested INTEGER NOT NULL,
    
    indication TEXT,
    urgency VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'routine', -- routine, urgent, emergency
    
    requested_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    required_by TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Approval
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, approved, partial_issued, issued, cancelled
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    units_approved INTEGER,
    units_issued INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Cross Match
    sample_collected BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    sample_collected_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, requisition_number)
);

-- Transfusion Records
CREATE TABLE transfusion_records (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    requisition_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES blood_requisitions(id),
    blood_unit_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES blood_bank_inventory(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- Pre-Transfusion
    pre_transfusion_vitals JSONB,
    pre_transfusion_check_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Transfusion
    transfusion_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    transfusion_completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    administered_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    supervised_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Monitoring
    monitoring_chart JSONB, -- Vitals every 15 mins
    
    -- Reaction
    reaction_occurred BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    reaction_type VARCHAR(100),
    reaction_severity VARCHAR(20),
    reaction_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    action_taken TEXT,
    
    -- Post-Transfusion
    post_transfusion_vitals JSONB,
    
    outcome VARCHAR(50), -- completed, stopped_reaction, stopped_other
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

);

    -- Visitor Info

-- Canteen Orders
CREATE TABLE canteen_orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    order_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Customer
    customer_type VARCHAR(50), -- staff, visitor, patient_attendant
    employee_id UUID REFERENCES employees(id),
    customer_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    order_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- Items
    items JSONB NOT NULL, -- [{item_id, quantity, price}]
    
    subtotal DECIMAL(10, 2),
    discount DECIMAL(8, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Payment
    payment_method payment_method_type,
    payment_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    
    -- Fulfillment
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, preparing, ready, delivered, cancelled
    
    prepared_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    delivered_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, order_number)
);

-- Kitchen Management
CREATE TABLE kitchen_orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    order_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    order_type VARCHAR(50), -- patient_meal, canteen, event
    
    order_date DATE NOT NULL,
    meal_type VARCHAR(20), -- breakfast, lunch, dinner, snack
    
    -- Source
    source_type VARCHAR(50), -- diet_order, canteen_order, manual
    source_id UUID,
    
    -- Items
    items JSONB NOT NULL,
    
    -- Quantities
    total_portions INTEGER,
    
    -- Timing
    required_by TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, preparing, ready, dispatched
    
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    prepared_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, order_number)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 37: DATABASE MAINTENANCE PROCEDURES

-- Procedure to archive old data
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE archive_old_data(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_archive_before_date DATE
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Archive old audit logs
    INSERT INTO audit_logs_archive
    SELECT * FROM audit_logs 
    WHERE organization_id = p_organization_id 
    AND created_at < p_archive_before_date;
    
    DELETE FROM audit_logs 
    WHERE organization_id = p_organization_id 
    AND created_at < p_archive_before_date;
    
    -- Archive old notification logs
    INSERT INTO notification_log_archive
    SELECT * FROM notification_log 
    WHERE created_at < p_archive_before_date;
    
    DELETE FROM notification_log 
    WHERE created_at < p_archive_before_date;
    
    RAISE NOTICE 'Data archived successfully for organization %', p_organization_id;
END;
$$;

-- Procedure to clean up expired sessions
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE cleanup_expired_sessions()
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_deleted_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    DELETE FROM user_sessions 
    WHERE expires_at < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    OR (last_activity_at < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '24 hours' AND is_active = TRUE);
    
    GET DIAGNOSTICS v_deleted_count = ROW_COUNT;
    
    RAISE NOTICE 'Cleaned up % expired sessions', v_deleted_count;
END;
$$;

-- Procedure to update bed census
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE update_daily_census(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_census_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO ward_census (
        organization_id, branch_id, ward_id, census_date,
        total_beds, occupied_beds, available_beds, reserved_beds,
        cleaning_beds, maintenance_beds, occupancy_percentage,
        admissions_today, discharges_today, transfers_in, transfers_out, deaths_today
    )
    SELECT 
        w.organization_id,
        w.branch_id,
        w.id as ward_id,
        p_census_date,
        COUNT(b.id) as total_beds,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END) as occupied_beds,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'available' THEN 1 END) as available_beds,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'reserved' THEN 1 END) as reserved_beds,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'cleaning' THEN 1 END) as cleaning_beds,
        COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'maintenance' THEN 1 END) as maintenance_beds,
        ROUND(COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END)::NUMERIC / NULLIF(COUNT(b.id), 0) * 100, 2),
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a WHERE a.admission_date = p_census_date AND a.branch_id = w.branch_id),
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a WHERE a.actual_discharge_date = p_census_date AND a.branch_id = w.branch_id),
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bed_transfer_requests btr WHERE DATE(btr.transferred_at) = p_census_date AND btr.to_ward_id = w.id AND btr.status = 'completed'),
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bed_transfer_requests btr WHERE DATE(btr.transferred_at) = p_census_date AND btr.from_ward_id = w.id AND btr.status = 'completed'),
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a WHERE a.actual_discharge_date = p_census_date AND a.discharge_type = 'expired' AND a.branch_id = w.branch_id)
    FROM wards w
    LEFT JOIN beds b ON w.id = b.ward_id AND b.is_active = TRUE
    WHERE w.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND w.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY w.organization_id, w.branch_id, w.id
    ON CONFLICT (ward_id, census_date)
    DO UPDATE SET
        total_beds = EXCLUDED.total_beds,
        occupied_beds = EXCLUDED.occupied_beds,
        available_beds = EXCLUDED.available_beds,
        reserved_beds = EXCLUDED.reserved_beds,
        cleaning_beds = EXCLUDED.cleaning_beds,
        maintenance_beds = EXCLUDED.maintenance_beds,
        occupancy_percentage = EXCLUDED.occupancy_percentage,
        admissions_today = EXCLUDED.admissions_today,
        discharges_today = EXCLUDED.discharges_today,
        transfers_in = EXCLUDED.transfers_in,
        transfers_out = EXCLUDED.transfers_out,
        deaths_today = EXCLUDED.deaths_today;
END;
$$;

-- Create application roles
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'hospital_app') THEN
        CREATE ROLE hospital_app LOGIN PASSWORD 'secure_password_here';
    END IF;
    
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'hospital_readonly') THEN
        CREATE ROLE hospital_readonly;
    END IF;
END
$$;

-- Grant permissions to application role
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO hospital_app;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO hospital_app;
GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO hospital_app;
GRANT EXECUTE ON ALL FUNCTIONS IN SCHEMA public TO hospital_app;

-- Grant readonly permissions
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO hospital_readonly;
GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO hospital_readonly;

-- Set default privileges for future objects
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON TABLES TO hospital_app;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON SEQUENCES TO hospital_app;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT SELECT ON TABLES TO hospital_readonly;

-- ============================================
-- SCHEMA COMPLETE
-- ============================================

-- Final statistics
DO $$
DECLARE
    table_count INTEGER;
    index_count INTEGER;
    function_count INTEGER;
    trigger_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO table_count FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE';
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO index_count FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname = 'public';
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO function_count FROM information_schema.routines WHERE routine_schema = 'public';
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO trigger_count FROM information_schema.triggers WHERE trigger_schema = 'public';
    
    RAISE NOTICE '==========================================';
    RAISE NOTICE 'Hospital ERP Schema Creation Complete';
    RAISE NOTICE '==========================================';
    RAISE NOTICE 'Tables created: %', table_count;
    RAISE NOTICE 'Indexes created: %', index_count;
    RAISE NOTICE 'Functions created: %', function_count;
    RAISE NOTICE 'Triggers created: %', trigger_count;
    RAISE NOTICE '==========================================';
END
$$;
```

### Key Features Implemented:

✅ **Multi-Tenancy** - Organization → Branch → Department hierarchy  
✅ **Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)** - Granular permissions  
✅ **Row-Level Security (RLS)** - Data isolation per organization  
✅ **Audit Logging** - Complete change tracking  
✅ **Materialized Views** - For reporting performance  
✅ **Triggers** - Automated workflows  
✅ **Stored Procedures** - Business logic  
✅ **Sequential Number Generation** - For all document numbers  
✅ **Full-Text Search Support** - Using pg_trgm  
✅ **JSONB for Flexibility** - For dynamic fields
