```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 41: TABLE PARTITIONING FOR LARGE TABLES
-- ============================================

-- Partition audit_logs by month for better performance
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_partitioned (
    id UUID DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    username VARCHAR(100),
    ip_address INET,
    user_agent TEXT,
    action VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    module VARCHAR(100),
    sub_module VARCHAR(100),
    table_name VARCHAR(100),
    record_id UUID,
    old_values JSONB,
    new_values JSONB,
    changed_fields TEXT[],
    description TEXT,
    metadata JSONB,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    PRIMARY KEY (id, created_at)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (created_at);

-- Create partitions for audit logs
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m01 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2024-02-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m02 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-02-01') TO ('2024-03-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m03 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-03-01') TO ('2024-04-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m04 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-04-01') TO ('2024-05-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m05 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-05-01') TO ('2024-06-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m06 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-06-01') TO ('2024-07-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m07 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-07-01') TO ('2024-08-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m08 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-08-01') TO ('2024-09-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m09 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-09-01') TO ('2024-10-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m10 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-10-01') TO ('2024-11-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m11 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-11-01') TO ('2024-12-01');
CREATE TABLE audit_logs_y2024m12 PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-12-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Function to create future partitions automatically
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_audit_partition()
RETURNS VOID AS $$
DECLARE
    partition_date DATE;
    partition_name TEXT;
    start_date DATE;
    end_date DATE;
BEGIN
    -- Create partitions for next 3 months
    FOR i IN 0..2 LOOP
        partition_date := DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE + (i || ' months')::INTERVAL);
        partition_name := 'audit_logs_y' || TO_CHAR(partition_date, 'YYYY') || 'm' || TO_CHAR(partition_date, 'MM');
        start_date := partition_date;
        end_date := partition_date + INTERVAL '1 month';
        
        -- Check if partition exists
        IF NOT EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM pg_class WHERE relname = partition_name
        ) THEN
            EXECUTE format(
                'CREATE TABLE %I PARTITION OF audit_logs_partitioned FOR VALUES FROM (%L) TO (%L)',
                partition_name, start_date, end_date
            );
            RAISE NOTICE 'Created partition: %', partition_name;
        END IF;
    END LOOP;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Partition lab_results by month
CREATE TABLE lab_results_partitioned (
    id UUID DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_id UUID NOT NULL,
    result_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    parameter_name VARCHAR(255),
    parameter_code VARCHAR(50),
    result_value VARCHAR(500),
    result_numeric DECIMAL(15, 5),
    result_unit VARCHAR(50),
    reference_min DECIMAL(15, 5),
    reference_max DECIMAL(15, 5),
    reference_text VARCHAR(255),
    abnormal_flag VARCHAR(10),
    is_critical BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    critical_notified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    critical_notified_to UUID,
    critical_notified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    previous_value DECIMAL(15, 5),
    previous_date DATE,
    delta_flag BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    result_comment TEXT,
    technical_comment TEXT,
    entered_by UUID,
    entered_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    verified_by UUID,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    status result_status_type DEFAULT 'preliminary',
    analyzer_name VARCHAR(100),
    analyzer_result_id VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    PRIMARY KEY (id, result_datetime)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (result_datetime);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 42: FULL-TEXT SEARCH CONFIGURATION
-- ============================================

-- Create custom text search configuration for Bangla/English
CREATE TEXT SEARCH CONFIGURATION hospital_search (COPY = pg_catalog.english);

-- Add full-text search columns to patients
ALTER TABLE patients ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS search_vector tsvector;

-- Function to update patient search vector
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_patient_search_vector()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.search_vector := 
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.mrn, '')), 'A') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.first_name, '')), 'A') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.last_name, '')), 'A') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.primary_phone, '')), 'B') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.nid, '')), 'B') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.email, '')), 'C') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.present_address_line1, '')), 'D');
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_patient_search_vector
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON patients
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION update_patient_search_vector();

-- Create GIN index for full-text search
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_search ON patients USING GIN(search_vector);

-- Full-text search function for patients
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION search_patients(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_search_term TEXT,
    p_limit INTEGER DEFAULT 50
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    id UUID,
    mrn VARCHAR,
    full_name VARCHAR,
    primary_phone VARCHAR,
    date_of_birth DATE,
    gender gender_type,
    rank REAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        p.id,
        p.mrn,
        p.full_name,
        p.primary_phone,
        p.date_of_birth,
        p.gender,
        ts_rank(p.search_vector, plainto_tsquery('english', p_search_term)) as rank
    FROM patients p
    WHERE p.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND p.is_active = TRUE
    AND (
        p.search_vector @@ plainto_tsquery('english', p_search_term)
        OR p.mrn ILIKE '%' || p_search_term || '%'
        OR p.primary_phone ILIKE '%' || p_search_term || '%'
        OR p.nid ILIKE '%' || p_search_term || '%'
        OR p.full_name ILIKE '%' || p_search_term || '%'
    )
    ORDER BY rank DESC, p.full_name
    LIMIT p_limit;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Add search vector to drug_master
ALTER TABLE drug_master ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS search_vector tsvector;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_drug_search_vector()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.search_vector := 
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.generic_name, '')), 'A') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.brand_name, '')), 'A') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.manufacturer, '')), 'B') ||
        setweight(to_tsvector('english', COALESCE(NEW.therapeutic_class, '')), 'C');
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_drug_search_vector
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON drug_master
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION update_drug_search_vector();

CREATE INDEX idx_drug_search ON drug_master USING GIN(search_vector);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 43: API HELPER FUNCTIONS
-- ============================================

-- Function to get patient summary
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_patient_summary(p_patient_id UUID)
RETURNS JSONB AS $$
DECLARE
    v_result JSONB;
BEGIN
    SELECT jsonb_build_object(
        'patient', jsonb_build_object(
            'id', p.id,
            'mrn', p.mrn,
            'full_name', p.full_name,
            'date_of_birth', p.date_of_birth,
            'age', calculate_age(p.date_of_birth),
            'gender', p.gender,
            'blood_group', p.blood_group,
            'phone', p.primary_phone,
            'email', p.email,
            'photo_url', p.photo_url,
            'is_vip', p.is_vip
        ),
        'allergies', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'allergen', pa.allergen_name,
                'severity', pa.severity,
                'reaction', pa.reaction
            )), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM patient_allergies pa
            WHERE pa.patient_id = p.id AND pa.is_active = TRUE
        ),
        'chronic_conditions', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'condition', pcc.condition_name,
                'diagnosed_date', pcc.diagnosis_date
            )), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM patient_chronic_conditions pcc
            WHERE pcc.patient_id = p.id AND pcc.is_active = TRUE
        ),
        'current_medications', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'medication', pm.medication_name,
                'dose', pm.dose,
                'frequency', pm.frequency
            )), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM patient_medications pm
            WHERE pm.patient_id = p.id AND pm.is_current = TRUE
        ),
        'recent_visits', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'date', ov.visit_date,
                'doctor', u.full_name,
                'chief_complaint', ov.chief_complaint
            ) ORDER BY ov.visit_date DESC), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM opd_visits ov
            JOIN doctors d ON ov.doctor_id = d.id
            JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
            WHERE ov.patient_id = p.id
            LIMIT 5
        ),
        'active_admission', (
            SELECT jsonb_build_object(
                'admission_number', a.admission_number,
                'admission_date', a.admission_date,
                'ward', w.name,
                'bed', b.bed_number,
                'doctor', u.full_name,
                'diagnosis', a.provisional_diagnosis
            )
            FROM admissions a
            LEFT JOIN bed_allocations ba ON a.id = ba.admission_id AND ba.release_datetime IS NULL
            LEFT JOIN beds b ON ba.bed_id = b.id
            LEFT JOIN wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
            LEFT JOIN doctors d ON a.primary_doctor_id = d.id
            LEFT JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
            WHERE a.patient_id = p.id AND a.status = 'admitted'
            LIMIT 1
        )
    ) INTO v_result
    FROM patients p
    WHERE p.id = p_patient_id;
    
    RETURN v_result;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Function to get admission details
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_admission_details(p_admission_id UUID)
RETURNS JSONB AS $$
DECLARE
    v_result JSONB;
BEGIN
    SELECT jsonb_build_object(
        'admission', jsonb_build_object(
            'id', a.id,
            'admission_number', a.admission_number,
            'admission_date', a.admission_date,
            'admission_time', a.admission_time,
            'status', a.status,
            'admission_type', a.admission_type,
            'provisional_diagnosis', a.provisional_diagnosis
        ),
        'patient', jsonb_build_object(
            'id', p.id,
            'mrn', p.mrn,
            'full_name', p.full_name,
            'age', calculate_age(p.date_of_birth),
            'gender', p.gender,
            'blood_group', p.blood_group
        ),
        'bed', (
            SELECT jsonb_build_object(
                'ward', w.name,
                'room', r.room_number,
                'bed', b.bed_number,
                'bed_type', b.bed_type,
                'daily_rate', b.daily_rate
            )
            FROM bed_allocations ba
            JOIN beds b ON ba.bed_id = b.id
            JOIN wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
            LEFT JOIN rooms r ON b.room_id = r.id
            WHERE ba.admission_id = a.id AND ba.release_datetime IS NULL
            LIMIT 1
        ),
        'primary_doctor', (
            SELECT jsonb_build_object(
                'id', d.id,
                'name', u.full_name,
                'specialization', d.specialization
            )
            FROM doctors d
            JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
            WHERE d.id = a.primary_doctor_id
        ),
        'vitals', (
            SELECT jsonb_build_object(
                'latest', (
                    SELECT jsonb_build_object(
                        'recorded_at', pv.recorded_datetime,
                        'temperature', pv.temperature,
                        'pulse_rate', pv.pulse_rate,
                        'bp_systolic', pv.blood_pressure_systolic,
                        'bp_diastolic', pv.blood_pressure_diastolic,
                        'spo2', pv.spo2,
                        'respiratory_rate', pv.respiratory_rate
                    )
                    FROM patient_vitals pv
                    WHERE pv.admission_id = a.id
                    ORDER BY pv.recorded_datetime DESC
                    LIMIT 1
                )
            )
        ),
        'pending_orders', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'order_number', do.order_number,
                'order_type', do.order_type,
                'order_text', do.order_text,
                'priority', do.priority,
                'ordered_at', do.order_datetime
            )), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM doctor_orders do
            WHERE do.admission_id = a.id AND do.status = 'pending'
        ),
        'pending_labs', (
            SELECT COUNT(*)
            FROM lab_order_items loi
            JOIN lab_orders lo ON loi.order_id = lo.id
            WHERE lo.admission_id = a.id AND loi.status = 'pending'
        ),
        'bill_summary', (
            SELECT jsonb_build_object(
                'total_charges', COALESCE(SUM(bi.net_amount), 0),
                'total_paid', COALESCE(SUM(p.amount), 0),
                'balance', COALESCE(SUM(bi.net_amount), 0) - COALESCE(SUM(p.amount), 0)
            )
            FROM bills b
            LEFT JOIN bill_items bi ON b.id = bi.bill_id
            LEFT JOIN payments p ON b.id = p.bill_id AND p.status = 'completed'
            WHERE b.admission_id = a.id AND b.is_cancelled = FALSE
        )
    ) INTO v_result
    FROM admissions a
    JOIN patients p ON a.patient_id = p.id
    WHERE a.id = p_admission_id;
    
    RETURN v_result;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Function to get ward dashboard
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_ward_dashboard(p_ward_id UUID)
RETURNS JSONB AS $$
DECLARE
    v_result JSONB;
BEGIN
    SELECT jsonb_build_object(
        'ward_info', jsonb_build_object(
            'id', w.id,
            'name', w.name,
            'ward_type', w.ward_type,
            'total_beds', w.total_beds
        ),
        'bed_summary', jsonb_build_object(
            'total', COUNT(b.id),
            'occupied', COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END),
            'available', COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'available' THEN 1 END),
            'reserved', COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'reserved' THEN 1 END),
            'cleaning', COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'cleaning' THEN 1 END),
            'maintenance', COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'maintenance' THEN 1 END),
            'occupancy_rate', ROUND(
                COUNT(CASE WHEN b.status = 'occupied' THEN 1 END)::NUMERIC / 
                NULLIF(COUNT(b.id), 0) * 100, 1
            )
        ),
        'patients', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'bed_number', bed.bed_number,
                'patient_name', p.full_name,
                'mrn', p.mrn,
                'age', calculate_age(p.date_of_birth),
                'gender', p.gender,
                'admission_date', a.admission_date,
                'los_days', CURRENT_DATE - a.admission_date,
                'doctor', u.full_name,
                'diagnosis', a.provisional_diagnosis,
                'latest_vitals', (
                    SELECT jsonb_build_object(
                        'temp', pv.temperature,
                        'pulse', pv.pulse_rate,
                        'bp', pv.blood_pressure_systolic || '/' || pv.blood_pressure_diastolic,
                        'spo2', pv.spo2
                    )
                    FROM patient_vitals pv
                    WHERE pv.admission_id = a.id
                    ORDER BY pv.recorded_datetime DESC
                    LIMIT 1
                ),
                'pending_orders', (
                    SELECT COUNT(*)
                    FROM doctor_orders do
                    WHERE do.admission_id = a.id AND do.status = 'pending'
                )
            ) ORDER BY bed.bed_number), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM beds bed
            JOIN admissions a ON bed.current_admission_id = a.id
            JOIN patients p ON a.patient_id = p.id
            LEFT JOIN doctors d ON a.primary_doctor_id = d.id
            LEFT JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
            WHERE bed.ward_id = w.id AND bed.status = 'occupied'
        ),
        'today_stats', jsonb_build_object(
            'admissions', (
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions 
                WHERE admission_date = CURRENT_DATE 
                AND branch_id = w.branch_id
            ),
            'discharges', (
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions 
                WHERE actual_discharge_date = CURRENT_DATE 
                AND branch_id = w.branch_id
            ),
            'critical_patients', (
                SELECT COUNT(*) 
                FROM admissions a
                JOIN bed_allocations ba ON a.id = ba.admission_id
                JOIN beds b ON ba.bed_id = b.id
                WHERE b.ward_id = w.id 
                AND a.status = 'admitted'
                AND EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM patient_vitals pv 
                    WHERE pv.admission_id = a.id 
                    AND pv.recorded_datetime > CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '24 hours'
                    AND (pv.blood_pressure_systolic < 90 OR pv.spo2 < 90)
                )
            )
        ),
        'nurses_on_duty', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'name', u.full_name,
                'shift', ndr.shift,
                'is_head_nurse', n.is_head_nurse
            )), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM nurse_duty_roster ndr
            JOIN nurses n ON ndr.nurse_id = n.id
            JOIN users u ON n.user_id = u.id
            WHERE ndr.ward_id = w.id 
            AND ndr.duty_date = CURRENT_DATE
            AND ndr.status = 'scheduled'
        )
    ) INTO v_result
    FROM wards w
    LEFT JOIN beds b ON w.id = b.ward_id AND b.is_active = TRUE
    WHERE w.id = p_ward_id
    GROUP BY w.id, w.name, w.ward_type, w.total_beds, w.branch_id;
    
    RETURN v_result;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Function to get doctor schedule
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_doctor_schedule(
    p_doctor_id UUID,
    p_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS JSONB AS $$
DECLARE
    v_result JSONB;
BEGIN
    SELECT jsonb_build_object(
        'doctor', jsonb_build_object(
            'id', d.id,
            'name', u.full_name,
            'specialization', d.specialization,
            'opd_fee', d.opd_fee
        ),
        'date', p_date,
        'schedule', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'branch', br.name,
                'session1_start', dst.session1_start,
                'session1_end', dst.session1_end,
                'session1_room', dst.session1_room,
                'session2_start', dst.session2_start,
                'session2_end', dst.session2_end,
                'session2_room', dst.session2_room
            )), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM doctor_schedule_templates dst
            JOIN branches br ON dst.branch_id = br.id
            WHERE dst.doctor_id = d.id
            AND dst.day_of_week = EXTRACT(DOW FROM p_date)
            AND dst.is_active = TRUE
            AND NOT EXISTS (
                SELECT 1 FROM doctor_schedule_overrides dso
                WHERE dso.doctor_id = d.id
                AND dso.override_date = p_date
                AND dso.override_type = 'leave'
            )
        ),
        'appointments', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'time', a.appointment_time,
                'patient_name', p.full_name,
                'patient_phone', p.primary_phone,
                'type', a.appointment_type,
                'status', a.status,
                'token', a.token_number
            ) ORDER BY a.appointment_time), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM appointments a
            JOIN patients p ON a.patient_id = p.id
            WHERE a.doctor_id = d.id
            AND a.appointment_date = p_date
            AND a.status NOT IN ('cancelled')
        ),
        'appointment_summary', jsonb_build_object(
            'total', (
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM appointments 
                WHERE doctor_id = d.id AND appointment_date = p_date
                AND status NOT IN ('cancelled')
            ),
            'completed', (
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM appointments 
                WHERE doctor_id = d.id AND appointment_date = p_date
                AND status = 'completed'
            ),
            'waiting', (
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM appointments 
                WHERE doctor_id = d.id AND appointment_date = p_date
                AND status IN ('scheduled', 'checked_in')
            ),
            'no_show', (
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM appointments 
                WHERE doctor_id = d.id AND appointment_date = p_date
                AND status = 'no_show'
            )
        ),
        'ipd_patients', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'patient_name', p.full_name,
                'ward', w.name,
                'bed', b.bed_number,
                'diagnosis', adm.provisional_diagnosis,
                'los_days', CURRENT_DATE - adm.admission_date
            )), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM admissions adm
            JOIN patients p ON adm.patient_id = p.id
            JOIN bed_allocations ba ON adm.id = ba.admission_id AND ba.release_datetime IS NULL
            JOIN beds b ON ba.bed_id = b.id
            JOIN wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
            WHERE adm.primary_doctor_id = d.id
            AND adm.status = 'admitted'
        ),
        'surgeries', (
            SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
                'time', s.scheduled_start_time,
                'patient_name', p.full_name,
                'surgery', s.surgery_name,
                'ot_room', otr.room_number,
                'status', s.status
            ) ORDER BY s.scheduled_start_time), '[]'::jsonb)
            FROM surgeries s
            JOIN patients p ON s.patient_id = p.id
            JOIN ot_rooms otr ON s.ot_room_id = otr.id
            WHERE s.primary_surgeon_id = d.id
            AND s.scheduled_date = p_date
            AND s.status NOT IN ('cancelled')
        )
    ) INTO v_result
    FROM doctors d
    JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
    WHERE d.id = p_doctor_id;
    
    RETURN v_result;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 44: VALIDATION FUNCTIONS
-- ============================================

-- Validate NID format (Bangladesh)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_nid(p_nid VARCHAR)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
    -- NID can be 10, 13, or 17 digits
    IF p_nid IS NULL THEN
        RETURN TRUE;
    END IF;
    
    IF p_nid ~ '^\d{10}$' OR p_nid ~ '^\d{13}$' OR p_nid ~ '^\d{17}$' THEN
        RETURN TRUE;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN FALSE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE;

-- Validate phone number (Bangladesh)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_phone_bd(p_phone VARCHAR)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
    IF p_phone IS NULL THEN
        RETURN TRUE;
    END IF;
    
    -- Accepts: +8801XXXXXXXXX, 01XXXXXXXXX, 8801XXXXXXXXX
    IF p_phone ~ '^\+?880?1[3-9]\d{8}$' THEN
        RETURN TRUE;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN FALSE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE;

-- Validate email
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_email(p_email VARCHAR)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
    IF p_email IS NULL THEN
        RETURN TRUE;
    END IF;
    
    IF p_email ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$' THEN
        RETURN TRUE;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN FALSE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE;

-- Add constraints using validation functions
ALTER TABLE patients ADD CONSTRAINT chk_patient_nid CHECK (validate_nid(nid));
ALTER TABLE patients ADD CONSTRAINT chk_patient_phone CHECK (validate_phone_bd(primary_phone));
ALTER TABLE patients ADD CONSTRAINT chk_patient_email CHECK (validate_email(email));

-- Validate age for procedures
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_age_restriction(
    p_patient_id UUID,
    p_procedure_code VARCHAR,
    p_min_age_years INTEGER DEFAULT NULL,
    p_max_age_years INTEGER DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
DECLARE
    v_age_years INTEGER;
BEGIN
    SELECT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM age(CURRENT_DATE, date_of_birth))
    INTO v_age_years
    FROM patients
    WHERE id = p_patient_id;
    
    IF p_min_age_years IS NOT NULL AND v_age_years < p_min_age_years THEN
        RETURN FALSE;
    END IF;
    
    IF p_max_age_years IS NOT NULL AND v_age_years > p_max_age_years THEN
        RETURN FALSE;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN TRUE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 45: BUSINESS LOGIC FUNCTIONS
-- ============================================

-- Function to calculate bill total
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION calculate_bill_total(p_bill_id UUID)
RETURNS TABLE(
    gross_amount DECIMAL,
    discount_amount DECIMAL,
    tax_amount DECIMAL,
    net_amount DECIMAL,
    insurance_amount DECIMAL,
    patient_payable DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH bill_calc AS (
        SELECT 
            COALESCE(SUM(bi.net_amount), 0) as gross,
            COALESCE(SUM(bi.discount_amount), 0) as discount,
            COALESCE(SUM(bi.tax_amount), 0) as tax
        FROM bill_items bi
        WHERE bi.bill_id = p_bill_id
        AND bi.is_cancelled = FALSE
    ),
    insurance_calc AS (
        SELECT COALESCE(ip.approved_amount, 0) as ins_amount
        FROM bills b
        LEFT JOIN insurance_preauth ip ON b.preauth_id = ip.id
        WHERE b.id = p_bill_id
    )
    SELECT 
        bc.gross,
        bc.discount,
        bc.tax,
        bc.gross - bc.discount + bc.tax as net,
        ic.ins_amount,
        GREATEST(bc.gross - bc.discount + bc.tax - ic.ins_amount, 0) as patient_pay
    FROM bill_calc bc, insurance_calc ic;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Function to check drug availability
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_drug_availability(
    p_pharmacy_id UUID,
    p_drug_id UUID,
    p_quantity INTEGER
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    is_available BOOLEAN,
    available_quantity INTEGER,
    nearest_expiry DATE,
    batches JSONB
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        SUM(mb.current_quantity) >= p_quantity,
        COALESCE(SUM(mb.current_quantity)::INTEGER, 0),
        MIN(mb.expiry_date),
        jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
            'batch_number', mb.batch_number,
            'quantity', mb.current_quantity,
            'expiry_date', mb.expiry_date
        ) ORDER BY mb.expiry_date)
    FROM medicine_batches mb
    WHERE mb.pharmacy_id = p_pharmacy_id
    AND mb.drug_id = p_drug_id
    AND mb.current_quantity > 0
    AND mb.expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE
    AND mb.is_active = TRUE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Function to get next available appointment slot
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_next_available_slot(
    p_doctor_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_days_ahead INTEGER DEFAULT 7
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    slot_date DATE,
    slot_time TIME,
    is_available BOOLEAN
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_date DATE;
    v_day_of_week INTEGER;
    v_schedule RECORD;
    v_slot_time TIME;
    v_slot_duration INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Get consultation duration
    SELECT consultation_duration INTO v_slot_duration
    FROM doctors WHERE id = p_doctor_id;
    
    v_slot_duration := COALESCE(v_slot_duration, 15);
    
    FOR v_date IN SELECT generate_series(p_from_date, p_from_date + p_days_ahead, '1 day')::DATE
    LOOP
        v_day_of_week := EXTRACT(DOW FROM v_date);
        
        -- Check if doctor has schedule for this day
        FOR v_schedule IN
            SELECT session1_start, session1_end, session2_start, session2_end
            FROM doctor_schedule_templates
            WHERE doctor_id = p_doctor_id
            AND branch_id = p_branch_id
            AND day_of_week = v_day_of_week
            AND is_active = TRUE
            AND NOT EXISTS (
                SELECT 1 FROM doctor_schedule_overrides
                WHERE doctor_id = p_doctor_id
                AND override_date = v_date
                AND override_type = 'leave'
            )
        LOOP
            -- Session 1 slots
            IF v_schedule.session1_start IS NOT NULL THEN
                v_slot_time := v_schedule.session1_start;
                WHILE v_slot_time < v_schedule.session1_end LOOP
                    slot_date := v_date;
                    slot_time := v_slot_time;
                    is_available := NOT EXISTS (
                        SELECT 1 FROM appointments
                        WHERE doctor_id = p_doctor_id
                        AND appointment_date = v_date
                        AND appointment_time = v_slot_time
                        AND status NOT IN ('cancelled')
                    );
                    
                    IF is_available THEN
                        RETURN NEXT;
                    END IF;
                    
                    v_slot_time := v_slot_time + (v_slot_duration || ' minutes')::INTERVAL;
                END LOOP;
            END IF;
            
            -- Session 2 slots
            IF v_schedule.session2_start IS NOT NULL THEN
                v_slot_time := v_schedule.session2_start;
                WHILE v_slot_time < v_schedule.session2_end LOOP
                    slot_date := v_date;
                    slot_time := v_slot_time;
                    is_available := NOT EXISTS (
                        SELECT 1 FROM appointments
                        WHERE doctor_id = p_doctor_id
                        AND appointment_date = v_date
                        AND appointment_time = v_slot_time
                        AND status NOT IN ('cancelled')
                    );
                    
                    IF is_available THEN
                        RETURN NEXT;
                    END IF;
                    
                    v_slot_time := v_slot_time + (v_slot_duration || ' minutes')::INTERVAL;
                END LOOP;
            END IF;
        END LOOP;
    END LOOP;
    
    RETURN;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Function to calculate LOS (Length of Stay)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION calculate_los(
    p_admission_date DATE,
    p_admission_time TIME,
    p_discharge_date DATE DEFAULT NULL,
    p_discharge_time TIME DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    total_days INTEGER,
    billable_days INTEGER,
    hours INTEGER,
    minutes INTEGER
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_admission TIMESTAMPTZ;
    v_discharge TIMESTAMPTZ;
    v_interval INTERVAL;
BEGIN
    v_admission := (p_admission_date || ' ' || COALESCE(p_admission_time, '00:00:00'))::TIMESTAMPTZ;
    v_discharge := (COALESCE(p_discharge_date, CURRENT_DATE) || ' ' || COALESCE(p_discharge_time, CURRENT_TIME))::TIMESTAMPTZ;
    
    v_interval := v_discharge - v_admission;
    
    total_days := EXTRACT(DAY FROM v_interval);
    billable_days := GREATEST(CEIL(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM v_interval) / 86400), 1)::INTEGER;
    hours := EXTRACT(HOUR FROM v_interval);
    minutes := EXTRACT(MINUTE FROM v_interval);
    
    RETURN NEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 46: NOTIFICATION TRIGGERS
-- ============================================

-- Trigger for critical lab results
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION notify_critical_lab_result()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.is_critical = TRUE AND NEW.critical_notified = FALSE THEN
        -- Insert into notification queue
        INSERT INTO notification_queue (
            organization_id,
            channel,
            recipient_type,
            recipient_id,
            recipient_contact,
            subject,
            body,
            reference_type,
            reference_id,
            status
        )
        SELECT
            lo.organization_id,
            'push',
            'doctor',
            a.primary_doctor_id,
            u.email,
            'CRITICAL LAB RESULT',
            'Critical result for patient ' || p.full_name || ': ' || tc.name || ' = ' || NEW.result_value,
            'lab_result',
            NEW.id,
            'pending'
        FROM lab_orders lo
        JOIN patients p ON lo.patient_id = p.id
        LEFT JOIN admissions a ON lo.admission_id = a.id
        LEFT JOIN doctors d ON a.primary_doctor_id = d.id
        LEFT JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
        JOIN test_catalog tc ON NEW.test_id = tc.id
        WHERE lo.id = NEW.order_id;
        
        -- Update notification flag
        NEW.critical_notified := TRUE;
        NEW.critical_notified_at := CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_critical_lab_notification
BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON lab_results
FOR EACH ROW
WHEN (NEW.is_critical = TRUE)
EXECUTE FUNCTION notify_critical_lab_result();

-- Trigger for appointment reminders
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION queue_appointment_reminder()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Queue SMS reminder for next day appointments
    IF NEW.appointment_date = CURRENT_DATE + 1 THEN
        INSERT INTO notification_queue (
            organization_id,
            channel,
            recipient_type,
            recipient_id,
            recipient_contact,
            subject,
            body,
            reference_type,
            reference_id,
            scheduled_at
        )
        SELECT
            NEW.organization_id,
            'sms',
            'patient',
            NEW.patient_id,
            p.primary_phone,
            'Appointment Reminder',
            'Dear ' || p.first_name || ', you have an appointment tomorrow at ' || 
            TO_CHAR(NEW.appointment_time, 'HH:MI AM') || ' with Dr. ' || u.full_name,
            'appointment',
            NEW.id,
            CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        FROM patients p
        JOIN doctors d ON NEW.doctor_id = d.id
        JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
        WHERE p.id = NEW.patient_id
        AND p.consent_for_sms = TRUE;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 47: REPORTING VIEWS
-- ============================================

-- Daily OPD Report View
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW vw_daily_opd_report AS
SELECT 
    a.organization_id,
    a.branch_id,
    a.appointment_date,
    dep.name as department,
    u.full_name as doctor_name,
    d.specialization,
    COUNT(*) as total_appointments,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'completed' THEN 1 END) as completed,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status IN ('scheduled', 'checked_in') THEN 1 END) as waiting,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'no_show' THEN 1 END) as no_shows,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.status = 'cancelled' THEN 1 END) as cancelled,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.appointment_type = 'new' THEN 1 END) as new_patients,
    COUNT(CASE WHEN a.appointment_type = 'followup' THEN 1 END) as followups,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'completed' THEN a.consultation_fee ELSE 0 END) as revenue
FROM appointments a
JOIN doctors d ON a.doctor_id = d.id
JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
LEFT JOIN departments dep ON u.department_id = dep.id
GROUP BY a.organization_id, a.branch_id, a.appointment_date, dep.name, u.full_name, d.specialization;

-- Daily IPD Census View
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW vw_daily_ipd_census AS
SELECT 
    w.organization_id,
    w.branch_id,
    w.id as ward_id,
    w.name as ward_name,
    w.ward_type,
    CURRENT_DATE as census_date,
    COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.is_active) as total_beds,
    COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.status = 'occupied') as occupied,
    COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.status = 'available') as available,
    ROUND(
        COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.status = 'occupied')::NUMERIC / 
        NULLIF(COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.is_active), 0) * 100, 1
    ) as occupancy_percent,
    (
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions 
        WHERE admission_date = CURRENT_DATE 
        AND branch_id = w.branch_id
    ) as today_admissions,
    (
        SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions 
        WHERE actual_discharge_date = CURRENT_DATE 
        AND branch_id = w.branch_id
    ) as today_discharges
FROM wards w
LEFT JOIN beds b ON w.id = b.ward_id
WHERE w.is_active = TRUE
GROUP BY w.organization_id, w.branch_id, w.id, w.name, w.ward_type;

-- Revenue Summary View
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW vw_revenue_summary AS
SELECT 
    b.organization_id,
    b.branch_id,
    b.bill_date,
    b.bill_type,
    bi.category as service_category,
    COUNT(DISTINCT b.id) as bill_count,
    SUM(bi.net_amount) as gross_revenue,
    SUM(bi.discount_amount) as discount,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.is_insurance THEN bi.net_amount ELSE 0 END) as insurance_revenue,
    SUM(CASE WHEN b.is_corporate THEN bi.net_amount ELSE 0 END) as corporate_revenue,
    SUM(CASE WHEN NOT b.is_insurance AND NOT b.is_corporate THEN bi.net_amount ELSE 0 END) as cash_revenue
FROM bills b
JOIN bill_items bi ON b.id = bi.bill_id
WHERE b.is_cancelled = FALSE
GROUP BY b.organization_id, b.branch_id, b.bill_date, b.bill_type, bi.category;

-- Pending Tasks Dashboard View
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW vw_pending_tasks AS
SELECT 
    'pending_lab_orders' as task_type,
    lo.organization_id,
    lo.branch_id,
    COUNT(*) as count,
    MIN(lo.order_datetime) as oldest_since
FROM lab_orders lo
WHERE lo.status IN ('ordered', 'collected', 'received')
GROUP BY lo.organization_id, lo.branch_id

UNION ALL

SELECT 
    'pending_radiology' as task_type,
    ro.organization_id,
    ro.branch_id,
    COUNT(*) as count,
    MIN(ro.order_datetime) as oldest_since
FROM radiology_orders ro
WHERE ro.status IN ('ordered', 'scheduled')
GROUP BY ro.organization_id, ro.branch_id

UNION ALL

SELECT 
    'pending_doctor_orders' as task_type,
    do.organization_id,
    do.branch_id,
    COUNT(*) as count,
    MIN(do.order_datetime) as oldest_since
FROM doctor_orders do
WHERE do.status = 'pending'
GROUP BY do.organization_id, do.branch_id

UNION ALL

SELECT 
    'beds_under_cleaning' as task_type,
    bct.organization_id,
    bct.branch_id,
    COUNT(*) as count,
    MIN(bct.created_at) as oldest_since
FROM bed_cleaning_tasks bct
WHERE bct.status IN ('pending', 'in_progress')
GROUP BY bct.organization_id, bct.branch_id

UNION ALL

SELECT 
    'pending_discharges' as task_type,
    a.organization_id,
    a.branch_id,
    COUNT(*) as count,
    MIN(a.expected_discharge_date) as oldest_since
FROM admissions a
WHERE a.status = 'admitted'
AND a.expected_discharge_date <= CURRENT_DATE
GROUP BY a.organization_id, a.branch_id;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 48: SAMPLE DATA GENERATORS
-- ============================================

-- Function to generate sample patients
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION generate_sample_patients(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_count INTEGER DEFAULT 100
)
RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_first_names TEXT[] := ARRAY['Abdul', 'Mohammad', 'Fatima', 'Ayesha', 'Rahman', 'Karim', 'Nasreen', 'Sultana', 'Hassan', 'Hossain', 'Rahim', 'Khadija', 'Ahmed', 'Begum', 'Ali'];
    v_last_names TEXT[] := ARRAY['Islam', 'Ahmed', 'Rahman', 'Hossain', 'Khan', 'Chowdhury', 'Akter', 'Khatun', 'Miah', 'Sheikh', 'Siddiqui', 'Uddin', 'Bhuiyan', 'Sarkar', 'Das'];
    v_genders gender_type[] := ARRAY['male', 'female']::gender_type[];
    v_blood_groups blood_group_type[] := ARRAY['A+', 'A-', 'B+', 'B-', 'AB+', 'AB-', 'O+', 'O-']::blood_group_type[];
    v_counter INTEGER := 0;
    v_mrn_prefix VARCHAR;
BEGIN
    -- Get MRN prefix
    SELECT COALESCE(code, 'MRN') INTO v_mrn_prefix FROM branches WHERE id = p_branch_id;
    
    FOR i IN 1..p_count LOOP
        INSERT INTO patients (
            organization_id,
            mrn,
            first_name,
            last_name,
            gender,
            date_of_birth,
            blood_group,
            primary_phone,
            present_address_line1,
            present_city,
            present_district,
            registered_branch_id
        ) VALUES (
            p_organization_id,
            v_mrn_prefix || '-' || TO_CHAR(CURRENT_DATE, 'YYYYMM') || '-' || LPAD((100000 + i)::TEXT, 6, '0'),
            v_first_names[1 + floor(random() * array_length(v_first_names, 1))],
            v_last_names[1 + floor(random() * array_length(v_last_names, 1))],
            v_genders[1 + floor(random() * 2)],
            CURRENT_DATE - (floor(random() * 25000 + 365)::INTEGER),
            v_blood_groups[1 + floor(random() * array_length(v_blood_groups, 1))],
            '01' || (3 + floor(random() * 7))::TEXT || (10000000 + floor(random() * 90000000))::TEXT,
            'House ' || (1 + floor(random() * 500))::TEXT || ', Road ' || (1 + floor(random() * 50))::TEXT,
            'Dhaka',
            'Dhaka',
            p_branch_id
        );
        
        v_counter := v_counter + 1;
    END LOOP;
    
    RETURN v_counter;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 49: PERFORMANCE INDEXES
-- ============================================

-- Composite indexes for common query patterns
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_appointments_lookup 
ON appointments(organization_id, doctor_id, appointment_date, status);

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_admissions_active 
ON admissions(organization_id, branch_id, status) 
WHERE status = 'admitted';

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_bills_unpaid 
ON bills(organization_id, patient_id, payment_status) 
WHERE payment_status IN ('pending', 'partial');

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_lab_orders_pending 
ON lab_orders(organization_id, branch_id, status, order_datetime) 
WHERE status IN ('ordered', 'collected', 'received', 'processing');

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_medicine_low_stock 
ON medicine_inventory(pharmacy_id, drug_id) 
WHERE quantity_available <= reorder_level;

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_maintenance_open 
ON maintenance_tickets(organization_id, status, priority) 
WHERE status IN ('open', 'assigned', 'in_progress');

-- Partial indexes for active records
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_patients_active 
ON patients(organization_id, mrn) 
WHERE is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_users_active 
ON users(organization_id, username) 
WHERE is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_beds_available 
ON beds(ward_id, status) 
WHERE status = 'available' AND is_active = TRUE;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 50: FINAL CLEANUP & OPTIMIZATION
-- ============================================

-- Analyze all tables for query planner
ANALYZE;

-- Create statistics for frequently joined columns
CREATE STATISTICS IF NOT EXISTS stat_patient_org ON organization_id, mrn FROM patients;
CREATE STATISTICS IF NOT EXISTS stat_admission_patient ON organization_id, patient_id, status FROM admissions;
CREATE STATISTICS IF NOT EXISTS stat_appointment_doctor ON organization_id, doctor_id, appointment_date FROM appointments;

-- Function to vacuum and analyze specific schema
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION maintenance_vacuum_analyze()
RETURNS VOID AS $$
DECLARE
    r RECORD;
BEGIN
    FOR r IN SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables WHERE schemaname = 'public'
    LOOP
        EXECUTE 'VACUUM ANALYZE ' || quote_ident(r.tablename);
    END LOOP;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Schedule maintenance (requires pg_cron)
-- SELECT cron.schedule('nightly-vacuum', '0 2 * * *', 'SELECT maintenance_vacuum_analyze()');

-- ============================================
-- SCHEMA COMPLETION SUMMARY
-- ============================================

DO $$
DECLARE
    v_tables INTEGER;
    v_views INTEGER;
    v_indexes INTEGER;
    v_functions INTEGER;
    v_triggers INTEGER;
    v_types INTEGER;
BEGIN
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_tables 
    FROM information_schema.tables 
    WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE';
    
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_views 
    FROM information_schema.views 
    WHERE table_schema = 'public';
    
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_indexes 
    FROM pg_indexes 
    WHERE schemaname = 'public';
    
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_functions 
    FROM information_schema.routines 
    WHERE routine_schema = 'public';
    
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_triggers 
    FROM information_schema.triggers 
    WHERE trigger_schema = 'public';
    
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_types 
    FROM pg_type t
    JOIN pg_namespace n ON t.typnamespace = n.oid
    WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND t.typtype = 'e';

    RAISE NOTICE '';
    RAISE NOTICE '╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗';
    RAISE NOTICE '║     HOSPITAL ERP POSTGRESQL SCHEMA - COMPLETE                ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  Tables Created:        %                                    ║', LPAD(v_tables::TEXT, 4);
    RAISE NOTICE '║  Views Created:         %                                    ║', LPAD(v_views::TEXT, 4);
    RAISE NOTICE '║  Indexes Created:       %                                    ║', LPAD(v_indexes::TEXT, 4);
    RAISE NOTICE '║  Functions Created:     %                                    ║', LPAD(v_functions::TEXT, 4);
    RAISE NOTICE '║  Triggers Created:      %                                    ║', LPAD(v_triggers::TEXT, 4);
    RAISE NOTICE '║  Custom Types Created:  %                                    ║', LPAD(v_types::TEXT, 4);
    RAISE NOTICE '╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  MODULES COVERED:                                            ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ Multi-Tenancy & Access Control                           ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ Patient Management                                        ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ OPD/Appointments                                          ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ IPD/Admissions                                            ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ EMR/Clinical Documentation                                ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ Nurse Station                                             ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ Bed Management                                            ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ OT Management                                             ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ Diagnostics (Lab & Radiology)                             ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ Pharmacy                                                  ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ Inventory & Supply Chain                                  ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ Insurance & Claims                                        ║';
    RAISE NOTICE '║  ✓ Billing & Payments                                        ║';

END;
$$;
```

```
-- Pre-deployment checks
SELECT 'Extensions' as check_type, 
       CASE WHEN COUNT(*) >= 4 THEN '✅ OK' ELSE '❌ Missing' END as status
FROM pg_extension 
WHERE extname IN ('uuid-ossp', 'pgcrypto', 'pg_trgm', 'btree_gist');

-- Post-deployment verification
SELECT 
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE') as tables,
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname = 'public') as indexes,
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.routines WHERE routine_schema = 'public') as functions;
```
