## 📁 PART 20: HEALTHCARE INTEGRATIONS & STANDARDS

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 51: HL7/FHIR INTEGRATION TABLES
-- ============================================

-- HL7 Message Log
CREATE TABLE hl7_message_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    message_type VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL, -- ADT, ORM, ORU, RDE, MDM, etc.
    message_event VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL, -- A01, A02, A03, O01, R01, etc.
    message_control_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    direction VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL, -- inbound, outbound
    
    -- Endpoints
    sending_application VARCHAR(100),
    sending_facility VARCHAR(100),
    receiving_application VARCHAR(100),
    receiving_facility VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Content
    raw_message TEXT NOT NULL,
    parsed_message JSONB,
    
    -- Processing
    processing_status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, processed, failed, acknowledged
    processing_error TEXT,
    processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Acknowledgment
    ack_code VARCHAR(10), -- AA, AE, AR
    ack_message TEXT,
    ack_sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Reference
    reference_type VARCHAR(50), -- patient, admission, lab_order, etc.
    reference_id UUID,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, message_control_id)
);

-- FHIR Resource Cache
CREATE TABLE fhir_resources (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    resource_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- Patient, Encounter, Observation, etc.
    resource_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    version_id INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    
    -- Content
    resource_json JSONB NOT NULL,
    
    -- Reference to internal record
    internal_table VARCHAR(100),
    internal_id UUID,
    
    -- Metadata
    last_updated TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    is_deleted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, resource_type, resource_id)
);

-- External System Mappings
CREATE TABLE external_system_mappings (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    external_system VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, -- lab_lis, pacs, pharmacy_system, insurance_portal
    
    internal_table VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    internal_id UUID NOT NULL,
    internal_code VARCHAR(100),
    
    external_id VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    external_code VARCHAR(100),
    
    mapping_type VARCHAR(50), -- patient, test, drug, doctor, etc.
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_synced_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, external_system, internal_table, internal_id)
);

-- Integration Endpoints Configuration
CREATE TABLE integration_endpoints (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    endpoint_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    endpoint_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- hl7_mllp, hl7_http, fhir, rest_api, soap, sftp
    
    -- Connection
    host VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    port INTEGER,
    path VARCHAR(500),
    
    -- Authentication
    auth_type VARCHAR(50), -- none, basic, bearer, oauth2, certificate
    auth_credentials JSONB, -- Encrypted credentials
    
    -- SSL/TLS
    use_ssl BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    ssl_certificate TEXT,
    
    -- Message Configuration
    message_types TEXT[], -- Which message types to send/receive
    
    -- Retry Configuration
    retry_count INTEGER DEFAULT 3,
    retry_interval_seconds INTEGER DEFAULT 60,
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_connection_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_connection_status VARCHAR(50),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, endpoint_name)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 52: CLINICAL DECISION SUPPORT
-- ============================================

-- Clinical Rules Engine
CREATE TABLE clinical_rules (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id), -- NULL for global rules
    
    rule_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    rule_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    rule_category VARCHAR(50), -- drug_interaction, allergy, dosing, lab_alert, clinical_pathway
    
    -- Rule Definition
    trigger_event VARCHAR(100), -- on_prescription, on_lab_result, on_admission, etc.
    
    condition_type VARCHAR(50), -- simple, complex, ml_model
    condition_expression TEXT, -- SQL or JSON expression
    condition_parameters JSONB,
    
    -- Action
    action_type VARCHAR(50), -- alert, block, suggest, auto_order, notify
    action_severity VARCHAR(20), -- info, warning, critical
    action_message TEXT,
    action_message_bangla TEXT,
    action_parameters JSONB,
    
    -- Override
    can_override BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    override_requires_reason BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    override_requires_approval BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Validity
    effective_from DATE,
    effective_to DATE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(COALESCE(organization_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), rule_code)
);

-- Clinical Rule Execution Log
CREATE TABLE clinical_rule_executions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    rule_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical_rules(id),
    
    -- Context
    patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    trigger_event VARCHAR(100),
    trigger_data JSONB,
    
    -- Execution
    executed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    rule_matched BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    action_taken VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Override
    was_overridden BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    override_reason TEXT,
    overridden_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Drug Formulary
CREATE TABLE drug_formulary (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES drug_master(id),
    
    formulary_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- preferred, allowed, restricted, non_formulary
    
    -- Restrictions
    restriction_type VARCHAR(50), -- none, prior_auth, specialist_only, specific_indication
    restriction_criteria TEXT,
    
    -- Therapeutic Alternatives
    therapeutic_alternatives UUID[], -- Array of alternative drug IDs
    
    -- Cost
    acquisition_cost DECIMAL(10, 2),
    is_high_cost BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Monitoring
    requires_monitoring BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    monitoring_parameters TEXT,
    
    effective_from DATE,
    effective_to DATE,
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, drug_id)
);

-- Clinical Protocols/Pathways
CREATE TABLE clinical_pathways (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    pathway_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    -- Applicability
    diagnosis_codes VARCHAR(20)[], -- ICD codes
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    -- Pathway Definition
    pathway_steps JSONB NOT NULL, 
    /* Structure:
    [
        {
            "day": 1,
            "step": "Admission Assessment",
            "orders": [
                {"type": "lab", "tests": ["CBC", "RFT", "LFT"]},
                {"type": "medication", "drug": "...", "dose": "..."}
            ],
            "assessments": ["nursing_assessment", "nutrition_assessment"],
            "expected_outcomes": ["..."]
        }
    ]
    */
    
    expected_los_days INTEGER,
    
    -- Version Control
    version VARCHAR(20),
    previous_version_id UUID REFERENCES clinical_pathways(id),
    
    -- Approval
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft, review, approved, retired
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, pathway_code, version)
);

-- Patient Pathway Enrollment
CREATE TABLE patient_pathway_enrollments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    pathway_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clinical_pathways(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    enrolled_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    enrolled_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    current_day INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    current_step INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    
    -- Progress
    progress_data JSONB, -- Tracks completion of each step
    
    -- Variance
    variances JSONB, -- Records deviations from pathway
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active', -- active, completed, discontinued, deviated
    
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    discontinued_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    discontinuation_reason TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 53: QUALITY INDICATORS & METRICS
-- ============================================

-- Quality Indicators Definition
CREATE TABLE quality_indicators (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id), -- NULL for standard indicators
    
    indicator_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    category VARCHAR(50), -- patient_safety, clinical_outcome, process, patient_experience
    sub_category VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Measurement
    numerator_definition TEXT,
    denominator_definition TEXT,
    exclusion_criteria TEXT,
    
    data_source VARCHAR(100),
    calculation_query TEXT,
    
    -- Target
    target_value DECIMAL(10, 4),
    target_direction VARCHAR(20), -- higher_better, lower_better, target_range
    target_min DECIMAL(10, 4),
    target_max DECIMAL(10, 4),
    
    -- Unit
    unit VARCHAR(50), -- percentage, rate_per_1000, count, days
    
    -- Frequency
    measurement_frequency VARCHAR(20), -- daily, weekly, monthly, quarterly
    
    -- Standards
    nabh_indicator BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    jci_indicator BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    who_indicator BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(COALESCE(organization_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), indicator_code)
);

-- Quality Indicator Results
CREATE TABLE quality_indicator_results (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    indicator_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES quality_indicators(id),
    
    measurement_period_start DATE NOT NULL,
    measurement_period_end DATE NOT NULL,
    
    numerator_value DECIMAL(12, 2),
    denominator_value DECIMAL(12, 2),
    result_value DECIMAL(10, 4) NOT NULL,
    
    target_value DECIMAL(10, 4),
    
    -- Status
    meets_target BOOLEAN,
    variance_from_target DECIMAL(10, 4),
    trend VARCHAR(20), -- improving, stable, declining
    
    -- Analysis
    root_cause_analysis TEXT,
    action_plan TEXT,
    
    calculated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    calculated_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Review
    reviewed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    review_comments TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, branch_id, indicator_id, measurement_period_start)
);

-- Infection Control Surveillance
CREATE TABLE infection_surveillance (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    surveillance_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- Infection Details
    infection_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, 
    -- HAI, SSI, CAUTI, CLABSI, VAP, C_DIFF, MRSA, etc.
    
    infection_site VARCHAR(100),
    
    onset_date DATE NOT NULL,
    onset_type VARCHAR(20), -- community, hospital_acquired
    
    -- If hospital acquired
    days_post_admission INTEGER,
    
    -- Organism
    organism_name VARCHAR(255),
    is_mdro BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE, -- Multi-drug resistant organism
    
    -- Source/Risk Factors
    risk_factors JSONB,
    -- {"central_line": true, "urinary_catheter": true, "ventilator": false}
    
    device_related BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    device_type VARCHAR(100),
    device_days INTEGER,
    
    -- Surgery Related
    surgery_id UUID REFERENCES surgeries(id),
    surgery_class VARCHAR(50), -- clean, clean_contaminated, contaminated, dirty
    
    -- Treatment
    treatment_started DATE,
    treatment_antibiotics JSONB,
    
    -- Outcome
    outcome VARCHAR(50), -- resolved, ongoing, death_related, death_unrelated
    outcome_date DATE,
    
    -- Investigation
    investigated_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    investigation_date DATE,
    investigation_findings TEXT,
    
    -- Prevention Analysis
    preventable BOOLEAN,
    prevention_measures TEXT,
    
    -- Reporting
    reported_to_authority BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    reporting_date DATE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, surveillance_number)
);

-- Incident Reporting (Patient Safety)
CREATE TABLE incident_reports (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    incident_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    -- When/Where
    incident_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    location VARCHAR(255),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    ward_id UUID REFERENCES wards(id),
    
    -- Who
    patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    visitor_involved BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    staff_involved BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    involved_staff_ids UUID[],
    
    -- What
    incident_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    -- medication_error, fall, pressure_injury, wrong_site, near_miss, 
    -- equipment_failure, security, violence, other
    
    incident_category VARCHAR(50), -- clinical, non_clinical
    
    description TEXT NOT NULL,
    
    -- Severity
    severity_actual VARCHAR(20), -- none, mild, moderate, severe, death
    severity_potential VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Harm
    harm_occurred BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    harm_description TEXT,
    
    -- Immediate Actions
    immediate_actions TEXT,
    
    -- Reporter
    reported_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    reported_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    reporter_role VARCHAR(100),
    is_anonymous BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Investigation
    investigation_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    investigation_status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, in_progress, completed
    
    assigned_investigator_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    root_cause_analysis TEXT,
    contributing_factors JSONB,
    
    -- Actions
    corrective_actions TEXT,
    preventive_actions TEXT,
    actions_due_date DATE,
    actions_completed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    actions_completed_date DATE,
    
    -- Review
    reviewed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    review_comments TEXT,
    
    -- Closure
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'open', -- open, investigating, action_pending, closed
    closed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    closed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Disclosure
    patient_notified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    notification_date DATE,
    
    -- External Reporting
    reportable_externally BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    external_report_date DATE,
    external_report_reference VARCHAR(100),
    
    attachments TEXT[],
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, incident_number)
);

-- Mortality Review
CREATE TABLE mortality_reviews (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    death_record_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_death_records(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- Review Details
    review_date DATE NOT NULL,
    review_committee TEXT,
    
    -- Classification
    death_expected BOOLEAN,
    death_category VARCHAR(50), -- natural, accidental, suicide, homicide, undetermined
    
    -- Care Analysis
    care_appropriate BOOLEAN,
    care_issues_identified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    care_issues_description TEXT,
    
    -- Preventability
    potentially_preventable BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    preventability_score INTEGER, -- 1-6 scale
    
    -- Contributing Factors
    contributing_factors JSONB,
    system_issues TEXT,
    
    -- Recommendations
    recommendations TEXT,
    action_items JSONB,
    
    -- Quality Metrics
    early_warning_score_documented BOOLEAN,
    escalation_appropriate BOOLEAN,
    palliative_care_involved BOOLEAN,
    
    -- Reviewers
    primary_reviewer_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    reviewed_by_committee BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    committee_date DATE,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, reviewed, closed
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 54: DOCUMENT MANAGEMENT SYSTEM
-- ============================================

-- Document Categories
CREATE TABLE document_categories (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    category_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    parent_category_id UUID REFERENCES document_categories(id),
    
    -- Retention
    retention_period_years INTEGER,
    
    -- Access
    default_access_level VARCHAR(20), -- public, internal, confidential, restricted
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(COALESCE(organization_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), category_code)
);

-- Documents
CREATE TABLE documents (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    document_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    category_id UUID REFERENCES document_categories(id),
    
    title VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    -- File
    file_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_size BIGINT,
    mime_type VARCHAR(100),
    file_hash VARCHAR(64), -- SHA-256 for integrity
    
    -- Version Control
    version INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    parent_document_id UUID REFERENCES documents(id),
    is_latest_version BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Classification
    access_level VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'internal',
    
    -- Tags
    tags TEXT[],
    
    -- Dates
    document_date DATE,
    expiry_date DATE,
    
    -- Review
    requires_review BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    next_review_date DATE,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active', -- draft, active, archived, deleted
    
    -- Ownership
    owner_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    -- Audit
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, document_number)
);

-- Document Access Log
CREATE TABLE document_access_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    document_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES documents(id),
    
    accessed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    accessed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    access_type VARCHAR(50), -- view, download, print, edit
    
    ip_address INET,
    user_agent TEXT
);

-- Document Workflows
CREATE TABLE document_workflows (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    workflow_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Steps
    workflow_steps JSONB NOT NULL,
    /*
    [
        {"step": 1, "name": "Draft", "role": "creator", "action": "create"},
        {"step": 2, "name": "Review", "role": "reviewer", "action": "approve_reject"},
        {"step": 3, "name": "Approve", "role": "approver", "action": "approve_reject"},
        {"step": 4, "name": "Publish", "role": "publisher", "action": "publish"}
    ]
    */
    
    applicable_categories UUID[],
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, workflow_code)
);

-- Document Workflow Instances
CREATE TABLE document_workflow_instances (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    document_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES documents(id),
    workflow_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES document_workflows(id),
    
    current_step INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'in_progress', -- in_progress, completed, rejected, cancelled
    
    -- Step History
    step_history JSONB,
    
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    started_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 55: ADVANCED REPORTING
-- ============================================

-- Report Schedules
CREATE TABLE report_schedules (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    schedule_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    report_definition_id UUID REFERENCES report_definitions(id),
    
    -- Custom Query (if not using report_definition)
    custom_query TEXT,
    
    -- Parameters
    parameters JSONB,
    
    -- Schedule (cron expression)
    schedule_cron VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, -- "0 8 * * *" for daily at 8 AM
    timezone VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'Asia/Dhaka',
    
    -- Output
    output_format VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pdf', -- pdf, excel, csv
    
    -- Delivery
    delivery_method VARCHAR(50), -- email, sftp, storage
    
    email_recipients TEXT[],
    email_subject VARCHAR(500),
    email_body TEXT,
    
    sftp_endpoint_id UUID REFERENCES integration_endpoints(id),
    sftp_path VARCHAR(500),
    
    storage_path VARCHAR(500),
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    last_run_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_run_status VARCHAR(20),
    last_run_error TEXT,
    
    next_run_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Report Execution History
CREATE TABLE report_execution_history (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    report_definition_id UUID REFERENCES report_definitions(id),
    schedule_id UUID REFERENCES report_schedules(id),
    
    execution_type VARCHAR(20), -- scheduled, manual, api
    
    -- Parameters Used
    parameters JSONB,
    
    -- Execution
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    duration_seconds INTEGER,
    
    status VARCHAR(20), -- running, completed, failed
    error_message TEXT,
    
    -- Output
    output_format VARCHAR(20),
    output_file_path TEXT,
    output_file_size BIGINT,
    row_count INTEGER,
    
    -- Delivery
    delivery_status VARCHAR(20),
    delivered_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    delivery_error TEXT,
    
    executed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Dashboard Configurations
CREATE TABLE dashboard_configurations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    dashboard_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    -- Layout
    layout_config JSONB NOT NULL,
    /*
    {
        "columns": 12,
        "rows": 8,
        "widgets": [
            {
                "widget_id": "...",
                "position": {"x": 0, "y": 0, "w": 4, "h": 2},
                "config": {...}
            }
        ]
    }
    */
    
    -- Filters
    global_filters JSONB,
    
    -- Refresh
    auto_refresh BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    refresh_interval_seconds INTEGER DEFAULT 300,
    
    -- Access
    is_public BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    allowed_roles UUID[],
    
    is_default BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, dashboard_code)
);

-- KPI Definitions
CREATE TABLE kpi_definitions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    kpi_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    category VARCHAR(50), -- financial, operational, clinical, hr
    
    -- Calculation
    calculation_type VARCHAR(50), -- count, sum, average, percentage, ratio, formula
    calculation_query TEXT,
    calculation_formula TEXT,
    
    -- Data Source
    source_tables TEXT[],
    
    -- Unit
    unit VARCHAR(50),
    decimal_places INTEGER DEFAULT 2,
    
    -- Target
    target_type VARCHAR(20), -- fixed, dynamic, benchmark
    target_value DECIMAL(15, 4),
    target_query TEXT, -- For dynamic targets
    
    -- Thresholds
    threshold_good DECIMAL(15, 4),
    threshold_warning DECIMAL(15, 4),
    threshold_critical DECIMAL(15, 4),
    
    -- Trend
    trend_direction VARCHAR(20), -- higher_better, lower_better
    
    -- Frequency
    calculation_frequency VARCHAR(20), -- realtime, hourly, daily, weekly, monthly
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(COALESCE(organization_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), kpi_code)
);

-- KPI Values Cache
CREATE TABLE kpi_values (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    kpi_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kpi_definitions(id),
    
    period_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- hour, day, week, month, quarter, year
    period_start TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    period_end TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    
    value DECIMAL(15, 4) NOT NULL,
    target_value DECIMAL(15, 4),
    
    previous_value DECIMAL(15, 4),
    change_percent DECIMAL(8, 2),
    
    status VARCHAR(20), -- good, warning, critical
    
    calculated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, branch_id, kpi_id, period_type, period_start)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 56: WORKFLOW ENGINE
-- ============================================

-- Workflow Definitions
CREATE TABLE workflow_definitions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    workflow_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    -- Trigger
    trigger_type VARCHAR(50), -- manual, event, schedule
    trigger_event VARCHAR(100), -- on_admission, on_discharge, on_lab_result, etc.
    trigger_condition JSONB,
    
    -- States
    states JSONB NOT NULL,
    /*
    [
        {"state": "submitted", "type": "initial"},
        {"state": "under_review", "type": "intermediate"},
        {"state": "approved", "type": "final"},
        {"state": "rejected", "type": "final"}
    ]
    */
    
    -- Transitions
    transitions JSONB NOT NULL,
    /*
    [
        {
            "from": "submitted",
            "to": "under_review",
            "action": "review",
            "allowed_roles": ["reviewer"],
            "conditions": [],
            "actions": [{"type": "notify", "to": "approver"}]
        }
    ]
    */
    
    -- Timeout/Escalation
    sla_config JSONB,
    
    version INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, workflow_code, version)
);

-- Workflow Instances
CREATE TABLE workflow_instances (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    workflow_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES workflow_definitions(id),
    
    instance_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Reference
    reference_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, -- leave_application, purchase_requisition, etc.
    reference_id UUID NOT NULL,
    
    -- Current State
    current_state VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Data
    workflow_data JSONB,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active', -- active, completed, cancelled, suspended
    
    -- Timing
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    started_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- SLA
    sla_due_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    sla_breached BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, instance_number)
);

-- Workflow Tasks
CREATE TABLE workflow_tasks (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    workflow_instance_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES workflow_instances(id),
    
    task_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    state VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    action_required VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Assignment
    assigned_to UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    assigned_role UUID REFERENCES roles(id),
    assigned_department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, in_progress, completed, skipped
    
    -- Action Taken
    action_taken VARCHAR(100),
    action_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    action_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    action_comments TEXT,
    
    -- Timing
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    due_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- Workflow History
CREATE TABLE workflow_history (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    workflow_instance_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES workflow_instances(id),
    
    from_state VARCHAR(100),
    to_state VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    action VARCHAR(100),
    
    performed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    performed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    comments TEXT,
    
    task_id UUID REFERENCES workflow_tasks(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 57: LOCALIZATION & CONFIGURATION
-- ============================================

-- System Configuration
CREATE TABLE system_configuration (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id), -- NULL for global config
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id), -- NULL for org-level config
    
    config_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    config_value TEXT,
    config_value_json JSONB,
    
    data_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'string', -- string, integer, decimal, boolean, json
    
    category VARCHAR(100),
    description TEXT,
    
    is_encrypted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_readonly BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    default_value TEXT,
    
    updated_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(COALESCE(organization_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), 
           COALESCE(branch_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), 
           config_key)
);

-- Translations
CREATE TABLE translations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    
    language_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL, -- en, bn, ar, etc.
    
    translation_key VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    translation_value TEXT NOT NULL,
    
    context VARCHAR(100), -- ui, report, notification, clinical
    module VARCHAR(100),
    
    is_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(language_code, translation_key)
);

-- Number Sequences
CREATE TABLE number_sequences (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    sequence_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    prefix VARCHAR(20),
    suffix VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Format
    number_format VARCHAR(100), -- {PREFIX}-{YYYY}{MM}-{#####}
    
    current_number BIGINT DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Reset
    reset_frequency VARCHAR(20), -- never, daily, monthly, yearly
    last_reset_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Range
    min_value BIGINT DEFAULT 1,
    max_value BIGINT,
    
    padding_length INTEGER DEFAULT 5,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, COALESCE(branch_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), sequence_name)
);

-- Function to get next sequence number
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_next_sequence_number(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID,
    p_sequence_name VARCHAR
)
RETURNS VARCHAR AS $$
DECLARE
    v_seq RECORD;
    v_next_number BIGINT;
    v_result VARCHAR;
BEGIN
    -- Lock and get sequence
    SELECT * INTO v_seq
    FROM number_sequences
    WHERE organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND COALESCE(branch_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID) = 
        COALESCE(p_branch_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID)
    AND sequence_name = p_sequence_name
    AND is_active = TRUE
    FOR UPDATE;
    
    IF v_seq IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sequence not found: %', p_sequence_name;
    END IF;
    
    -- Check if reset needed
    IF v_seq.reset_frequency = 'daily' AND v_seq.last_reset_at::DATE < CURRENT_DATE THEN
        v_next_number := v_seq.min_value;
    ELSIF v_seq.reset_frequency = 'monthly' AND DATE_TRUNC('month', v_seq.last_reset_at) < DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) THEN
        v_next_number := v_seq.min_value;
    ELSIF v_seq.reset_frequency = 'yearly' AND DATE_TRUNC('year', v_seq.last_reset_at) < DATE_TRUNC('year', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) THEN
        v_next_number := v_seq.min_value;
    ELSE
        v_next_number := v_seq.current_number + 1;
    END IF;
    
    -- Check max value
    IF v_seq.max_value IS NOT NULL AND v_next_number > v_seq.max_value THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Sequence % has reached maximum value', p_sequence_name;
    END IF;
    
    -- Update sequence
    UPDATE number_sequences
    SET current_number = v_next_number,
        last_reset_at = CASE 
            WHEN v_next_number = v_seq.min_value THEN CURRENT_TIMESTAMP 
            ELSE last_reset_at 
        END,
        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    WHERE id = v_seq.id;
    
    -- Format result
    v_result := v_seq.number_format;
    v_result := REPLACE(v_result, '{PREFIX}', COALESCE(v_seq.prefix, ''));
    v_result := REPLACE(v_result, '{SUFFIX}', COALESCE(v_seq.suffix, ''));
    v_result := REPLACE(v_result, '{YYYY}', TO_CHAR(CURRENT_DATE, 'YYYY'));
    v_result := REPLACE(v_result, '{YY}', TO_CHAR(CURRENT_DATE, 'YY'));
    v_result := REPLACE(v_result, '{MM}', TO_CHAR(CURRENT_DATE, 'MM'));
    v_result := REPLACE(v_result, '{DD}', TO_CHAR(CURRENT_DATE, 'DD'));
    v_result := REPLACE(v_result, '{#####}', LPAD(v_next_number::TEXT, v_seq.padding_length, '0'));
    v_result := REPLACE(v_result, '{####}', LPAD(v_next_number::TEXT, 4, '0'));
    v_result := REPLACE(v_result, '{###}', LPAD(v_next_number::TEXT, 3, '0'));
    
    RETURN v_result;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 58: ADVANCED SECURITY
-- ============================================

-- API Keys
CREATE TABLE api_keys (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    key_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    api_key_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, -- Store hashed key
    api_key_prefix VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL, -- First 8 chars for identification
    
    -- Permissions
    permissions JSONB, -- Specific API permissions
    allowed_ips INET[],
    
    -- Rate Limiting
    rate_limit_per_minute INTEGER DEFAULT 60,
    rate_limit_per_day INTEGER DEFAULT 10000,
    
    -- Validity
    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_used_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, key_name)
);

-- API Request Log
CREATE TABLE api_request_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    
    api_key_id UUID REFERENCES api_keys(id),
    user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    request_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    method VARCHAR(10),
    endpoint VARCHAR(500),
    
    request_headers JSONB,
    request_body JSONB,
    
    response_status INTEGER,
    response_time_ms INTEGER,
    
    ip_address INET,
    user_agent TEXT,
    
    error_message TEXT
) PARTITION BY RANGE (request_timestamp);

-- Create partitions for API log
CREATE TABLE api_request_log_default PARTITION OF api_request_log DEFAULT;

-- Security Events
CREATE TABLE security_events (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    event_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    -- login_success, login_failed, password_changed, role_changed,
    -- permission_denied, suspicious_activity, brute_force_detected
    
    severity VARCHAR(20), -- info, warning, critical
    
    user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    username VARCHAR(100),
    
    ip_address INET,
    user_agent TEXT,
    location_info JSONB,
    
    details JSONB,
    
    -- Response
    blocked BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    block_duration_minutes INTEGER,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Password History
CREATE TABLE password_history (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Function to check password history
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_password_history(
    p_user_id UUID,
    p_new_password_hash VARCHAR,
    p_history_count INTEGER DEFAULT 5
)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
DECLARE
    v_exists BOOLEAN;
BEGIN
    SELECT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM password_history
        WHERE user_id = p_user_id
        AND password_hash = p_new_password_hash
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
        LIMIT p_history_count
    ) INTO v_exists;
    
    RETURN NOT v_exists;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Data Masking Rules
CREATE TABLE data_masking_rules (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    table_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    column_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    masking_type VARCHAR(50), -- full, partial, hash, custom
    
    -- Partial masking config
    visible_start INTEGER,
    visible_end INTEGER,
    mask_character CHAR(1) DEFAULT '*',
    
    -- Custom masking
    custom_function VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Access
    exempt_roles UUID[],
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    UNIQUE(COALESCE(organization_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), table_name, column_name)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 60: FINAL STATISTICS & COMPLETION
-- ============================================

-- Comprehensive schema statistics
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_schema_statistics()
RETURNS TABLE(
    category VARCHAR,
    count INTEGER
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 'Tables'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM information_schema.tables 
    WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 'Views'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM information_schema.views 
    WHERE table_schema = 'public'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 'Materialized Views'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM pg_matviews 
    WHERE schemaname = 'public'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 'Indexes'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM pg_indexes 
    WHERE schemaname = 'public'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 'Functions'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM information_schema.routines 
    WHERE routine_schema = 'public' AND routine_type = 'FUNCTION'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 'Procedures'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM information_schema.routines 
    WHERE routine_schema = 'public' AND routine_type = 'PROCEDURE'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 'Triggers'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM information_schema.triggers 
    WHERE trigger_schema = 'public'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 'Custom Types (Enums)'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM pg_type t
    JOIN pg_namespace n ON t.typnamespace = n.oid
    WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND t.typtype = 'e'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 'Foreign Keys'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM information_schema.table_constraints 
    WHERE constraint_schema = 'public' AND constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 'Check Constraints'::VARCHAR, COUNT(*)::INTEGER 
    FROM information_schema.table_constraints 
    WHERE constraint_schema = 'public' AND constraint_type = 'CHECK';
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Display final statistics
SELECT * FROM get_schema_statistics();
```

## 🚀 DEPLOYMENT SCRIPT

SQL

```
-- Master deployment script
-- Run this to deploy the complete schema

-- Step 1: Create extensions
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pg_trgm";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "btree_gist";

-- Step 2: Run all schema parts in order
-- \i part_01_foundation.sql
-- \i part_02_patient.sql
-- \i part_03_visits.sql
-- ... etc.

-- Step 3: Verify deployment
SELECT * FROM get_schema_statistics();

-- Step 4: Create initial admin user
-- (Run from application or separate secure script)

-- Step 5: Initialize sequences
INSERT INTO number_sequences (organization_id, sequence_name, prefix, number_format, padding_length)
SELECT 
    o.id,
    seq.name,
    seq.prefix,
    seq.format,
    seq.padding
FROM organizations o
CROSS JOIN (VALUES
    ('MRN', 'MRN', '{PREFIX}-{YYYY}{MM}-{#####}', 5),
    ('ADMISSION', 'ADM', '{PREFIX}-{YYYY}{MM}-{####}', 4),
    ('BILL', 'BIL', '{PREFIX}-{YYYY}{MM}{DD}-{####}', 4),
    ('PRESCRIPTION', 'RX', '{PREFIX}-{YYYY}{MM}{DD}-{####}', 4),
    ('LAB_ORDER', 'LAB', '{PREFIX}-{YYYY}{MM}{DD}-{####}', 4)
) AS seq(name, prefix, format, padding);

-- Step 6: Run analyze
ANALYZE;

RAISE NOTICE 'Hospital ERP Schema deployed successfully!';
```

## 📁 PART 21: MOBILE & PORTAL SUPPORT

SQL

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 61: MOBILE APP SUPPORT
-- ============================================

-- Mobile Device Registration
CREATE TABLE mobile_devices (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    device_id VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, -- Unique device identifier
    device_token VARCHAR(500), -- FCM/APNs token
    
    device_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- ios, android, web
    device_model VARCHAR(100),
    os_version VARCHAR(50),
    app_version VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Push Notifications
    push_enabled BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    push_token_updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_active_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Security
    is_trusted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    trusted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    trusted_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(user_id, device_id)
);

-- Mobile App Versions
CREATE TABLE mobile_app_versions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    
    platform VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- ios, android
    version_code INTEGER NOT NULL,
    version_name VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    
    release_date DATE,
    
    -- Requirements
    min_os_version VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Update Policy
    is_mandatory BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    force_update_message TEXT,
    
    -- Features
    release_notes TEXT,
    new_features JSONB,
    
    -- URLs
    download_url TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(platform, version_code)
);

-- Mobile Sync Queue
CREATE TABLE mobile_sync_queue (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    device_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES mobile_devices(id),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    sync_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- full, incremental, selective
    
    -- Data to sync
    entity_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL, -- patients, appointments, prescriptions
    entity_id UUID,
    operation VARCHAR(20), -- create, update, delete
    
    payload JSONB,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, synced, failed, conflict
    
    -- Timestamps
    queued_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    synced_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    retry_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    last_error TEXT,
    
    -- Conflict Resolution
    has_conflict BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    conflict_data JSONB,
    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- Offline Data Cache
CREATE TABLE offline_data_cache (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    device_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES mobile_devices(id),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    
    cache_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    entity_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    data JSONB NOT NULL,
    data_hash VARCHAR(64), -- For change detection
    
    cached_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    last_accessed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    UNIQUE(device_id, cache_key)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 62: PATIENT PORTAL
-- ============================================

-- Patient Portal Users
CREATE TABLE patient_portal_users (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(255),
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    
    password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Verification
    email_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    email_verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    phone_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    phone_verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Security
    two_factor_enabled BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    two_factor_method VARCHAR(20), -- sms, email, authenticator
    two_factor_secret VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_locked BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    locked_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    failed_login_attempts INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Activity
    last_login_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_login_ip INET,
    
    -- Terms
    terms_accepted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    terms_accepted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    terms_version VARCHAR(20),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, username)
);

-- Portal Sessions
CREATE TABLE patient_portal_sessions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    portal_user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_portal_users(id),
    
    session_token VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    refresh_token VARCHAR(500),
    
    device_info JSONB,
    ip_address INET,
    user_agent TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    last_activity_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Patient Portal Preferences
CREATE TABLE patient_portal_preferences (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    portal_user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_portal_users(id),
    
    -- Notification Preferences
    notify_appointment_reminder BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    notify_lab_results BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    notify_prescription_ready BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    notify_bill_generated BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    notify_promotional BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Communication Channels
    preferred_channel VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'email', -- email, sms, push
    
    -- Language
    preferred_language VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'en',
    
    -- Display
    date_format VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'DD/MM/YYYY',
    time_format VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT '12h',
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(portal_user_id)
);

-- Patient Messages (Secure Messaging)
CREATE TABLE patient_messages (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    thread_id UUID, -- For conversation threading
    parent_message_id UUID REFERENCES patient_messages(id),
    
    -- Participants
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    sender_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- patient, staff
    sender_patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    sender_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    recipient_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    recipient_patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    recipient_user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    recipient_department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    -- Message
    subject VARCHAR(500),
    body TEXT NOT NULL,
    
    -- Attachments
    attachments JSONB, -- [{file_name, file_url, file_size, mime_type}]
    
    -- Category
    category VARCHAR(50), -- general, appointment, prescription, billing, lab_result
    priority VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'normal',
    
    -- Status
    is_read BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    read_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    is_archived BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_deleted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Response
    requires_response BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    response_due_by TIMESTAMPTZ,
    responded_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Online Appointment Requests
CREATE TABLE online_appointment_requests (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    request_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Patient
    patient_id UUID REFERENCES patients(id),
    portal_user_id UUID REFERENCES patient_portal_users(id),
    
    -- For new patients
    patient_name VARCHAR(255),
    patient_phone VARCHAR(20),
    patient_email VARCHAR(255),
    date_of_birth DATE,
    gender gender_type,
    
    -- Request Details
    preferred_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    specialization VARCHAR(100),
    
    preferred_date DATE,
    preferred_time_slot VARCHAR(20), -- morning, afternoon, evening
    alternate_date DATE,
    
    consultation_type VARCHAR(50), -- opd, telemedicine
    
    -- Reason
    reason_for_visit TEXT,
    is_followup BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    previous_visit_date DATE,
    
    -- Insurance
    has_insurance BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    insurance_company VARCHAR(255),
    insurance_policy_number VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, confirmed, rejected, cancelled
    
    -- Processing
    processed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Confirmed Appointment
    confirmed_appointment_id UUID REFERENCES appointments(id),
    
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Communication
    confirmation_sent BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    confirmation_sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, request_number)
);

-- Online Bill Payments
CREATE TABLE online_payments (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    transaction_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    portal_user_id UUID REFERENCES patient_portal_users(id),
    
    -- Bill Reference
    bill_id UUID REFERENCES bills(id),
    
    -- Amount
    amount DECIMAL(14, 2) NOT NULL,
    currency VARCHAR(3) DEFAULT 'BDT',
    
    -- Payment Gateway
    payment_gateway VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- sslcommerz, bkash, nagad, stripe
    gateway_transaction_id VARCHAR(255),
    gateway_response JSONB,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'initiated', -- initiated, pending, success, failed, refunded
    
    -- Timestamps
    initiated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Failure
    failure_reason TEXT,
    failure_code VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Refund
    refund_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    refund_reason TEXT,
    refunded_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    refund_transaction_id VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Linked Payment Record
    payment_id UUID REFERENCES payments(id),
    
    -- Metadata
    client_ip INET,
    user_agent TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, transaction_id)
);

-- Patient Health Records Sharing
CREATE TABLE health_record_sharing (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- Share With
    shared_with_type VARCHAR(50), -- doctor, hospital, family_member, insurance
    shared_with_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    shared_with_organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id),
    shared_with_email VARCHAR(255),
    shared_with_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- What to Share
    share_scope VARCHAR(50), -- all, selected
    shared_record_types TEXT[], -- lab_results, prescriptions, discharge_summary
    shared_record_ids UUID[],
    
    -- Validity
    valid_from TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Access
    access_code VARCHAR(100), -- One-time access code
    access_code_hash VARCHAR(255),
    
    max_access_count INTEGER,
    current_access_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active', -- active, expired, revoked
    
    revoked_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    revoked_reason TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    -- Access Log
    last_accessed_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);

-- Sharing Access Log
CREATE TABLE health_record_access_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    sharing_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES health_record_sharing(id),
    
    accessed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    accessor_ip INET,
    accessor_user_agent TEXT,
    accessor_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    records_accessed JSONB
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 63: REFERRAL MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Referral Sources
CREATE TABLE referral_sources (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    source_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    source_type VARCHAR(50), -- doctor, hospital, clinic, diagnostic_center, corporate, marketing
    
    -- Contact
    contact_person VARCHAR(255),
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    email VARCHAR(255),
    address TEXT,
    
    -- Commission
    commission_type VARCHAR(20), -- none, fixed, percentage
    commission_value DECIMAL(10, 2),
    
    -- Agreement
    agreement_start_date DATE,
    agreement_end_date DATE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, source_code)
);

-- Incoming Referrals
CREATE TABLE incoming_referrals (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    referral_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    -- Referral Source
    source_id UUID REFERENCES referral_sources(id),
    referring_doctor_name VARCHAR(255),
    referring_hospital VARCHAR(255),
    referring_doctor_phone VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Referral Details
    referral_date DATE NOT NULL,
    referral_reason TEXT,
    referral_diagnosis TEXT,
    
    -- Documents
    referral_letter_url TEXT,
    attached_documents JSONB,
    
    -- Destination
    referred_to_department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    referred_to_doctor_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    -- Urgency
    urgency VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'routine',
    
    -- Outcome
    outcome VARCHAR(50), -- admitted, opd_treated, not_accepted, redirected
    outcome_date DATE,
    outcome_notes TEXT,
    
    -- Linked Records
    appointment_id UUID REFERENCES appointments(id),
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, referral_number)
);

-- Outgoing Referrals
CREATE TABLE outgoing_referrals (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    referral_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    -- Referring Doctor (from our hospital)
    referring_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES doctors(id),
    
    -- Referred To
    referred_to_hospital VARCHAR(255),
    referred_to_doctor VARCHAR(255),
    referred_to_department VARCHAR(100),
    referred_to_phone VARCHAR(20),
    referred_to_address TEXT,
    
    -- Reason
    referral_reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    clinical_summary TEXT,
    
    -- Priority
    urgency VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'routine',
    
    -- Documents
    referral_letter_url TEXT,
    attached_reports JSONB,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'initiated', -- initiated, accepted, completed, feedback_received
    
    -- Feedback
    feedback_received BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    feedback_date DATE,
    feedback_notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, referral_number)
);

-- Referral Commission Tracking
CREATE TABLE referral_commissions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    referral_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES incoming_referrals(id),
    source_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES referral_sources(id),
    
    -- Revenue
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    bill_id UUID REFERENCES bills(id),
    
    bill_amount DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Commission
    commission_type VARCHAR(20),
    commission_rate DECIMAL(10, 2),
    commission_amount DECIMAL(12, 2) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Payment
    payment_status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, approved, paid
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    paid_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    payment_reference VARCHAR(100),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 64: RESEARCH & CLINICAL TRIALS
-- ============================================

-- Research Studies
CREATE TABLE research_studies (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    study_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    title VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    
    study_type VARCHAR(50), -- observational, interventional, clinical_trial
    phase VARCHAR(20), -- phase_1, phase_2, phase_3, phase_4
    
    -- Principal Investigator
    principal_investigator_id UUID REFERENCES doctors(id),
    co_investigators UUID[],
    
    -- Sponsor
    sponsor_name VARCHAR(255),
    sponsor_type VARCHAR(50), -- industry, academic, government, self
    
    -- Regulatory
    irb_approval_number VARCHAR(100),
    irb_approval_date DATE,
    irb_expiry_date DATE,
    
    ctri_registration VARCHAR(100), -- Clinical Trials Registry India
    
    -- Timeline
    planned_start_date DATE,
    planned_end_date DATE,
    actual_start_date DATE,
    actual_end_date DATE,
    
    -- Enrollment
    target_enrollment INTEGER,
    current_enrollment INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Budget
    total_budget DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'planning', -- planning, recruiting, active, completed, suspended, terminated
    
    -- Documents
    protocol_url TEXT,
    consent_form_url TEXT,
    
    description TEXT,
    inclusion_criteria TEXT,
    exclusion_criteria TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, study_code)
);

-- Research Study Subjects
CREATE TABLE research_subjects (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    study_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES research_studies(id),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    
    subject_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- Anonymized subject ID
    
    -- Enrollment
    screening_date DATE,
    enrollment_date DATE,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'screening', -- screening, enrolled, active, completed, withdrawn, excluded
    
    -- Arm/Group
    study_arm VARCHAR(100),
    randomization_number VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Withdrawal
    withdrawal_date DATE,
    withdrawal_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Consent
    consent_date DATE,
    consent_version VARCHAR(20),
    consent_document_url TEXT,
    
    -- Visits
    next_visit_date DATE,
    visits_completed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(study_id, subject_id)
);

-- Research Visit Schedule
CREATE TABLE research_visit_schedule (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    study_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES research_studies(id),
    
    visit_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    visit_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    visit_day INTEGER, -- Day from enrollment (e.g., Day 0, Day 7, Day 30)
    visit_window_before INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- Days before
    visit_window_after INTEGER DEFAULT 0, -- Days after
    
    -- Procedures
    procedures JSONB, -- [{procedure_name, is_mandatory}]
    
    -- Assessments
    assessments JSONB,
    
    -- Labs
    lab_tests JSONB,
    
    is_mandatory BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    display_order INTEGER,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Research Subject Visits
CREATE TABLE research_subject_visits (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    subject_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES research_subjects(id),
    visit_schedule_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES research_visit_schedule(id),
    
    -- Scheduled
    scheduled_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Actual
    actual_date DATE,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'scheduled', -- scheduled, completed, missed, rescheduled
    
    -- Window Compliance
    within_window BOOLEAN,
    
    -- Data Collection
    procedures_completed JSONB,
    assessments_completed JSONB,
    
    -- Adverse Events
    adverse_events_reported BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    completed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Adverse Events
CREATE TABLE adverse_events (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    study_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES research_studies(id),
    subject_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES research_subjects(id),
    
    ae_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Event Details
    event_term VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    event_description TEXT,
    
    onset_date DATE NOT NULL,
    resolution_date DATE,
    
    -- Severity
    severity VARCHAR(20), -- mild, moderate, severe, life_threatening, death
    
    -- Seriousness
    is_serious BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    seriousness_criteria TEXT[], -- death, hospitalization, disability, etc.
    
    -- Causality
    relationship_to_study VARCHAR(50), -- unrelated, unlikely, possible, probable, definite
    
    -- Outcome
    outcome VARCHAR(50), -- recovered, recovering, not_recovered, fatal, unknown
    
    -- Action Taken
    action_with_study_drug VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Reporting
    reported_to_sponsor BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    sponsor_reported_date DATE,
    reported_to_irb BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    irb_reported_date DATE,
    
    -- Follow-up
    requires_followup BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    followup_notes TEXT,
    
    reported_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(study_id, ae_number)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 65: COST ACCOUNTING
-- ============================================

-- Cost Centers
CREATE TABLE cost_centers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    
    cost_center_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    cost_center_type VARCHAR(50), -- revenue, support, administrative
    
    parent_cost_center_id UUID REFERENCES cost_centers(id),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    -- Budget
    annual_budget DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    manager_id UUID REFERENCES employees(id),
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, cost_center_code)
);

-- Cost Allocation Rules
CREATE TABLE cost_allocation_rules (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    rule_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    source_cost_center_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES cost_centers(id),
    
    -- Allocation Method
    allocation_method VARCHAR(50), -- direct, step_down, activity_based
    
    -- Allocation Basis
    allocation_basis VARCHAR(50), -- revenue, patient_days, sq_feet, headcount, fixed_percent
    
    -- Target Cost Centers
    allocations JSONB, -- [{cost_center_id, percentage or basis_value}]
    
    effective_from DATE,
    effective_to DATE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Activity Costs
CREATE TABLE activity_costs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    activity_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    activity_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    cost_center_id UUID REFERENCES cost_centers(id),
    
    -- Cost Components
    direct_material_cost DECIMAL(12, 2),
    direct_labor_cost DECIMAL(12, 2),
    overhead_cost DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    total_cost DECIMAL(12, 2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        COALESCE(direct_material_cost, 0) + 
        COALESCE(direct_labor_cost, 0) + 
        COALESCE(overhead_cost, 0)
    ) STORED,
    
    -- Per Unit
    cost_per_unit DECIMAL(12, 4),
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(50),
    
    -- Period
    effective_from DATE,
    effective_to DATE,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, activity_code, effective_from)
);

-- Patient Service Costs
CREATE TABLE patient_service_costs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patients(id),
    visit_id UUID,
    admission_id UUID REFERENCES admissions(id),
    
    service_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Service
    service_type VARCHAR(50), -- lab, radiology, pharmacy, procedure, room, consultation
    service_id UUID,
    service_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Cost Components
    direct_cost DECIMAL(12, 2),
    indirect_cost DECIMAL(12, 2),
    total_cost DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Revenue
    billed_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    -- Margin
    margin DECIMAL(12, 2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (billed_amount - total_cost) STORED,
    margin_percent DECIMAL(8, 2),
    
    -- Cost Center
    cost_center_id UUID REFERENCES cost_centers(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Monthly Cost Summary
CREATE TABLE monthly_cost_summary (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    branch_id UUID REFERENCES branches(id),
    cost_center_id UUID REFERENCES cost_centers(id),
    
    year_month DATE NOT NULL, -- First day of month
    
    -- Revenue
    total_revenue DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Costs
    direct_costs DECIMAL(14, 2),
    indirect_costs DECIMAL(14, 2),
    allocated_costs DECIMAL(14, 2),
    total_costs DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Profitability
    gross_margin DECIMAL(14, 2),
    net_margin DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    -- Statistics
    patient_days INTEGER,
    procedures_count INTEGER,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, branch_id, cost_center_id, year_month)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 66: CONTRACT MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================

-- Contracts
CREATE TABLE contracts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    contract_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    title VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    
    contract_type VARCHAR(50), -- vendor, service, amc, lease, consulting, insurance, corporate
    
    -- Parties
    party_type VARCHAR(50), -- supplier, customer, corporate, insurance
    party_id UUID, -- Reference to supplier/corporate/insurance
    party_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Value
    contract_value DECIMAL(14, 2),
    currency VARCHAR(3) DEFAULT 'BDT',
    
    -- Duration
    start_date DATE NOT NULL,
    end_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Auto Renewal
    auto_renew BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    renewal_notice_days INTEGER,
    renewal_terms TEXT,
    
    -- Payment Terms
    payment_terms TEXT,
    payment_frequency VARCHAR(20), -- one_time, monthly, quarterly, annually
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'draft', -- draft, pending_approval, active, expired, terminated
    
    -- Approval
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Documents
    contract_document_url TEXT,
    attachments JSONB,
    
    -- Key Terms
    key_terms JSONB,
    sla_terms JSONB,
    penalty_terms TEXT,
    
    -- Ownership
    contract_owner_id UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES departments(id),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(organization_id, contract_number)
);

-- Contract Milestones
CREATE TABLE contract_milestones (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    contract_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES contracts(id),
    
    milestone_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    
    due_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Payment
    payment_amount DECIMAL(12, 2),
    payment_percentage DECIMAL(5, 2),
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending, completed, overdue
    
    completed_date DATE,
    completed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    notes TEXT,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Contract Renewals
CREATE TABLE contract_renewals (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    original_contract_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES contracts(id),
    renewed_contract_id UUID REFERENCES contracts(id),
    
    renewal_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    previous_end_date DATE,
    new_end_date DATE,
    
    previous_value DECIMAL(14, 2),
    new_value DECIMAL(14, 2),
    
    renewal_terms TEXT,
    
    processed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Contract Alerts
CREATE TABLE contract_alerts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    contract_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES contracts(id),
    
    alert_type VARCHAR(50), -- expiry, renewal, milestone, payment, sla_breach
    
    alert_date DATE NOT NULL,
    
    description TEXT,
    
    -- Status
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active', -- active, acknowledged, resolved
    
    acknowledged_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    acknowledged_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 67: INDEXES FOR NEW TABLES
-- ============================================

-- Mobile & Portal
CREATE INDEX idx_mobile_devices_user ON mobile_devices(user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_mobile_devices_active ON mobile_devices(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_mobile_sync_queue_device ON mobile_sync_queue(device_id, status);

CREATE INDEX idx_portal_users_patient ON patient_portal_users(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_portal_users_org ON patient_portal_users(organization_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_messages_patient ON patient_messages(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_messages_thread ON patient_messages(thread_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_online_appointments_status ON online_appointment_requests(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_online_payments_status ON online_payments(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_health_sharing_patient ON health_record_sharing(patient_id);

-- Referrals
CREATE INDEX idx_incoming_referrals_source ON incoming_referrals(source_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_incoming_referrals_patient ON incoming_referrals(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_outgoing_referrals_doctor ON outgoing_referrals(referring_doctor_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_referral_commissions_status ON referral_commissions(payment_status);

-- Research
CREATE INDEX idx_research_studies_status ON research_studies(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_research_subjects_study ON research_subjects(study_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_research_subjects_patient ON research_subjects(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_adverse_events_study ON adverse_events(study_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_adverse_events_serious ON adverse_events(is_serious) WHERE is_serious = TRUE;

-- Cost Accounting
CREATE INDEX idx_cost_centers_parent ON cost_centers(parent_cost_center_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_service_costs_patient ON patient_service_costs(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_service_costs_date ON patient_service_costs(service_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_monthly_cost_summary_period ON monthly_cost_summary(year_month);

-- Contracts
CREATE INDEX idx_contracts_status ON contracts(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_contracts_end_date ON contracts(end_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_contracts_party ON contracts(party_type, party_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_contract_alerts_date ON contract_alerts(alert_date, status);
```

```sql
-- ============================================

-- Create sample organization
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_sample_organization()
RETURNS UUID AS $$
DECLARE
    v_org_id UUID;
    v_branch_id UUID;
    v_admin_role_id UUID;
    v_admin_user_id UUID;
BEGIN
    -- Create Organization
    INSERT INTO organizations (
        code, name, name_bangla, 
        email, phone,
        address_line1, city, district, division,
        settings
    ) VALUES (
        'DEMO-HOSP',
        'Demo General Hospital',
        'ডেমো জেনারেল হাসপাতাল',
        'info@demohospital.com',
        '+8801700000000',
        '123 Hospital Road',
        'Dhaka',
        'Dhaka',
        'Dhaka',
        '{"timezone": "Asia/Dhaka", "currency": "BDT", "date_format": "DD/MM/YYYY"}'::JSONB
    ) RETURNING id INTO v_org_id;
    
    -- Create Main Branch
    INSERT INTO branches (
        organization_id, code, name, name_bangla,
        email, phone, 
        city, district, division,
        is_main_branch, total_beds
    ) VALUES (
        v_org_id,
        'MAIN',
        'Main Branch',
        'প্রধান শাখা',
        'main@demohospital.com',
        '+8801700000001',
        'Dhaka',
        'Dhaka',
        'Dhaka',
        TRUE,
        200
    ) RETURNING id INTO v_branch_id;
    
    -- Get or create admin role
    SELECT id INTO v_admin_role_id FROM roles WHERE code = 'ORG_ADMIN' LIMIT 1;
    
    -- Create Admin User
    INSERT INTO users (
        organization_id,
        username, email, phone,
        password_hash,
        first_name, last_name,
        is_active
    ) VALUES (
        v_org_id,
        'admin',
        'admin@demohospital.com',
        '+8801700000002',
        crypt('Admin@123', gen_salt('bf')),
        'System',
        'Administrator',
        TRUE
    ) RETURNING id INTO v_admin_user_id;
    
    -- Assign Role
    INSERT INTO user_roles (user_id, role_id, is_primary)
    VALUES (v_admin_user_id, v_admin_role_id, TRUE);
    
    -- Create Branch Access
    INSERT INTO user_branch_access (user_id, branch_id, is_default)
    VALUES (v_admin_user_id, v_branch_id, TRUE);
    
    RETURN v_org_id;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Create sample departments
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_sample_departments(p_org_id UUID, p_branch_id UUID)
RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
DECLARE
```
