```sql
BEGIN
    INSERT INTO departments (organization_id, branch_id, code, name, department_type, is_clinical)
    VALUES
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'MED', 'Medicine', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'SURG', 'Surgery', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'ORTHO', 'Orthopedics', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'GYN', 'Obstetrics & Gynecology', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'PED', 'Pediatrics', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'CARD', 'Cardiology', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'NEURO', 'Neurology', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'ENT', 'ENT', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'DERM', 'Dermatology', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'OPTH', 'Ophthalmology', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'RAD', 'Radiology', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'PATH', 'Pathology', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'EMRG', 'Emergency', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'ICU', 'Intensive Care', 'clinical', TRUE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'ADMIN', 'Administration', 'administrative', FALSE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'HR', 'Human Resources', 'administrative', FALSE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'FIN', 'Finance', 'administrative', FALSE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'IT', 'Information Technology', 'support', FALSE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'PHARM', 'Pharmacy', 'support', FALSE),
        (p_org_id, p_branch_id, 'NURSE', 'Nursing', 'clinical', TRUE)

-- Create sample wards and beds
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_sample_wards_beds(p_org_id UUID, p_branch_id UUID)
RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_ward_id UUID;
    v_room_id UUID;
    v_ward RECORD;
    v_bed_count INTEGER := 0;
BEGIN
    -- Create Wards
    FOR v_ward IN 
        SELECT * FROM (VALUES
            ('GEN-M', 'General Ward - Male', 'general', 'male', 30),
            ('GEN-F', 'General Ward - Female', 'general', 'female', 30),
            ('PVT', 'Private Ward', 'private', NULL, 20),
            ('ICU', 'Intensive Care Unit', 'icu', NULL, 10),
            ('NICU', 'Neonatal ICU', 'nicu', NULL, 8),
            ('CCU', 'Coronary Care Unit', 'ccu', NULL, 8),
            ('PED', 'Pediatric Ward', 'pediatric', NULL, 20),
            ('MAT', 'Maternity Ward', 'maternity', 'female', 25),
            ('SURG', 'Surgical Ward', 'general', NULL, 25),
            ('EMRG', 'Emergency Observation', 'general', NULL, 15)
        ) AS w(code, name, ward_type, gender, beds)
    LOOP
        INSERT INTO wards (
            organization_id, branch_id, code, name, ward_type, 
            gender_restriction, total_beds
        ) VALUES (
            p_org_id, p_branch_id, v_ward.code, v_ward.name, 
            v_ward.ward_type, v_ward.gender::gender_type, v_ward.beds
        ) 
        ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
        RETURNING id INTO v_ward_id;
        
        IF v_ward_id IS NOT NULL THEN
            -- Create Beds for this ward
            FOR i IN 1..v_ward.beds LOOP
                INSERT INTO beds (
                    organization_id, branch_id, ward_id,
                    bed_number, bed_type, status,
                    has_oxygen, daily_rate
                ) VALUES (
                    p_org_id, p_branch_id, v_ward_id,
                    v_ward.code || '-' || LPAD(i::TEXT, 2, '0'),
                    CASE 
                        WHEN v_ward.ward_type = 'icu' THEN 'icu'
                        WHEN v_ward.ward_type = 'nicu' THEN 'nicu'
                        ELSE 'standard'
                    END,
                    'available',
                    TRUE,
                    CASE 
                        WHEN v_ward.ward_type = 'icu' THEN 8000
                        WHEN v_ward.ward_type = 'private' THEN 5000
                        ELSE 1500
                    END
                )
                ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
                
                v_bed_count := v_bed_count + 1;
            END LOOP;
        END IF;
    END LOOP;
    
    RETURN v_bed_count;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Create sample doctors
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_sample_doctors(p_org_id UUID, p_branch_id UUID, p_count INTEGER DEFAULT 20)
RETURNS INTEGER AS $$
DECLARE
    v_user_id UUID;
    v_doctor_id UUID;
    v_dept_id UUID;
    v_role_id UUID;
    v_specializations TEXT[] := ARRAY['Medicine', 'Surgery', 'Cardiology', 'Orthopedics', 'Gynecology', 'Pediatrics', 'Neurology', 'Dermatology', 'ENT', 'Ophthalmology'];
    v_first_names TEXT[] := ARRAY['Dr. Ahmed', 'Dr. Rahman', 'Dr. Karim', 'Dr. Hassan', 'Dr. Ali', 'Dr. Fatima', 'Dr. Ayesha', 'Dr. Nasreen', 'Dr. Sultana', 'Dr. Khadija'];
    v_last_names TEXT[] := ARRAY['Islam', 'Khan', 'Chowdhury', 'Hossain', 'Uddin', 'Begum', 'Akter', 'Sheikh', 'Siddiqui', 'Ahmed'];
    v_counter INTEGER := 0;
    v_spec TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- Get doctor role
    SELECT id INTO v_role_id FROM roles WHERE code = 'DOCTOR' LIMIT 1;
    
    FOR i IN 1..p_count LOOP
        v_spec := v_specializations[1 + (i % array_length(v_specializations, 1))];
        
        -- Get department for specialization
        SELECT id INTO v_dept_id FROM departments 
        WHERE organization_id = p_org_id 
        AND (name ILIKE '%' || v_spec || '%' OR code ILIKE LEFT(v_spec, 3) || '%')
        LIMIT 1;
        
        -- Create User
        INSERT INTO users (
            organization_id,
            username, email, phone,
            password_hash,
            first_name, last_name,
            department_id,
            designation,
            is_active
        ) VALUES (
            p_org_id,
            'dr' || LPAD(i::TEXT, 3, '0'),
            'dr' || i || '@demohospital.com',
            '01' || (7 + (i % 3))::TEXT || (10000000 + i * 1000)::TEXT,
            crypt('Doctor@123', gen_salt('bf')),
            v_first_names[1 + (i % array_length(v_first_names, 1))],
            v_last_names[1 + (i % array_length(v_last_names, 1))],
            v_dept_id,
            'Consultant',
            TRUE
        ) RETURNING id INTO v_user_id;
        
        -- Create Doctor Profile
        INSERT INTO doctors (
            user_id, organization_id,
            doctor_code,
            bmdc_registration,
            specialization,
            opd_fee, followup_fee, telemedicine_fee,
            consultation_duration,
            is_available_opd, is_available_telemedicine
        ) VALUES (
            v_user_id, p_org_id,
            'DOC' || LPAD(i::TEXT, 4, '0'),
            'A-' || (10000 + i)::TEXT,
            v_spec,
            (500 + (i % 10) * 100),
            (300 + (i % 10) * 50),
            (400 + (i % 10) * 50),
            15,
            TRUE,
            i % 2 = 0
        ) RETURNING id INTO v_doctor_id;
        
        -- Assign Role
        INSERT INTO user_roles (user_id, role_id, is_primary)
        VALUES (v_user_id, v_role_id, TRUE)
        ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
        
        -- Branch Access
        INSERT INTO user_branch_access (user_id, branch_id, is_default)
        VALUES (v_user_id, p_branch_id, TRUE)
        ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
        
        -- Create Schedule Template (Mon-Fri)
        FOR d IN 0..4 LOOP
            INSERT INTO doctor_schedule_templates (
                doctor_id, branch_id, day_of_week,
                session1_start, session1_end, session1_max_patients,
                session2_start, session2_end, session2_max_patients
            ) VALUES (
                v_doctor_id, p_branch_id, d + 1,
                '09:00', '13:00', 20,
                '17:00', '20:00', 15
            ) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
        END LOOP;
        
        v_counter := v_counter + 1;
    END LOOP;
    
    RETURN v_counter;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

        ('Insulin Mixtard', 'Mixtard', 'Novo Nordisk', 'injection', '100IU/ml', 'Insulin', 'sc', 550.00, TRUE),
        
        -- Others

        ('Pantoprazole', 'Pantonix', 'Square', 'injection', '40mg', 'PPI', 'iv', 100.00, TRUE),
        ('Ondansetron', 'Emistat', 'Square', 'tablet', '4mg', 'Antiemetic', 'oral', 10.00, TRUE),
        ('Tramadol', 'Tramal', 'Grunenthal', 'capsule', '50mg', 'Analgesic', 'oral', 15.00, TRUE)
    ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
    
    GET DIAGNOSTICS v_count = ROW_COUNT;
    RETURN v_count;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Master function to create all sample data
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_all_sample_data()
RETURNS TABLE(entity VARCHAR, count INTEGER) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_org_id UUID;
    v_branch_id UUID;
BEGIN
    -- Create organization
    v_org_id := create_sample_organization();
    
    -- Get branch
    SELECT id INTO v_branch_id FROM branches 
    WHERE organization_id = v_org_id AND is_main_branch = TRUE LIMIT 1;
    
    -- Create sample data
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 'Organization'::VARCHAR, 1
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Departments'::VARCHAR, create_sample_departments(v_org_id, v_branch_id)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Wards & Beds'::VARCHAR, create_sample_wards_beds(v_org_id, v_branch_id)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Doctors'::VARCHAR, create_sample_doctors(v_org_id, v_branch_id, 20)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Test Catalog'::VARCHAR, create_sample_test_catalog(v_org_id)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Drugs'::VARCHAR, create_sample_drugs()
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'Patients'::VARCHAR, generate_sample_patients(v_org_id, v_branch_id, 100);
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 69: TESTING & VALIDATION
-- ============================================

-- Test foreign key relationships
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION test_foreign_keys()
RETURNS TABLE(
    constraint_name VARCHAR,
    table_name VARCHAR,
    column_name VARCHAR,
    foreign_table VARCHAR,
    foreign_column VARCHAR,
    status VARCHAR
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        tc.constraint_name::VARCHAR,
        tc.table_name::VARCHAR,
        kcu.column_name::VARCHAR,
        ccu.table_name::VARCHAR AS foreign_table,
        ccu.column_name::VARCHAR AS foreign_column,
        'OK'::VARCHAR AS status
    FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
    JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu 
        ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
    JOIN information_schema.constraint_column_usage ccu 
        ON tc.constraint_name = ccu.constraint_name
    WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY'
    AND tc.table_schema = 'public'
    ORDER BY tc.table_name, tc.constraint_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Test indexes exist for foreign keys
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_missing_fk_indexes()
RETURNS TABLE(
    table_name VARCHAR,
    column_name VARCHAR,
    constraint_name VARCHAR,
    recommendation VARCHAR
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        tc.table_name::VARCHAR,
        kcu.column_name::VARCHAR,
        tc.constraint_name::VARCHAR,
        ('CREATE INDEX idx_' || tc.table_name || '_' || kcu.column_name || 
         ' ON ' || tc.table_name || '(' || kcu.column_name || ')')::VARCHAR AS recommendation
    FROM information_schema.table_constraints tc
    JOIN information_schema.key_column_usage kcu 
        ON tc.constraint_name = kcu.constraint_name
    WHERE tc.constraint_type = 'FOREIGN KEY'
    AND tc.table_schema = 'public'
    AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_indexes 
        WHERE tablename = tc.table_name 
        AND indexdef LIKE '%' || kcu.column_name || '%'
    );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Validate data integrity
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_data_integrity()
RETURNS TABLE(
    check_name VARCHAR,
    status VARCHAR,
    details TEXT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Check orphan patients
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        'Orphan Patients'::VARCHAR,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN 'PASS' ELSE 'FAIL' END::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*)::TEXT || ' patients without organization'
    FROM patients p
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations o WHERE o.id = p.organization_id);
    
    -- Check orphan admissions
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        'Orphan Admissions'::VARCHAR,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN 'PASS' ELSE 'FAIL' END::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*)::TEXT || ' admissions without patient'
    FROM admissions a
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM patients p WHERE p.id = a.patient_id);
    
    -- Check beds without wards
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        'Beds Without Wards'::VARCHAR,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN 'PASS' ELSE 'FAIL' END::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*)::TEXT || ' beds without valid ward'
    FROM beds b
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM wards w WHERE w.id = b.ward_id);
    
    -- Check appointments without doctors
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        'Appointments Without Doctors'::VARCHAR,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN 'PASS' ELSE 'FAIL' END::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*)::TEXT || ' appointments without valid doctor'
    FROM appointments a
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM doctors d WHERE d.id = a.doctor_id);
    
    -- Check bills without patients
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        'Bills Without Patients'::VARCHAR,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN 'PASS' ELSE 'FAIL' END::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*)::TEXT || ' bills without valid patient'
    FROM bills b
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM patients p WHERE p.id = b.patient_id);
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Performance test function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION test_query_performance()
RETURNS TABLE(
    query_name VARCHAR,
    execution_time_ms NUMERIC,
    row_count INTEGER
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_start TIMESTAMPTZ;
    v_end TIMESTAMPTZ;
    v_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Test patient search
    v_start := clock_timestamp();
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count FROM patients WHERE full_name ILIKE '%ahmed%';
    v_end := clock_timestamp();
    RETURN QUERY SELECT 'Patient Search'::VARCHAR, 
        EXTRACT(MILLISECOND FROM (v_end - v_start))::NUMERIC, v_count;
    
    -- Test active admissions
    v_start := clock_timestamp();
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count FROM admissions WHERE status = 'admitted';
    v_end := clock_timestamp();
    RETURN QUERY SELECT 'Active Admissions'::VARCHAR, 
        EXTRACT(MILLISECOND FROM (v_end - v_start))::NUMERIC, v_count;
    
    -- Test today's appointments
    v_start := clock_timestamp();
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count FROM appointments WHERE appointment_date = CURRENT_DATE;
    v_end := clock_timestamp();
    RETURN QUERY SELECT 'Today Appointments'::VARCHAR, 
        EXTRACT(MILLISECOND FROM (v_end - v_start))::NUMERIC, v_count;
    
    -- Test available beds
    v_start := clock_timestamp();
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count FROM beds WHERE status = 'available' AND is_active = TRUE;
    v_end := clock_timestamp();
    RETURN QUERY SELECT 'Available Beds'::VARCHAR, 
        EXTRACT(MILLISECOND FROM (v_end - v_start))::NUMERIC, v_count;
    
    -- Test pending lab orders
    v_start := clock_timestamp();
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count FROM lab_orders WHERE status IN ('ordered', 'collected');
    v_end := clock_timestamp();
    RETURN QUERY SELECT 'Pending Lab Orders'::VARCHAR, 
        EXTRACT(MILLISECOND FROM (v_end - v_start))::NUMERIC, v_count;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

```sql
-- ============================================
-- SECTION 70: BACKUP & RECOVERY
-- ============================================

-- Backup configuration table
CREATE TABLE backup_configurations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    
    backup_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- full, incremental, differential
    
    schedule_cron VARCHAR(100), -- Cron expression
    
    retention_days INTEGER DEFAULT 30,
    
    -- Destination
    storage_type VARCHAR(50), -- local, s3, gcs, azure
    storage_path TEXT,
    storage_credentials JSONB, -- Encrypted
    
    -- Options
    compress BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    encrypt BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    encryption_key_id VARCHAR(100),
    
    include_schemas TEXT[] DEFAULT ARRAY['public'],
    exclude_tables TEXT[],
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    last_backup_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_backup_status VARCHAR(20),
    last_backup_size_mb DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Backup history
CREATE TABLE backup_history (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    configuration_id UUID REFERENCES backup_configurations(id),
    
    backup_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    status VARCHAR(20), -- running, completed, failed
    
    -- Details
    file_name VARCHAR(500),
    file_path TEXT,
    file_size_mb DECIMAL(12, 2),
    
    tables_backed_up INTEGER,
    rows_backed_up BIGINT,
    
    error_message TEXT,
    
    -- Verification
    verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Retention
    expires_at DATE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Create backup script generator
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION generate_backup_script(
    p_backup_type VARCHAR DEFAULT 'full',
    p_output_path VARCHAR DEFAULT '/backup'
)
RETURNS TEXT AS $$
DECLARE
    v_script TEXT;
    v_filename TEXT;
BEGIN
    v_filename := 'hospital_erp_' || p_backup_type || '_' || 
                  TO_CHAR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'YYYYMMDD_HH24MISS') || '.sql';
    
    v_script := '#!/bin/bash
# Hospital ERP Database Backup Script
# Generated: ' || CURRENT_TIMESTAMP || '
# Type: ' || p_backup_type || '

BACKUP_DIR="' || p_output_path || '"
FILENAME="' || v_filename || '"
DB_NAME="hospital_erp"

# Create backup directory if not exists
mkdir -p $BACKUP_DIR

# Run pg_dump
pg_dump -h localhost -U postgres -d $DB_NAME \
    --format=custom \
    --compress=9 \
    --verbose \
    --file="$BACKUP_DIR/$FILENAME"

# Verify backup
if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "Backup completed successfully: $BACKUP_DIR/$FILENAME"
    # Get file size
    ls -lh "$BACKUP_DIR/$FILENAME"
else
    echo "Backup failed!"
    exit 1
fi

# Optional: Upload to S3
# aws s3 cp "$BACKUP_DIR/$FILENAME" s3://your-bucket/backups/

# Optional: Remove old backups (older than 30 days)
find $BACKUP_DIR -name "hospital_erp_*.sql" -mtime +30 -delete

echo "Backup process completed."
';
    
    RETURN v_script;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Generate restore script
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION generate_restore_script(
    p_backup_file VARCHAR,
    p_target_db VARCHAR DEFAULT 'hospital_erp_restored'
)
RETURNS TEXT AS $$
DECLARE
    v_script TEXT;
BEGIN
    v_script := '#!/bin/bash
# Hospital ERP Database Restore Script
# Generated: ' || CURRENT_TIMESTAMP || '

BACKUP_FILE="' || p_backup_file || '"
TARGET_DB="' || p_target_db || '"

# Check if backup file exists
if [ ! -f "$BACKUP_FILE" ]; then
    echo "Backup file not found: $BACKUP_FILE"
    exit 1
fi

# Create target database
createdb -h localhost -U postgres $TARGET_DB

# Restore backup
pg_restore -h localhost -U postgres \
    --dbname=$TARGET_DB \
    --verbose \
    --clean \
    --if-exists \
    "$BACKUP_FILE"

if [ $? -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "Restore completed successfully to database: $TARGET_DB"
else
    echo "Restore failed!"
    exit 1
fi

# Verify restore
psql -h localhost -U postgres -d $TARGET_DB -c "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM patients;"

echo "Restore process completed."
';
    
    RETURN v_script;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 71: DATABASE MAINTENANCE PROCEDURES
-- ============================================

-- Comprehensive maintenance procedure
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE perform_database_maintenance()
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_start TIMESTAMPTZ := clock_timestamp();
    v_table RECORD;
BEGIN
    RAISE NOTICE 'Starting database maintenance at %', v_start;
    
    -- 1. Vacuum and Analyze all tables
    RAISE NOTICE 'Step 1: VACUUM ANALYZE...';
    FOR v_table IN 
        SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables WHERE schemaname = 'public'
    LOOP
        EXECUTE 'VACUUM ANALYZE ' || quote_ident(v_table.tablename);
    END LOOP;
    
    -- 2. Reindex critical tables
    RAISE NOTICE 'Step 2: Reindexing critical tables...';
    REINDEX TABLE patients;
    REINDEX TABLE admissions;
    REINDEX TABLE appointments;
    REINDEX TABLE bills;
    REINDEX TABLE lab_orders;
    
    -- 3. Refresh materialized views
    RAISE NOTICE 'Step 3: Refreshing materialized views...';
    BEGIN
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv_daily_census;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        RAISE NOTICE 'Could not refresh mv_daily_census: %', SQLERRM;
    END;
    
    BEGIN
        REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY mv_revenue_summary;
    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
        RAISE NOTICE 'Could not refresh mv_revenue_summary: %', SQLERRM;
    END;
    
    -- 4. Clean up expired sessions
    RAISE NOTICE 'Step 4: Cleaning expired sessions...';
    DELETE FROM user_sessions WHERE expires_at < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
    DELETE FROM patient_portal_sessions WHERE expires_at < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
    
    -- 5. Archive old audit logs (older than 90 days)
    RAISE NOTICE 'Step 5: Processing old audit logs...';
    -- In production, you would move to archive table
    -- DELETE FROM audit_logs WHERE created_at < CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '90 days';
    
    -- 6. Update table statistics
    RAISE NOTICE 'Step 6: Updating statistics...';
    ANALYZE;
    
    -- 7. Check for bloat
    RAISE NOTICE 'Step 7: Checking for table bloat...';
    -- Log tables with high bloat for manual attention
    
    RAISE NOTICE 'Database maintenance completed in % seconds', 
        EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (clock_timestamp() - v_start));
END;
$$;

-- Procedure to purge old data
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE purge_old_data(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_cutoff_date DATE,
    p_dry_run BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_counts JSONB := '{}'::JSONB;
    v_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    IF p_dry_run THEN
        RAISE NOTICE 'DRY RUN MODE - No data will be deleted';
    END IF;
    
    -- Count records to be deleted
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count 
    FROM audit_logs 
    WHERE organization_id = p_organization_id 
    AND created_at < p_cutoff_date;
    v_counts := v_counts || jsonb_build_object('audit_logs', v_count);
    
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count 
    FROM notification_log 
    WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_date;
    v_counts := v_counts || jsonb_build_object('notification_log', v_count);
    
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_count 
    FROM api_request_log 
    WHERE request_timestamp < p_cutoff_date;
    v_counts := v_counts || jsonb_build_object('api_request_log', v_count);
    
    RAISE NOTICE 'Records to be deleted: %', v_counts;
    
    IF NOT p_dry_run THEN
        -- Delete old audit logs
        DELETE FROM audit_logs 
        WHERE organization_id = p_organization_id 
        AND created_at < p_cutoff_date;
        
        -- Delete old notification logs
        DELETE FROM notification_log 
        WHERE created_at < p_cutoff_date;
        
        -- Delete old API logs
        DELETE FROM api_request_log 
        WHERE request_timestamp < p_cutoff_date;
        
        RAISE NOTICE 'Data purge completed';
    END IF;
END;
$$;
```
