-- ============================================
-- SECTION 73: REPORT GENERATOR FRAMEWORK
-- ============================================

-- Report Categories
CREATE TABLE report_categories (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(100),
    
    parent_category_id UUID REFERENCES report_categories(id),
    
    icon VARCHAR(50),
    display_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Insert default report categories
INSERT INTO report_categories (code, name, name_bangla, display_order) VALUES
    ('CLINICAL', 'Clinical Reports', 'ক্লিনিক্যাল রিপোর্ট', 1),
    ('FINANCIAL', 'Financial Reports', 'আর্থিক রিপোর্ট', 2),
    ('OPERATIONAL', 'Operational Reports', 'অপারেশনাল রিপোর্ট', 3),
    ('INVENTORY', 'Inventory Reports', 'ইনভেন্টরি রিপোর্ট', 4),
    ('HR', 'HR Reports', 'এইচআর রিপোর্ট', 5),
    ('QUALITY', 'Quality Reports', 'কোয়ালিটি রিপোর্ট', 6),
    ('MIS', 'MIS Reports', 'এমআইএস রিপোর্ট', 7),
    ('REGULATORY', 'Regulatory Reports', 'রেগুলেটরি রিপোর্ট', 8),
    ('CUSTOM', 'Custom Reports', 'কাস্টম রিপোর্ট', 9)
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

-- Enhanced Report Definitions
CREATE TABLE report_master (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id), -- NULL for system reports
    
    report_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name_bangla VARCHAR(255),
    description TEXT,
    
    category_id UUID REFERENCES report_categories(id),
    
    -- Report Type
    report_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'tabular', -- tabular, summary, chart, matrix, form
    
    -- Data Source
    source_type VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'query', -- query, function, view, api
    source_query TEXT,
    source_function VARCHAR(100),
    source_view VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Parameters
    parameters JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    /*
    [
        {
            "name": "from_date",
            "label": "From Date",
            "label_bn": "তারিখ থেকে",
            "type": "date",
            "required": true,
            "default": "current_date - 30"
        },
        {
            "name": "branch_id",
            "label": "Branch",
            "type": "select",
            "source": "branches",
            "required": false
        }
    ]
    */
    
    -- Columns/Fields
    columns JSONB DEFAULT '[]',
    /*
    [
        {
            "field": "patient_name",
            "header": "Patient Name",
            "header_bn": "রোগীর নাম",
            "type": "string",
            "width": 200,
            "sortable": true,
            "filterable": true,
            "aggregate": null
        },
        {
            "field": "amount",
            "header": "Amount",
            "type": "currency",
            "width": 120,
            "align": "right",
            "aggregate": "sum",
            "format": "#,##0.00"
        }
    ]
    */
    
    -- Grouping & Sorting
    default_group_by TEXT[],
    default_sort_by TEXT[],
    default_sort_order VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'ASC',
    
    -- Aggregations
    show_totals BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    show_subtotals BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Layout
    orientation VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'portrait', -- portrait, landscape
    page_size VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'A4',
    
    -- Header/Footer
    header_template TEXT,
    footer_template TEXT,
    
    -- Export Options
    export_formats TEXT[] DEFAULT ARRAY['pdf', 'excel', 'csv'],
    
    -- Access Control
    allowed_roles UUID[],
    is_public BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Scheduling
    can_schedule BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_system BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Metadata
    version INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
    created_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(COALESCE(organization_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), report_code)
);

-- Report Execution Log
CREATE TABLE report_execution_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    report_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES report_master(id),
    
    -- Execution Context
    execution_type VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'manual', -- manual, scheduled, api
    schedule_id UUID,
    
    -- User
    executed_by UUID REFERENCES users(id),
    
    -- Parameters Used
    parameters_used JSONB,
    
    -- Execution Details
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    duration_ms INTEGER,
    
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'running', -- running, completed, failed, cancelled
    error_message TEXT,
    
    -- Results
    row_count INTEGER,
    
    -- Output
    output_format VARCHAR(20),
    output_file_path TEXT,
    output_file_size BIGINT,
    
    -- Cache
    cache_key VARCHAR(255),
    cache_expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Delivery
    delivered BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    delivered_to TEXT[],
    delivered_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Report Templates (For custom formatting)
CREATE TABLE report_templates (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID REFERENCES organizations(id),
    
    template_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    template_type VARCHAR(50), -- header, footer, full_page, letterhead
    
    -- Content
    html_template TEXT,
    css_styles TEXT,
    
    -- Variables
    available_variables JSONB,
    
    -- Images
    logo_url TEXT,
    watermark_url TEXT,
    
    is_default BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    UNIQUE(COALESCE(organization_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::UUID), template_code)
);

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 74: CLINICAL REPORTS
-- ============================================

-- Daily OPD Report Function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_daily_opd_summary(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    department VARCHAR,
    doctor_name VARCHAR,
    specialization VARCHAR,
    total_appointments INTEGER,
    completed INTEGER,
    cancelled INTEGER,
    no_show INTEGER,
    new_patients INTEGER,
    followup_patients INTEGER,
    telemedicine INTEGER,
    total_revenue DECIMAL,
    avg_wait_time_minutes DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        d.name::VARCHAR as department,
        u.full_name::VARCHAR as doctor_name,
        doc.specialization::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*)::INTEGER as total_appointments,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'completed')::INTEGER as completed,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'cancelled')::INTEGER as cancelled,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'no_show')::INTEGER as no_show,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_type = 'new')::INTEGER as new_patients,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_type = 'followup')::INTEGER as followup_patients,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.consultation_type = 'telemedicine')::INTEGER as telemedicine,
        COALESCE(SUM(a.consultation_fee), 0)::DECIMAL as total_revenue,
        ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (a.consultation_start_at - a.checked_in_at))/60), 1)::DECIMAL as avg_wait_time_minutes
    FROM appointments a
    JOIN doctors doc ON a.doctor_id = doc.id
    JOIN users u ON doc.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN departments d ON u.department_id = d.id
    WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND a.appointment_date = p_date
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    GROUP BY d.name, u.full_name, doc.specialization
    ORDER BY d.name, u.full_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Daily IPD Census Report Function
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_daily_ipd_census(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    ward_name VARCHAR,
    ward_type VARCHAR,
    total_beds INTEGER,
    occupied_beds INTEGER,
    available_beds INTEGER,
    reserved_beds INTEGER,
    under_cleaning INTEGER,
    occupancy_rate DECIMAL,
    admissions_today INTEGER,
    discharges_today INTEGER,
    transfers_in INTEGER,
    transfers_out INTEGER,
    deaths_today INTEGER,
    critical_patients INTEGER,
    average_los DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        w.name::VARCHAR as ward_name,
        w.ward_type::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(b.id)::INTEGER as total_beds,
        COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.status = 'occupied')::INTEGER as occupied_beds,
        COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.status = 'available')::INTEGER as available_beds,
        COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.status = 'reserved')::INTEGER as reserved_beds,
        COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.status = 'cleaning')::INTEGER as under_cleaning,
        ROUND(COUNT(b.id) FILTER (WHERE b.status = 'occupied')::DECIMAL / 
              NULLIF(COUNT(b.id), 0) * 100, 1)::DECIMAL as occupancy_rate,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a2 
         JOIN bed_allocations ba2 ON a2.id = ba2.admission_id
         WHERE ba2.ward_id = w.id AND a2.admission_date = p_date)::INTEGER as admissions_today,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a3
         JOIN bed_allocations ba3 ON a3.id = ba3.admission_id
         WHERE ba3.ward_id = w.id AND a3.actual_discharge_date = p_date)::INTEGER as discharges_today,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bed_transfer_requests btr
         WHERE btr.to_ward_id = w.id AND DATE(btr.transferred_at) = p_date
         AND btr.status = 'completed')::INTEGER as transfers_in,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bed_transfer_requests btr2
         WHERE btr2.from_ward_id = w.id AND DATE(btr2.transferred_at) = p_date
         AND btr2.status = 'completed')::INTEGER as transfers_out,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a4
         JOIN bed_allocations ba4 ON a4.id = ba4.admission_id
         WHERE ba4.ward_id = w.id AND a4.actual_discharge_date = p_date
         AND a4.discharge_type = 'expired')::INTEGER as deaths_today,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a5
         JOIN bed_allocations ba5 ON a5.id = ba5.admission_id
         WHERE ba5.ward_id = w.id AND a5.status = 'admitted'
         AND ba5.release_datetime IS NULL
         AND EXISTS (
             SELECT 1 FROM patient_vitals pv
             WHERE pv.admission_id = a5.id
             AND pv.recorded_datetime > CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '24 hours'
             AND (pv.blood_pressure_systolic < 90 OR pv.spo2 < 90)
         ))::INTEGER as critical_patients,
        ROUND(AVG(CASE 
            WHEN a.actual_discharge_date IS NOT NULL 
            THEN a.actual_discharge_date - a.admission_date 
            ELSE CURRENT_DATE - a.admission_date 
        END), 1)::DECIMAL as average_los
    FROM wards w
    LEFT JOIN beds b ON w.id = b.ward_id AND b.is_active = TRUE
    LEFT JOIN admissions a ON b.current_admission_id = a.id
    WHERE w.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR w.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND w.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY w.id, w.name, w.ward_type
    ORDER BY w.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Patient Statistics Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_patient_statistics(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    metric VARCHAR,
    value BIGINT,
    previous_period_value BIGINT,
    change_percent DECIMAL
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_period_days INTEGER;
    v_prev_from DATE;
    v_prev_to DATE;
BEGIN
    v_period_days := p_to_date - p_from_date + 1;
    v_prev_from := p_from_date - v_period_days;
    v_prev_to := p_from_date - 1;
    
    RETURN QUERY
    
    -- New Registrations
    SELECT 
        'New Patient Registrations'::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE p.registration_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date),
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE p.registration_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        ROUND(
            (COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE p.registration_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date) -
             COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE p.registration_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to))::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE p.registration_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to), 0) * 100, 1
        )
    FROM patients p
    WHERE p.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR p.registered_branch_id = p_branch_id)
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- OPD Visits
    SELECT 
        'OPD Visits'::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date),
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        ROUND(
            (COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date) -
             COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to))::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to), 0) * 100, 1
        )
    FROM appointments a
    WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND a.status = 'completed'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Admissions
    SELECT 
        'IPD Admissions'::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.admission_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date),
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.admission_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        ROUND(
            (COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.admission_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date) -
             COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.admission_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to))::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.admission_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to), 0) * 100, 1
        )
    FROM admissions ad
    WHERE ad.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ad.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Discharges
    SELECT 
        'IPD Discharges'::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.actual_discharge_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date),
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.actual_discharge_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        ROUND(
            (COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.actual_discharge_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date) -
             COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.actual_discharge_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to))::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.actual_discharge_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to), 0) * 100, 1
        )
    FROM admissions ad
    WHERE ad.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ad.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Emergency Visits
    SELECT 
        'Emergency Visits'::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE DATE(ev.arrival_datetime) BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date),
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE DATE(ev.arrival_datetime) BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to),
        ROUND(
            (COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE DATE(ev.arrival_datetime) BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date) -
             COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE DATE(ev.arrival_datetime) BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to))::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE DATE(ev.arrival_datetime) BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to), 0) * 100, 1
        )
    FROM emergency_visits ev
    WHERE ev.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ev.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Surgeries
    SELECT 
        'Surgeries Performed'::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.scheduled_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date AND s.status = 'completed'),
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.scheduled_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to AND s.status = 'completed'),
        ROUND(
            (COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.scheduled_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date AND s.status = 'completed') -
             COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.scheduled_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to AND s.status = 'completed'))::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.scheduled_date BETWEEN v_prev_from AND v_prev_to AND s.status = 'completed'), 0) * 100, 1
        )
    FROM surgeries s
    WHERE s.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR s.branch_id = p_branch_id);
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Disease-wise Statistics (ICD)
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_disease_statistics(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    p_top_n INTEGER DEFAULT 20
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    rank INTEGER,
    icd_code VARCHAR,
    disease_name VARCHAR,
    opd_cases BIGINT,
    ipd_cases BIGINT,
    total_cases BIGINT,
    percentage DECIMAL,
    avg_los DECIMAL,
    mortality_count BIGINT
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH disease_counts AS (
        SELECT 
            ic.code,
            ic.short_description,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE cd.visit_id IS NOT NULL) as opd,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE cd.admission_id IS NOT NULL) as ipd,
            COUNT(*) as total,
            AVG(CASE 
                WHEN a.actual_discharge_date IS NOT NULL 
                THEN a.actual_discharge_date - a.admission_date 
            END) as avg_length_of_stay,
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.discharge_type = 'expired') as deaths
        FROM clinical_diagnoses cd
        JOIN icd_codes ic ON cd.icd_code_id = ic.id
        LEFT JOIN admissions a ON cd.admission_id = a.id
        WHERE cd.diagnosed_at BETWEEN p_from_date AND (p_to_date + 1)
        AND EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM patients p 
            WHERE p.id = cd.patient_id 
            AND p.organization_id = p_organization_id
        )
        GROUP BY ic.code, ic.short_description
    )
    SELECT 
        ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY dc.total DESC)::INTEGER as rank,
        dc.code::VARCHAR,
        dc.short_description::VARCHAR,
        dc.opd,
        dc.ipd,
        dc.total,
        ROUND(dc.total::DECIMAL / SUM(dc.total) OVER () * 100, 2) as percentage,
        ROUND(dc.avg_length_of_stay, 1) as avg_los,
        dc.deaths
    FROM disease_counts dc
    ORDER BY dc.total DESC
    LIMIT p_top_n;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Doctor Performance Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_doctor_performance(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    doctor_id UUID,
    doctor_name VARCHAR,
    specialization VARCHAR,
    department VARCHAR,
    opd_patients INTEGER,
    ipd_patients INTEGER,
    surgeries INTEGER,
    total_revenue DECIMAL,
    avg_rating DECIMAL,
    avg_consultation_time INTEGER,
    followup_rate DECIMAL,
    patient_satisfaction DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        d.id as doctor_id,
        u.full_name::VARCHAR as doctor_name,
        d.specialization::VARCHAR,
        dep.name::VARCHAR as department,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM appointments a 
         WHERE a.doctor_id = d.id 
         AND a.appointment_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
         AND a.status = 'completed')::INTEGER as opd_patients,
        (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT ad.id) FROM admissions ad 
         WHERE ad.primary_doctor_id = d.id 
         AND ad.admission_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date)::INTEGER as ipd_patients,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM surgeries s 
         WHERE s.primary_surgeon_id = d.id 
         AND s.scheduled_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
         AND s.status = 'completed')::INTEGER as surgeries,
        COALESCE((SELECT SUM(bi.net_amount) FROM bill_items bi
         JOIN bills b ON bi.bill_id = b.id
         WHERE bi.doctor_id = d.id
         AND b.bill_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
         AND b.is_cancelled = FALSE), 0)::DECIMAL as total_revenue,
        (SELECT ROUND(AVG(ps.doctor_rating), 2) FROM patient_surveys ps
         JOIN appointments a ON ps.visit_id::TEXT = a.id::TEXT
         WHERE a.doctor_id = d.id
         AND DATE(ps.submitted_at) BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date)::DECIMAL as avg_rating,
        (SELECT ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (a.consultation_end_at - a.consultation_start_at))/60))
         FROM appointments a
         WHERE a.doctor_id = d.id
         AND a.appointment_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
         AND a.consultation_start_at IS NOT NULL
         AND a.consultation_end_at IS NOT NULL)::INTEGER as avg_consultation_time,
        (SELECT ROUND(
            COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_type = 'followup')::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0) * 100, 1)
         FROM appointments a
         WHERE a.doctor_id = d.id
         AND a.appointment_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date)::DECIMAL as followup_rate,
        (SELECT ROUND(AVG(ps.overall_rating), 2) FROM patient_surveys ps
         JOIN appointments a ON ps.visit_id::TEXT = a.id::TEXT
         WHERE a.doctor_id = d.id
         AND DATE(ps.submitted_at) BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date)::DECIMAL as patient_satisfaction
    FROM doctors d
    JOIN users u ON d.user_id = u.id
    LEFT JOIN departments dep ON u.department_id = dep.id
    WHERE d.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND d.is_active = TRUE
    ORDER BY opd_patients DESC, ipd_patients DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Lab Turnaround Time Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_lab_tat_analysis(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    department VARCHAR,
    test_category VARCHAR,
    total_tests BIGINT,
    avg_tat_hours DECIMAL,
    median_tat_hours DECIMAL,
    within_target INTEGER,
    within_target_pct DECIMAL,
    exceeded_target INTEGER,
    critical_results INTEGER,
    critical_notified_on_time INTEGER
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        tc.department::VARCHAR,
        tc.category::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT as total_tests,
        ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.result_datetime - lo.order_datetime))/3600), 1)::DECIMAL as avg_tat_hours,
        ROUND(PERCENTILE_CONT(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (
            ORDER BY EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.result_datetime - lo.order_datetime))/3600
        ), 1)::DECIMAL as median_tat_hours,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE 
            EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.result_datetime - lo.order_datetime))/3600 <= tc.tat_routine_hours
        )::INTEGER as within_target,
        ROUND(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE 
            EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.result_datetime - lo.order_datetime))/3600 <= tc.tat_routine_hours
        )::DECIMAL / NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0) * 100, 1)::DECIMAL as within_target_pct,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE 
            EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.result_datetime - lo.order_datetime))/3600 > tc.tat_routine_hours
        )::INTEGER as exceeded_target,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE lr.is_critical = TRUE)::INTEGER as critical_results,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE 
            lr.is_critical = TRUE AND lr.critical_notified = TRUE
            AND EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (lr.critical_notified_at - lr.result_datetime))/60 <= 30
        )::INTEGER as critical_notified_on_time
    FROM lab_orders lo
    JOIN lab_order_items loi ON lo.id = loi.order_id
    JOIN test_catalog tc ON loi.test_id = tc.id
    LEFT JOIN lab_results lr ON loi.id = lr.order_item_id
    WHERE lo.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR lo.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND DATE(lo.order_datetime) BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    AND lr.result_datetime IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY tc.department, tc.category
    ORDER BY tc.department, tc.category;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 75: FINANCIAL REPORTS
-- ============================================

-- Daily Collection Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_daily_collection(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    collection_point VARCHAR,
    cash_amount DECIMAL,
    card_amount DECIMAL,
    upi_amount DECIMAL,
    mobile_banking DECIMAL,
    cheque_amount DECIMAL,
    bank_transfer DECIMAL,
    insurance_amount DECIMAL,
    corporate_amount DECIMAL,
    total_amount DECIMAL,
    transaction_count INTEGER,
    refund_amount DECIMAL,
    net_collection DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        COALESCE(d.name, 'General')::VARCHAR as collection_point,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_method = 'cash'), 0)::DECIMAL as cash_amount,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_method = 'card'), 0)::DECIMAL as card_amount,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_method = 'upi'), 0)::DECIMAL as upi_amount,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_method = 'mobile_banking'), 0)::DECIMAL as mobile_banking,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_method = 'cheque'), 0)::DECIMAL as cheque_amount,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_method = 'bank_transfer'), 0)::DECIMAL as bank_transfer,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_method = 'insurance'), 0)::DECIMAL as insurance_amount,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_method = 'corporate'), 0)::DECIMAL as corporate_amount,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE NOT p.is_refund), 0)::DECIMAL as total_amount,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE NOT p.is_refund)::INTEGER as transaction_count,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.is_refund), 0)::DECIMAL as refund_amount,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN p.is_refund THEN -p.amount ELSE p.amount END), 0)::DECIMAL as net_collection
    FROM payments p
    LEFT JOIN bills b ON p.bill_id = b.id
    LEFT JOIN departments d ON b.visit_id IS NOT NULL -- This is simplified; adjust based on actual logic
    WHERE p.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR p.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND p.payment_date = p_date
    AND p.status = 'completed'
    GROUP BY d.name
    ORDER BY total_amount DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Revenue Analysis Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_revenue_analysis(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    category VARCHAR,
    sub_category VARCHAR,
    gross_revenue DECIMAL,
    discount DECIMAL,
    net_revenue DECIMAL,
    cost DECIMAL,
    margin DECIMAL,
    margin_percent DECIMAL,
    transaction_count BIGINT,
    avg_ticket_size DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        bi.category::VARCHAR,
        COALESCE(sm.sub_category, 'Other')::VARCHAR as sub_category,
        SUM(bi.quantity * bi.unit_price)::DECIMAL as gross_revenue,
        SUM(bi.discount_amount)::DECIMAL as discount,
        SUM(bi.net_amount)::DECIMAL as net_revenue,
        COALESCE(SUM(psc.total_cost), 0)::DECIMAL as cost,
        (SUM(bi.net_amount) - COALESCE(SUM(psc.total_cost), 0))::DECIMAL as margin,
        ROUND((SUM(bi.net_amount) - COALESCE(SUM(psc.total_cost), 0)) / 
              NULLIF(SUM(bi.net_amount), 0) * 100, 2)::DECIMAL as margin_percent,
        COUNT(*)::BIGINT as transaction_count,
        ROUND(SUM(bi.net_amount) / NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0), 2)::DECIMAL as avg_ticket_size
    FROM bills b
    JOIN bill_items bi ON b.id = bi.bill_id
    LEFT JOIN service_master sm ON bi.service_id = sm.id
    LEFT JOIN patient_service_costs psc ON bi.service_id = psc.service_id 
        AND b.patient_id = psc.patient_id
        AND bi.service_date = psc.service_date
    WHERE b.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR b.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND b.bill_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    AND b.is_cancelled = FALSE
    GROUP BY bi.category, sm.sub_category
    ORDER BY net_revenue DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Outstanding Receivables Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_outstanding_receivables(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_as_of_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    receivable_type VARCHAR,
    party_name VARCHAR,
    total_billed DECIMAL,
    total_received DECIMAL,
    outstanding DECIMAL,
    aging_0_30 DECIMAL,
    aging_31_60 DECIMAL,
    aging_61_90 DECIMAL,
    aging_over_90 DECIMAL,
    oldest_invoice_date DATE,
    last_payment_date DATE
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    
    -- Patient Receivables
    SELECT 
        'Patient'::VARCHAR as receivable_type,
        p.full_name::VARCHAR as party_name,
        SUM(b.net_amount)::DECIMAL as total_billed,
        SUM(b.paid_amount)::DECIMAL as total_received,
        SUM(b.balance_amount)::DECIMAL as outstanding,
        SUM(b.balance_amount) FILTER (WHERE p_as_of_date - b.bill_date <= 30)::DECIMAL as aging_0_30,
        SUM(b.balance_amount) FILTER (WHERE p_as_of_date - b.bill_date BETWEEN 31 AND 60)::DECIMAL as aging_31_60,
        SUM(b.balance_amount) FILTER (WHERE p_as_of_date - b.bill_date BETWEEN 61 AND 90)::DECIMAL as aging_61_90,
        SUM(b.balance_amount) FILTER (WHERE p_as_of_date - b.bill_date > 90)::DECIMAL as aging_over_90,
        MIN(b.bill_date)::DATE as oldest_invoice_date,
        (SELECT MAX(py.payment_date) FROM payments py WHERE py.patient_id = p.id)::DATE as last_payment_date
    FROM bills b
    JOIN patients p ON b.patient_id = p.id
    WHERE b.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR b.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND b.is_cancelled = FALSE
    AND b.payment_status IN ('pending', 'partial')
    AND NOT b.is_insurance
    AND NOT b.is_corporate
    GROUP BY p.id, p.full_name
    HAVING SUM(b.balance_amount) > 0
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Insurance Receivables
    SELECT 
        'Insurance'::VARCHAR as receivable_type,
        ic.name::VARCHAR as party_name,
        SUM(ic2.net_claim_amount)::DECIMAL as total_billed,
        SUM(ic2.settled_amount)::DECIMAL as total_received,
        SUM(ic2.net_claim_amount - COALESCE(ic2.settled_amount, 0))::DECIMAL as outstanding,
        SUM(ic2.net_claim_amount - COALESCE(ic2.settled_amount, 0)) FILTER (
            WHERE p_as_of_date - ic2.submitted_at::DATE <= 30)::DECIMAL as aging_0_30,
        SUM(ic2.net_claim_amount - COALESCE(ic2.settled_amount, 0)) FILTER (
            WHERE p_as_of_date - ic2.submitted_at::DATE BETWEEN 31 AND 60)::DECIMAL as aging_31_60,
        SUM(ic2.net_claim_amount - COALESCE(ic2.settled_amount, 0)) FILTER (
            WHERE p_as_of_date - ic2.submitted_at::DATE BETWEEN 61 AND 90)::DECIMAL as aging_61_90,
        SUM(ic2.net_claim_amount - COALESCE(ic2.settled_amount, 0)) FILTER (
            WHERE p_as_of_date - ic2.submitted_at::DATE > 90)::DECIMAL as aging_over_90,
        MIN(ic2.submitted_at::DATE)::DATE as oldest_invoice_date,
        MAX(ic2.settlement_date)::DATE as last_payment_date
    FROM insurance_claims ic2
    JOIN insurance_companies ic ON ic2.insurance_company_id = ic.id
    WHERE ic2.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ic2.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND ic2.status NOT IN ('settled', 'rejected')
    GROUP BY ic.id, ic.name
    HAVING SUM(ic2.net_claim_amount - COALESCE(ic2.settled_amount, 0)) > 0
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Corporate Receivables
    SELECT 
        'Corporate'::VARCHAR as receivable_type,
        ca.company_name::VARCHAR as party_name,
        SUM(ci.total_amount)::DECIMAL as total_billed,
        SUM(ci.paid_amount)::DECIMAL as total_received,
        SUM(ci.balance_amount)::DECIMAL as outstanding,
        SUM(ci.balance_amount) FILTER (WHERE p_as_of_date - ci.invoice_date <= 30)::DECIMAL as aging_0_30,
        SUM(ci.balance_amount) FILTER (WHERE p_as_of_date - ci.invoice_date BETWEEN 31 AND 60)::DECIMAL as aging_31_60,
        SUM(ci.balance_amount) FILTER (WHERE p_as_of_date - ci.invoice_date BETWEEN 61 AND 90)::DECIMAL as aging_61_90,
        SUM(ci.balance_amount) FILTER (WHERE p_as_of_date - ci.invoice_date > 90)::DECIMAL as aging_over_90,
        MIN(ci.invoice_date)::DATE as oldest_invoice_date,
        MAX(ci.invoice_date) FILTER (WHERE ci.status = 'paid')::DATE as last_payment_date
    FROM corporate_invoices ci
    JOIN corporate_accounts ca ON ci.corporate_id = ca.id
    WHERE ci.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND ci.status NOT IN ('paid')
    GROUP BY ca.id, ca.company_name
    HAVING SUM(ci.balance_amount) > 0
    
    ORDER BY outstanding DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Profit & Loss Summary
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_profit_loss_summary(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    line_item VARCHAR,
    category VARCHAR,
    amount DECIMAL,
    percentage DECIMAL
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_total_revenue DECIMAL;
BEGIN
    -- Calculate total revenue first
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(bi.net_amount), 0) INTO v_total_revenue
    FROM bills b
    JOIN bill_items bi ON b.id = bi.bill_id
    WHERE b.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR b.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND b.bill_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    AND b.is_cancelled = FALSE;
    
    RETURN QUERY
    
    -- Revenue
    SELECT 
        'Revenue'::VARCHAR as line_item,
        bi.category::VARCHAR,
        SUM(bi.net_amount)::DECIMAL as amount,
        ROUND(SUM(bi.net_amount) / NULLIF(v_total_revenue, 0) * 100, 2)::DECIMAL as percentage
    FROM bills b
    JOIN bill_items bi ON b.id = bi.bill_id
    WHERE b.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR b.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND b.bill_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    AND b.is_cancelled = FALSE
    GROUP BY bi.category
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Total Revenue
    SELECT 
        'Total Revenue'::VARCHAR,
        'TOTAL'::VARCHAR,
        v_total_revenue,
        100.00::DECIMAL
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Expenses (from payroll)
    SELECT 
        'Expense - Salaries'::VARCHAR,
        'HR'::VARCHAR,
        COALESCE(SUM(pd.net_pay), 0)::DECIMAL,
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(pd.net_pay), 0) / NULLIF(v_total_revenue, 0) * 100, 2)::DECIMAL
    FROM payroll_runs pr
    JOIN payroll_details pd ON pr.id = pd.payroll_run_id
    WHERE pr.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR pr.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND DATE(pr.pay_period_from) >= p_from_date
    AND DATE(pr.pay_period_to) <= p_to_date
    AND pr.status = 'paid'
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Expenses (from purchases)
    SELECT 
        'Expense - Materials'::VARCHAR,
        'Procurement'::VARCHAR,
        COALESCE(SUM(grn.received_amount), 0)::DECIMAL,
        ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(grn.received_amount), 0) / NULLIF(v_total_revenue, 0) * 100, 2)::DECIMAL
    FROM goods_receipt_notes grn
    WHERE grn.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR grn.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND grn.receipt_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    AND grn.status = 'posted';
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 76: OPERATIONAL REPORTS
-- ============================================

-- OT Utilization Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_ot_utilization(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    ot_room VARCHAR,
    ot_type VARCHAR,
    total_surgeries INTEGER,
    total_hours DECIMAL,
    available_hours DECIMAL,
    utilization_percent DECIMAL,
    avg_surgery_duration DECIMAL,
    cancellations INTEGER,
    cancellation_rate DECIMAL,
    emergency_surgeries INTEGER,
    elective_surgeries INTEGER,
    avg_turnover_time DECIMAL
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_working_hours_per_day INTEGER := 10;
    v_days INTEGER;
BEGIN
    v_days := p_to_date - p_from_date + 1;
    
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        otr.room_number::VARCHAR as ot_room,
        otr.ot_type::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.status = 'completed')::INTEGER as total_surgeries,
        ROUND(SUM(s.actual_duration_minutes) FILTER (WHERE s.status = 'completed') / 60.0, 1)::DECIMAL as total_hours,
        (v_days * v_working_hours_per_day)::DECIMAL as available_hours,
        ROUND(SUM(s.actual_duration_minutes) FILTER (WHERE s.status = 'completed') / 60.0 / 
              (v_days * v_working_hours_per_day) * 100, 1)::DECIMAL as utilization_percent,
        ROUND(AVG(s.actual_duration_minutes) FILTER (WHERE s.status = 'completed'), 0)::DECIMAL as avg_surgery_duration,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.status = 'cancelled')::INTEGER as cancellations,
        ROUND(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.status = 'cancelled')::DECIMAL / 
              NULLIF(COUNT(*), 0) * 100, 1)::DECIMAL as cancellation_rate,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.surgery_category = 'emergency' AND s.status = 'completed')::INTEGER as emergency_surgeries,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.surgery_category = 'elective' AND s.status = 'completed')::INTEGER as elective_surgeries,
        ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (s2.actual_start_time - s.actual_end_time))/60), 0)::DECIMAL as avg_turnover_time
    FROM ot_rooms otr
    LEFT JOIN surgeries s ON otr.id = s.ot_room_id 
        AND s.scheduled_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    LEFT JOIN surgeries s2 ON otr.id = s2.ot_room_id 
        AND s2.scheduled_date = s.scheduled_date
        AND s2.actual_start_time > s.actual_end_time
    WHERE otr.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR otr.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND otr.is_active = TRUE
    GROUP BY otr.id, otr.room_number, otr.ot_type
    ORDER BY utilization_percent DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Pharmacy Stock Status Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_pharmacy_stock_status(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_pharmacy_id UUID DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    pharmacy_name VARCHAR,
    drug_name VARCHAR,
    generic_name VARCHAR,
    category VARCHAR,
    current_stock INTEGER,
    reorder_level INTEGER,
    stock_status VARCHAR,
    days_of_stock DECIMAL,
    expiring_soon INTEGER,
    expired INTEGER,
    last_purchase_price DECIMAL,
    stock_value DECIMAL,
    last_received_date DATE,
    last_issued_date DATE
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        pl.name::VARCHAR as pharmacy_name,
        dm.brand_name::VARCHAR as drug_name,
        dm.generic_name::VARCHAR,
        dm.therapeutic_class::VARCHAR as category,
        mi.quantity_on_hand::INTEGER as current_stock,
        mi.reorder_level::INTEGER,
        CASE 
            WHEN mi.quantity_on_hand <= 0 THEN 'Out of Stock'
            WHEN mi.quantity_on_hand <= mi.reorder_level THEN 'Low Stock'
            WHEN mi.quantity_on_hand <= mi.reorder_level * 1.5 THEN 'Warning'
            ELSE 'Adequate'
        END::VARCHAR as stock_status,
        ROUND(mi.quantity_on_hand::DECIMAL / 
              NULLIF((SELECT AVG(daily_consumption) FROM (
                  SELECT DATE(transaction_date), ABS(SUM(quantity)) as daily_consumption
                  FROM medicine_stock_transactions
                  WHERE drug_id = mi.drug_id
                  AND pharmacy_id = mi.pharmacy_id
                  AND transaction_type = 'sale'
                  AND transaction_date > CURRENT_DATE - 30
                  GROUP BY DATE(transaction_date)
              ) dc), 0), 1)::DECIMAL as days_of_stock,
        (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(current_quantity), 0) FROM medicine_batches mb
         WHERE mb.drug_id = mi.drug_id 
         AND mb.pharmacy_id = mi.pharmacy_id
         AND mb.expiry_date BETWEEN CURRENT_DATE AND CURRENT_DATE + 90)::INTEGER as expiring_soon,
        (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(current_quantity), 0) FROM medicine_batches mb
         WHERE mb.drug_id = mi.drug_id 
         AND mb.pharmacy_id = mi.pharmacy_id
         AND mb.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER as expired,
        mi.last_purchase_price::DECIMAL,
        (mi.quantity_on_hand * mi.average_cost)::DECIMAL as stock_value,
        mi.last_stock_date::DATE as last_received_date,
        (SELECT MAX(transaction_date)::DATE FROM medicine_stock_transactions mst
         WHERE mst.drug_id = mi.drug_id 
         AND mst.pharmacy_id = mi.pharmacy_id
         AND mst.transaction_type = 'sale') as last_issued_date
    FROM medicine_inventory mi
    JOIN pharmacy_locations pl ON mi.pharmacy_id = pl.id
    JOIN drug_master dm ON mi.drug_id = dm.id
    WHERE pl.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR pl.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND (p_pharmacy_id IS NULL OR pl.id = p_pharmacy_id)
    AND mi.is_active = TRUE
    ORDER BY 
        CASE 
            WHEN mi.quantity_on_hand <= 0 THEN 1
            WHEN mi.quantity_on_hand <= mi.reorder_level THEN 2
            ELSE 3
        END,
        dm.generic_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Bed Occupancy Trend Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_bed_occupancy_trend(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE - 30,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    report_date DATE,
    total_beds INTEGER,
    occupied_beds INTEGER,
    available_beds INTEGER,
    occupancy_rate DECIMAL,
    admissions INTEGER,
    discharges INTEGER,
    avg_los DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH date_series AS (
        SELECT generate_series(p_from_date, p_to_date, '1 day'::INTERVAL)::DATE as dt
    )
    SELECT 
        ds.dt as report_date,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM beds b 
         JOIN wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
         WHERE w.organization_id = p_organization_id
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR w.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND b.is_active = TRUE)::INTEGER as total_beds,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a
         WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND a.admission_date <= ds.dt
         AND (a.actual_discharge_date IS NULL OR a.actual_discharge_date > ds.dt)
         AND a.status IN ('admitted', 'discharged'))::INTEGER as occupied_beds,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM beds b 
         JOIN wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
         WHERE w.organization_id = p_organization_id
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR w.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND b.is_active = TRUE)::INTEGER -
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a
         WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND a.admission_date <= ds.dt
         AND (a.actual_discharge_date IS NULL OR a.actual_discharge_date > ds.dt)
         AND a.status IN ('admitted', 'discharged'))::INTEGER as available_beds,
        ROUND(
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a
             WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
             AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
             AND a.admission_date <= ds.dt
             AND (a.actual_discharge_date IS NULL OR a.actual_discharge_date > ds.dt)
             AND a.status IN ('admitted', 'discharged'))::DECIMAL /
            NULLIF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM beds b 
             JOIN wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id
             WHERE w.organization_id = p_organization_id
             AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR w.branch_id = p_branch_id)
             AND b.is_active = TRUE), 0) * 100, 1
        )::DECIMAL as occupancy_rate,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a
         WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND a.admission_date = ds.dt)::INTEGER as admissions,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions a
         WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND a.actual_discharge_date = ds.dt)::INTEGER as discharges,
        (SELECT ROUND(AVG(a.actual_discharge_date - a.admission_date), 1)
         FROM admissions a
         WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND a.actual_discharge_date = ds.dt)::DECIMAL as avg_los
    FROM date_series ds
    ORDER BY ds.dt;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 77: HR REPORTS
-- ============================================

-- Employee Attendance Summary
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_employee_attendance_summary(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_department_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    employee_code VARCHAR,
    employee_name VARCHAR,
    department VARCHAR,
    designation VARCHAR,
    total_days INTEGER,
    present_days DECIMAL,
    absent_days DECIMAL,
    leave_days DECIMAL,
    late_count INTEGER,
    early_leave_count INTEGER,
    overtime_hours DECIMAL,
    attendance_percentage DECIMAL
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_working_days INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Calculate working days (excluding Fridays as weekend in Bangladesh)
    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_working_days
    FROM generate_series(p_from_date, p_to_date, '1 day'::INTERVAL) d
    WHERE EXTRACT(DOW FROM d) != 5; -- 5 = Friday
    
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        e.employee_code::VARCHAR,
        e.full_name::VARCHAR,
        d.name::VARCHAR as department,
        e.designation::VARCHAR,
        v_working_days::INTEGER as total_days,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'present')::DECIMAL as present_days,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'absent')::DECIMAL as absent_days,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'leave')::DECIMAL as leave_days,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.late_minutes > 0)::INTEGER as late_count,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.early_leave_minutes > 0)::INTEGER as early_leave_count,
        COALESCE(SUM(a.overtime_hours), 0)::DECIMAL as overtime_hours,
        ROUND(COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.status = 'present')::DECIMAL / 
              NULLIF(v_working_days, 0) * 100, 1)::DECIMAL as attendance_percentage
    FROM employees e
    LEFT JOIN departments d ON e.department_id = d.id
    LEFT JOIN attendance a ON e.id = a.employee_id 
        AND a.attendance_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    WHERE e.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR e.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND (p_department_id IS NULL OR e.department_id = p_department_id)
    AND e.employment_status = 'active'
    GROUP BY e.id, e.employee_code, e.full_name, d.name, e.designation
    ORDER BY d.name, e.full_name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Payroll Summary Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_payroll_summary(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_year INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER,
    p_month INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(MONTH FROM CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    department VARCHAR,
    employee_count INTEGER,
    total_basic DECIMAL,
    total_allowances DECIMAL,
    total_gross DECIMAL,
    total_deductions DECIMAL,
    total_net_pay DECIMAL,
    total_employer_pf DECIMAL,
    total_tds DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        d.name::VARCHAR as department,
        COUNT(DISTINCT pd.employee_id)::INTEGER as employee_count,
        SUM(pd.basic_salary)::DECIMAL as total_basic,
        SUM(pd.total_earnings - pd.basic_salary)::DECIMAL as total_allowances,
        SUM(pd.gross_salary)::DECIMAL as total_gross,
        SUM(pd.total_deductions)::DECIMAL as total_deductions,
        SUM(pd.net_pay)::DECIMAL as total_net_pay,
        SUM(pd.employer_pf)::DECIMAL as total_employer_pf,
        SUM(pd.tds_amount)::DECIMAL as total_tds
    FROM payroll_runs pr
    JOIN payroll_details pd ON pr.id = pd.payroll_run_id
    JOIN employees e ON pd.employee_id = e.id
    LEFT JOIN departments d ON e.department_id = d.id
    WHERE pr.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR pr.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND pr.payroll_year = p_year
    AND pr.payroll_month = p_month
    AND pr.status IN ('approved', 'paid')
    GROUP BY d.name
    ORDER BY d.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Leave Balance Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_leave_balance(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_department_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_year INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    employee_code VARCHAR,
    employee_name VARCHAR,
    department VARCHAR,
    leave_type VARCHAR,
    opening_balance DECIMAL,
    accrued DECIMAL,
    taken DECIMAL,
    available_balance DECIMAL,
    pending_approval DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        e.employee_code::VARCHAR,
        e.full_name::VARCHAR,
        d.name::VARCHAR as department,
        lt.name::VARCHAR as leave_type,
        lb.opening_balance::DECIMAL,
        lb.accrued::DECIMAL,
        lb.taken::DECIMAL,
        lb.available_balance::DECIMAL,
        (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(la.number_of_days), 0) 
         FROM leave_applications la
         WHERE la.employee_id = e.id
         AND la.leave_type_id = lt.id
         AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM la.from_date) = p_year
         AND la.status = 'pending')::DECIMAL as pending_approval
    FROM employees e
    JOIN leave_balances lb ON e.id = lb.employee_id
    JOIN leave_types lt ON lb.leave_type_id = lt.id
    LEFT JOIN departments d ON e.department_id = d.id
    WHERE e.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR e.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND (p_department_id IS NULL OR e.department_id = p_department_id)
    AND lb.year = p_year
    AND e.employment_status = 'active'
    ORDER BY d.name, e.full_name, lt.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 78: INVENTORY REPORTS
-- ============================================

-- Stock Movement Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_stock_movement(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_store_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    item_code VARCHAR,
    item_name VARCHAR,
    category VARCHAR,
    opening_qty DECIMAL,
    received_qty DECIMAL,
    issued_qty DECIMAL,
    adjustment_qty DECIMAL,
    closing_qty DECIMAL,
    unit_cost DECIMAL,
    closing_value DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    WITH opening_stock AS (
        SELECT 
            it.item_id,
            SUM(CASE 
                WHEN it.transaction_type IN ('grn_receipt', 'transfer_in', 'adjustment_in', 'opening_balance')
                THEN it.quantity
                ELSE -it.quantity
            END) as opening
        FROM inventory_transactions it
        WHERE it.organization_id = p_organization_id
        AND (p_store_id IS NULL OR it.store_id = p_store_id)
        AND DATE(it.transaction_date) < p_from_date
        GROUP BY it.item_id
    ),
    movements AS (
        SELECT 
            it.item_id,
            SUM(it.quantity) FILTER (WHERE it.transaction_type IN ('grn_receipt', 'transfer_in')) as received,
            SUM(ABS(it.quantity)) FILTER (WHERE it.transaction_type IN ('issue', 'transfer_out')) as issued,
            SUM(CASE 
                WHEN it.transaction_type = 'adjustment_in' THEN it.quantity
                WHEN it.transaction_type = 'adjustment_out' THEN -it.quantity
                ELSE 0
            END) as adjustment
        FROM inventory_transactions it
        WHERE it.organization_id = p_organization_id
        AND (p_store_id IS NULL OR it.store_id = p_store_id)
        AND DATE(it.transaction_date) BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
        GROUP BY it.item_id
    )
    SELECT 
        im.item_code::VARCHAR,
        im.name::VARCHAR as item_name,
        ic.name::VARCHAR as category,
        COALESCE(os.opening, 0)::DECIMAL as opening_qty,
        COALESCE(m.received, 0)::DECIMAL as received_qty,
        COALESCE(m.issued, 0)::DECIMAL as issued_qty,
        COALESCE(m.adjustment, 0)::DECIMAL as adjustment_qty,
        (COALESCE(os.opening, 0) + COALESCE(m.received, 0) - 
         COALESCE(m.issued, 0) + COALESCE(m.adjustment, 0))::DECIMAL as closing_qty,
        si.average_cost::DECIMAL as unit_cost,
        ((COALESCE(os.opening, 0) + COALESCE(m.received, 0) - 
          COALESCE(m.issued, 0) + COALESCE(m.adjustment, 0)) * 
         COALESCE(si.average_cost, 0))::DECIMAL as closing_value
    FROM item_master im
    LEFT JOIN item_categories ic ON im.category_id = ic.id
    LEFT JOIN opening_stock os ON im.id = os.item_id
    LEFT JOIN movements m ON im.id = m.item_id
    LEFT JOIN store_inventory si ON im.id = si.item_id 
        AND (p_store_id IS NULL OR si.store_id = p_store_id)
    WHERE im.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND im.is_active = TRUE
    AND (os.opening IS NOT NULL OR m.received IS NOT NULL OR m.issued IS NOT NULL)
    ORDER BY ic.name, im.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Expiry Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_expiry_items(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_store_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_days_ahead INTEGER DEFAULT 90
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    store_name VARCHAR,
    item_code VARCHAR,
    item_name VARCHAR,
    batch_number VARCHAR,
    current_quantity DECIMAL,
    expiry_date DATE,
    days_to_expiry INTEGER,
    status VARCHAR,
    value DECIMAL
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        s.name::VARCHAR as store_name,
        im.item_code::VARCHAR,
        im.name::VARCHAR as item_name,
        ib.batch_number::VARCHAR,
        ib.current_quantity::DECIMAL,
        ib.expiry_date::DATE,
        (ib.expiry_date - CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER as days_to_expiry,
        CASE 
            WHEN ib.expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN 'Expired'
            WHEN ib.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + 30 THEN 'Critical'
            WHEN ib.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + 60 THEN 'Warning'
            ELSE 'Monitor'
        END::VARCHAR as status,
        (ib.current_quantity * ib.unit_cost)::DECIMAL as value
    FROM item_batches ib
    JOIN store_inventory si ON ib.inventory_id = si.id
    JOIN stores s ON ib.store_id = s.id
    JOIN item_master im ON ib.item_id = im.id
    WHERE s.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_store_id IS NULL OR s.id = p_store_id)
    AND ib.current_quantity > 0
    AND ib.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + p_days_ahead
    AND ib.is_active = TRUE
    ORDER BY ib.expiry_date, s.name, im.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 79: QUALITY & COMPLIANCE REPORTS
-- ============================================

-- Quality Indicator Dashboard
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_quality_dashboard(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    indicator_code VARCHAR,
    indicator_name VARCHAR,
    category VARCHAR,
    target_value DECIMAL,
    actual_value DECIMAL,
    variance DECIMAL,
    status VARCHAR,
    trend VARCHAR
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        qi.indicator_code::VARCHAR,
        qi.name::VARCHAR as indicator_name,
        qi.category::VARCHAR,
        qi.target_value::DECIMAL,
        qir.result_value::DECIMAL as actual_value,
        (qir.result_value - qi.target_value)::DECIMAL as variance,
        CASE 
            WHEN qi.target_direction = 'higher_better' AND qir.result_value >= qi.target_value THEN 'Met'
            WHEN qi.target_direction = 'lower_better' AND qir.result_value <= qi.target_value THEN 'Met'
            WHEN qi.target_direction = 'target_range' 
                AND qir.result_value BETWEEN qi.target_min AND qi.target_max THEN 'Met'
            ELSE 'Not Met'
        END::VARCHAR as status,
        COALESCE(qir.trend, 'stable')::VARCHAR
    FROM quality_indicators qi
    LEFT JOIN quality_indicator_results qir ON qi.id = qir.indicator_id
        AND qir.measurement_period_start >= p_from_date
        AND qir.measurement_period_end <= p_to_date
    WHERE (qi.organization_id IS NULL OR qi.organization_id = p_organization_id)
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR qir.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND qi.is_active = TRUE
    ORDER BY qi.category, qi.indicator_code;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Infection Control Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_infection_control(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    infection_type VARCHAR,
    total_cases INTEGER,
    hospital_acquired INTEGER,
    community_acquired INTEGER,
    device_related INTEGER,
    mdro_cases INTEGER,
    rate_per_1000 DECIMAL,
    mortality_count INTEGER,
    preventable_count INTEGER
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_patient_days BIGINT;
BEGIN
    -- Calculate patient days
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(
        CASE 
            WHEN a.actual_discharge_date IS NOT NULL 
            THEN LEAST(a.actual_discharge_date, p_to_date) - GREATEST(a.admission_date, p_from_date) + 1
            ELSE p_to_date - GREATEST(a.admission_date, p_from_date) + 1
        END
    ), 1) INTO v_patient_days
    FROM admissions a
    WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND a.admission_date <= p_to_date
    AND (a.actual_discharge_date IS NULL OR a.actual_discharge_date >= p_from_date);
    
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        ins.infection_type::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*)::INTEGER as total_cases,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ins.onset_type = 'hospital_acquired')::INTEGER as hospital_acquired,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ins.onset_type = 'community')::INTEGER as community_acquired,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ins.device_related = TRUE)::INTEGER as device_related,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ins.is_mdro = TRUE)::INTEGER as mdro_cases,
        ROUND(COUNT(*)::DECIMAL / v_patient_days * 1000, 2)::DECIMAL as rate_per_1000,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ins.outcome LIKE '%death%')::INTEGER as mortality_count,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ins.preventable = TRUE)::INTEGER as preventable_count
    FROM infection_surveillance ins
    WHERE ins.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ins.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND ins.onset_date BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY ins.infection_type
    ORDER BY total_cases DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Incident Summary Report
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_incident_summary(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_from_date DATE DEFAULT DATE_TRUNC('month', CURRENT_DATE)::DATE,
    p_to_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    incident_type VARCHAR,
    total_incidents INTEGER,
    severity_none INTEGER,
    severity_mild INTEGER,
    severity_moderate INTEGER,
    severity_severe INTEGER,
    open_count INTEGER,
    closed_count INTEGER,
    avg_resolution_days DECIMAL,
    preventable_count INTEGER
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        ir.incident_type::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*)::INTEGER as total_incidents,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ir.severity_actual = 'none')::INTEGER as severity_none,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ir.severity_actual = 'mild')::INTEGER as severity_mild,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ir.severity_actual = 'moderate')::INTEGER as severity_moderate,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ir.severity_actual = 'severe')::INTEGER as severity_severe,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ir.status IN ('open', 'investigating'))::INTEGER as open_count,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ir.status = 'closed')::INTEGER as closed_count,
        ROUND(AVG(EXTRACT(DAY FROM (ir.closed_at - ir.reported_at))) 
              FILTER (WHERE ir.closed_at IS NOT NULL), 1)::DECIMAL as avg_resolution_days,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM mortality_reviews mr 
            WHERE mr.death_record_id::TEXT = ir.id::TEXT 
            AND mr.potentially_preventable = TRUE
        ))::INTEGER as preventable_count
    FROM incident_reports ir
    WHERE ir.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ir.branch_id = p_branch_id)
    AND DATE(ir.incident_datetime) BETWEEN p_from_date AND p_to_date
    GROUP BY ir.incident_type
    ORDER BY total_incidents DESC;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 80: MIS & EXECUTIVE REPORTS
-- ============================================

-- Executive Dashboard Summary
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_executive_dashboard(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_date DATE DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    category VARCHAR,
    metric_name VARCHAR,
    today_value DECIMAL,
    mtd_value DECIMAL,
    ytd_value DECIMAL,
    target DECIMAL,
    achievement_percent DECIMAL
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_month_start DATE := DATE_TRUNC('month', p_date)::DATE;
    v_year_start DATE := DATE_TRUNC('year', p_date)::DATE;
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    
    -- Revenue Metrics
    SELECT 
        'Revenue'::VARCHAR as category,
        'Total Revenue'::VARCHAR as metric_name,
        COALESCE(SUM(bi.net_amount) FILTER (WHERE b.bill_date = p_date), 0)::DECIMAL as today_value,
        COALESCE(SUM(bi.net_amount) FILTER (WHERE b.bill_date >= v_month_start), 0)::DECIMAL as mtd_value,
        COALESCE(SUM(bi.net_amount) FILTER (WHERE b.bill_date >= v_year_start), 0)::DECIMAL as ytd_value,
        NULL::DECIMAL as target,
        NULL::DECIMAL as achievement_percent
    FROM bills b
    JOIN bill_items bi ON b.id = bi.bill_id
    WHERE b.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND b.bill_date <= p_date
    AND b.is_cancelled = FALSE
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Collection Metrics
    SELECT 
        'Collection'::VARCHAR,
        'Total Collection'::VARCHAR,
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_date = p_date), 0),
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_date >= v_month_start), 0),
        COALESCE(SUM(p.amount) FILTER (WHERE p.payment_date >= v_year_start), 0),
        NULL,
        NULL
    FROM payments p
    WHERE p.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND p.payment_date <= p_date
    AND p.status = 'completed'
    AND p.is_refund = FALSE
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- OPD Metrics
    SELECT 
        'OPD'::VARCHAR,
        'OPD Patients'::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_date = p_date AND a.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_date >= v_month_start AND a.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_date >= v_year_start AND a.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL,
        NULL,
        NULL
    FROM appointments a
    WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND a.appointment_date <= p_date
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- IPD Metrics
    SELECT 
        'IPD'::VARCHAR,
        'Current Census'::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ad.status = 'admitted')::DECIMAL,
        NULL::DECIMAL,
        NULL::DECIMAL,
        NULL,
        NULL
    FROM admissions ad
    WHERE ad.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND ad.admission_date <= p_date
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Bed Occupancy
    SELECT 
        'IPD'::VARCHAR,
        'Bed Occupancy %'::VARCHAR,
        ROUND((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM beds b 
               JOIN wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id 
               WHERE w.organization_id = p_organization_id 
               AND b.status = 'occupied')::DECIMAL /
              NULLIF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM beds b 
                      JOIN wards w ON b.ward_id = w.id 
                      WHERE w.organization_id = p_organization_id 
                      AND b.is_active = TRUE), 0) * 100, 1),
        NULL,
        NULL,
        85.00::DECIMAL, -- Target 85%
        NULL
    
    UNION ALL
    
    -- Surgery Metrics
    SELECT 
        'Surgery'::VARCHAR,
        'Surgeries'::VARCHAR,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.scheduled_date = p_date AND s.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.scheduled_date >= v_month_start AND s.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL,
        COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE s.scheduled_date >= v_year_start AND s.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL,
        NULL,
        NULL
    FROM surgeries s
    WHERE s.organization_id = p_organization_id
    AND s.scheduled_date <= p_date;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Monthly Performance Scorecard
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_monthly_scorecard(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_year INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER,
    p_month INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(MONTH FROM CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    kpi_category VARCHAR,
    kpi_name VARCHAR,
    current_month_value DECIMAL,
    previous_month_value DECIMAL,
    same_month_last_year DECIMAL,
    mom_change DECIMAL,
    yoy_change DECIMAL,
    target DECIMAL,
    status VARCHAR
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_current_start DATE := make_date(p_year, p_month, 1);
    v_current_end DATE := (v_current_start + INTERVAL '1 month - 1 day')::DATE;
    v_prev_start DATE := (v_current_start - INTERVAL '1 month')::DATE;
    v_prev_end DATE := v_current_start - 1;
    v_ly_start DATE := make_date(p_year - 1, p_month, 1);
    v_ly_end DATE := (v_ly_start + INTERVAL '1 month - 1 day')::DATE;
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        kd.category::VARCHAR as kpi_category,
        kd.name::VARCHAR as kpi_name,
        (SELECT kv.value FROM kpi_values kv 
         WHERE kv.kpi_id = kd.id 
         AND kv.period_start >= v_current_start 
         AND kv.period_end <= v_current_end
         ORDER BY kv.calculated_at DESC LIMIT 1)::DECIMAL as current_month_value,
        (SELECT kv.value FROM kpi_values kv 
         WHERE kv.kpi_id = kd.id 
         AND kv.period_start >= v_prev_start 
         AND kv.period_end <= v_prev_end
         ORDER BY kv.calculated_at DESC LIMIT 1)::DECIMAL as previous_month_value,
        (SELECT kv.value FROM kpi_values kv 
         WHERE kv.kpi_id = kd.id 
         AND kv.period_start >= v_ly_start 
         AND kv.period_end <= v_ly_end
         ORDER BY kv.calculated_at DESC LIMIT 1)::DECIMAL as same_month_last_year,
        NULL::DECIMAL as mom_change, -- Will be calculated
        NULL::DECIMAL as yoy_change, -- Will be calculated
        kd.target_value::DECIMAL as target,
        NULL::VARCHAR as status
    FROM kpi_definitions kd
    WHERE (kd.organization_id IS NULL OR kd.organization_id = p_organization_id)
    AND kd.is_active = TRUE
    ORDER BY kd.category, kd.name;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 81: REGULATORY REPORTS
-- ============================================

-- DGHS Monthly Report Format
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rpt_dghs_monthly(
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_branch_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_year INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER,
    p_month INTEGER DEFAULT EXTRACT(MONTH FROM CURRENT_DATE)::INTEGER
)
RETURNS TABLE(
    sl_no INTEGER,
    indicator VARCHAR,
    unit VARCHAR,
    value DECIMAL
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_start_date DATE := make_date(p_year, p_month, 1);
    v_end_date DATE := (v_start_date + INTERVAL '1 month - 1 day')::DATE;
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 1, 'Total Outdoor Patients', 'Number',
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM appointments a 
         WHERE a.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR a.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND a.appointment_date BETWEEN v_start_date AND v_end_date
         AND a.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 2, 'Total Indoor Patients', 'Number',
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions ad 
         WHERE ad.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ad.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND ad.admission_date BETWEEN v_start_date AND v_end_date)::DECIMAL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 3, 'Total Bed Days', 'Days',
        (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(
            CASE 
                WHEN ad.actual_discharge_date IS NOT NULL 
                THEN LEAST(ad.actual_discharge_date, v_end_date) - GREATEST(ad.admission_date, v_start_date) + 1
                ELSE v_end_date - GREATEST(ad.admission_date, v_start_date) + 1
            END
        ), 0) FROM admissions ad 
         WHERE ad.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ad.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND ad.admission_date <= v_end_date
         AND (ad.actual_discharge_date IS NULL OR ad.actual_discharge_date >= v_start_date))::DECIMAL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 4, 'Total Surgeries', 'Number',
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM surgeries s 
         WHERE s.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR s.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND s.scheduled_date BETWEEN v_start_date AND v_end_date
         AND s.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 5, 'Major Surgeries', 'Number',
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM surgeries s 
         JOIN ot_rooms otr ON s.ot_room_id = otr.id
         WHERE s.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR s.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND s.scheduled_date BETWEEN v_start_date AND v_end_date
         AND s.status = 'completed'
         AND otr.ot_type = 'major')::DECIMAL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 6, 'Normal Deliveries', 'Number',
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM surgeries s 
         WHERE s.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR s.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND s.scheduled_date BETWEEN v_start_date AND v_end_date
         AND s.surgery_name ILIKE '%normal delivery%'
         AND s.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 7, 'Cesarean Sections', 'Number',
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM surgeries s 
         WHERE s.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR s.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND s.scheduled_date BETWEEN v_start_date AND v_end_date
         AND (s.surgery_name ILIKE '%cesarean%' OR s.surgery_name ILIKE '%c-section%')
         AND s.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 8, 'Total Deaths', 'Number',
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admissions ad 
         WHERE ad.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ad.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND ad.actual_discharge_date BETWEEN v_start_date AND v_end_date
         AND ad.discharge_type = 'expired')::DECIMAL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 9, 'Lab Tests Conducted', 'Number',
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM lab_order_items loi
         JOIN lab_orders lo ON loi.order_id = lo.id
         WHERE lo.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR lo.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND DATE(lo.order_datetime) BETWEEN v_start_date AND v_end_date
         AND loi.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 10, 'X-Ray Examinations', 'Number',
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM radiology_orders ro
         WHERE ro.organization_id = p_organization_id 
         AND (p_branch_id IS NULL OR ro.branch_id = p_branch_id)
         AND DATE(ro.order_datetime) BETWEEN v_start_date AND v_end_date
         AND ro.modality_type = 'xray'
         AND ro.status = 'completed')::DECIMAL
    ORDER BY 1;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 82: REPORT HELPER FUNCTIONS
-- ============================================

-- Generate Report as JSON
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION generate_report_json(
    p_report_code VARCHAR,
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_parameters JSONB DEFAULT '{}'
)
RETURNS JSONB AS $$
DECLARE
    v_report RECORD;
    v_result JSONB;
    v_query TEXT;
    v_data JSONB;
BEGIN
    -- Get report definition
    SELECT * INTO v_report
    FROM report_master
    WHERE report_code = p_report_code
    AND (organization_id IS NULL OR organization_id = p_organization_id)
    AND is_active = TRUE
    LIMIT 1;
    
    IF v_report IS NULL THEN
        RETURN jsonb_build_object(
            'success', FALSE,
            'error', 'Report not found: ' || p_report_code
        );
    END IF;
    
    -- Build query with parameters
    v_query := v_report.source_query;
    
    -- Execute and get results
    EXECUTE 'SELECT jsonb_agg(row_to_json(t)) FROM (' || v_query || ') t'
    INTO v_data
    USING p_organization_id, p_parameters;
    
    -- Build result
    v_result := jsonb_build_object(
        'success', TRUE,
        'report_code', v_report.report_code,
        'report_name', v_report.name,
        'generated_at', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        'parameters', p_parameters,
        'columns', v_report.columns,
        'data', COALESCE(v_data, '[]'::JSONB),
        'row_count', jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(v_data, '[]'::JSONB))
    );
    
    -- Log execution
    INSERT INTO report_execution_log (
        organization_id, report_id, execution_type,
        parameters_used, started_at, completed_at,
        status, row_count
    ) VALUES (
        p_organization_id, v_report.id, 'api',
        p_parameters, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        'completed', jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(v_data, '[]'::JSONB))
    );
    
    RETURN v_result;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Export Report to CSV Format
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION export_report_csv(
    p_report_code VARCHAR,
    p_organization_id UUID,
    p_parameters JSONB DEFAULT '{}'
)
RETURNS TEXT AS $$
DECLARE
    v_report RECORD;
    v_columns JSONB;
    v_header TEXT := '';
    v_body TEXT := '';
    v_col RECORD;
    v_row RECORD;
    v_query TEXT;
BEGIN
    -- Get report definition
    SELECT * INTO v_report
    FROM report_master
    WHERE report_code = p_report_code
    AND (organization_id IS NULL OR organization_id = p_organization_id)
    AND is_active = TRUE
    LIMIT 1;
    
    IF v_report IS NULL THEN
        RETURN 'Error: Report not found';
    END IF;
    
    -- Build header
    FOR v_col IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(v_report.columns)
    LOOP
        v_header := v_header || '"' || (v_col.value->>'header') || '",';
    END LOOP;
    v_header := RTRIM(v_header, ',') || E'\n';
    
    -- Execute query and build body
    v_query := 'SELECT * FROM (' || v_report.source_query || ') t';
    
    FOR v_row IN EXECUTE v_query USING p_organization_id, p_parameters
    LOOP
        FOR v_col IN SELECT * FROM jsonb_array_elements(v_report.columns)
        LOOP
            -- Add column value (simplified - actual implementation would be more complex)
            v_body := v_body || '"' || COALESCE(v_row.*::TEXT, '') || '",';
        END LOOP;
        v_body := RTRIM(v_body, ',') || E'\n';
    END LOOP;
    
    RETURN v_header || v_body;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- SECTION 84: REPORT INDEXES
-- ============================================

CREATE INDEX idx_report_master_code ON report_master(report_code);
CREATE INDEX idx_report_master_category ON report_master(category_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_report_master_active ON report_master(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

CREATE INDEX idx_report_execution_report ON report_execution_log(report_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_report_execution_date ON report_execution_log(started_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_report_execution_user ON report_execution_log(executed_by);

CREATE INDEX idx_report_schedules_active ON report_schedules(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_report_schedules_next ON report_schedules(next_run_at);

    v_function_count INTEGER;

    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_report_count FROM report_master WHERE is_active = TRUE;

    WHERE routine_schema = 'public' 
    AND routine_name LIKE 'rpt_%';

    RAISE NOTICE '║  │   ├── Daily Collection                                          ║';

## 📊 REPORT GENERATOR SUMMARY

### Available Reports:

|Category|Report Name|Function|
|---|---|---|
|**Clinical**|Daily OPD Summary|`rpt_daily_opd_summary()`|
|**Clinical**|Daily IPD Census|`rpt_daily_ipd_census()`|
|**Clinical**|Patient Statistics|`rpt_patient_statistics()`|
|**Clinical**|Disease Statistics|`rpt_disease_statistics()`|
|**Clinical**|Doctor Performance|`rpt_doctor_performance()`|
|**Clinical**|Lab TAT Analysis|`rpt_lab_tat_analysis()`|
|**Financial**|Daily Collection|`rpt_daily_collection()`|
|**Financial**|Revenue Analysis|`rpt_revenue_analysis()`|
|**Financial**|Outstanding Receivables|`rpt_outstanding_receivables()`|
|**Financial**|Profit & Loss|`rpt_profit_loss_summary()`|
|**Operational**|OT Utilization|`rpt_ot_utilization()`|
|**Operational**|Bed Occupancy Trend|`rpt_bed_occupancy_trend()`|
|**Inventory**|Pharmacy Stock|`rpt_pharmacy_stock_status()`|
|**Inventory**|Stock Movement|`rpt_stock_movement()`|
|**Inventory**|Expiry Items|`rpt_expiry_items()`|
|**HR**|Attendance Summary|`rpt_employee_attendance_summary()`|
|**HR**|Payroll Summary|`rpt_payroll_summary()`|
|**HR**|Leave Balance|`rpt_leave_balance()`|
|**Quality**|Quality Dashboard|`rpt_quality_dashboard()`|
|**Quality**|Infection Control|`rpt_infection_control()`|
|**Quality**|Incident Summary|`rpt_incident_summary()`|
|**MIS**|Executive Dashboard|`rpt_executive_dashboard()`|
|**MIS**|Monthly Scorecard|`rpt_monthly_scorecard()`|
|**Regulatory**|DGHS Monthly|`rpt_dghs_monthly()`|

### Usage Examples:

SQL

```sql
-- Generate Daily OPD Report
SELECT * FROM rpt_daily_opd_summary(
    'your-org-id'::UUID,
    NULL,
    '2024-01-15'
);

-- Generate Revenue Analysis
SELECT * FROM rpt_revenue_analysis(
    'your-org-id'::UUID,
    'your-branch-id'::UUID,
    '2024-01-01',
    '2024-01-31'
);

-- Generate Report as JSON
SELECT generate_report_json(
    'RPT_DAILY_OPD',
    'your-org-id'::UUID,
    '{"date": "2024-01-15"}'::JSONB
);

-- Export to CSV
SELECT export_report_csv(
    'RPT_DAILY_COLLECTION',
    'your-org-id'::UUID,
    '{"date": "2024-01-15"}'::JSONB
);
```
